费用移民局 (USCIS)重要#EB-5 投资移民#费用

USCIS确定EB-5签证费用上调方案,2026年11月30日起生效

发布日期 2026年9月30日生效日期 2026年11月30日

美国国土安全部（DHS）与美国公民及移民服务局（USCIS）发布最终规则,调整EB-5移民投资者项目相关费用,将2022年《EB-5改革与诚信法案》的规定正式纳入法规。该规则将于2026年11月30日生效,是对2025年10月23日公布的拟议规则所收公众意见的回应。

要点

  • 最终规则将于2026年11月30日生效,适用于该日期之后邮戳的所有EB-5相关申请。
  • I-526E表格对应的EB-5诚信基金费用从1,000美元上调至1,100美元,涨幅10%,依据为2022年至2025年的通胀数据。
  • 区域中心（Regional Center）年度诚信基金费用从10,000美元上调至11,000美元,部分区域中心则从20,000美元上调至22,000美元。
  • 该规则还调整了EB-5相关表格的移民审查费账户（IEFA）费用,并根据《EB-5改革法案》增设了技术费。
  • 此前联邦法院在Moody诉Noem案中中止了2024年费用规则中的部分EB-5费用,USCIS因此恢复适用2024年4月1日前的费用标准作为比较基准。
  • DHS表示,新费用结构旨在弥补其最新EB-5费用研究中发现的约4,840万美元的成本与收入缺口。

影响对象

受影响的包括EB-5移民投资者、新商业企业、创造就业实体,以及向USCIS提交EB-5相关申请的区域中心。

建议行动

投资者、区域中心及其代理人应查阅G-1055表格中列明的最终费用表,并为2026年11月30日起邮戳的EB-5申请准备缴纳新费用。

全文翻译

官方原文全文已于 2026-10-12 保存在本站。即使原链接变更或被删除，仍可在此查看全文。译文由 AI 逐段翻译，仅英文原文具有法律效力。

AGENCY:
美国公民及移民服务局，美国国土安全部（DHS）。
U.S. Citizenship and Immigration Services, DHS.
行动：
ACTION:
最终规则。
Final rule.
摘要：
SUMMARY:
本最终规则调整了美国公民及移民服务局（USCIS）收取的基于就业的移民第五优先类别（EB-5）移民福利申请费用。本规则还将《2022年EB-5改革与诚信法案》的规定法典化，实施新的法定要求，并针对2025年10月23日发布的拟议收费规则所收到的公众意见作出回应。
This final rule adjusts the Employment-Based Immigration, Fifth Preference (EB-5) immigration benefit request fees charged by U.S. Citizenship and Immigration Services (USCIS). It also codifies provisions of the EB-5 Reform and Integrity Act of 2022, implements new statutory requirements, and addresses public comments received on the proposed fee rule published on October 23, 2025.
日期：
DATES:
本最终规则自2026年11月30日起生效。凡在该日期或该日期之后邮戳标注提交的任何申请书、请愿书或请求书，均须随附本最终规则规定的费用。
This final rule is effective November 30, 2026. Any application, petition, or request postmarked on or after this date must be accompanied by the fees established by this final rule.
如需更多信息，请联系：

一、执行摘要
I. Executive Summary
(一) 监管行动的目的
A. Purpose of the Regulatory Action
国土安全部(DHS)正在调整EB-5移民福利申请费用,并实施《2022年综合拨款法案》第BB分部《2022年EB-5改革与诚信法》(EB-5改革法)的相关规定,公法第117-103号,以确保项目管理有充足资金、加强诚信措施并遵守法定要求。本规则建立了更新的收费表,引入了技术费,将诚信基金费用和罚款法典化,并澄清了某些EB-5申请的程序。
DHS is adjusting EB-5 immigration benefit request fees and implementing related provisions of the EB-5 Reform and Integrity Act of 2022, div. BB of the Consolidated Appropriations Act, 2022, Public Law 117-103 (EB-5 Reform Act), to ensure adequate funding for program administration, enhance integrity measures, and comply with statutory requirements. This rule establishes an updated fee schedule, introduces a technology fee, codifies Integrity Fund fees and penalties, and clarifies procedures for certain EB-5 filings.
(二) 法律授权
B. Legal Authority
国土安全部依据《EB-5改革法》的授权发布本规则。《EB-5改革法》废除了区域中心项目此前的法定规定(公法第102-395号,106 Stat. 1828,第610条),并修订了《移民与国籍法》(INA),以改革EB-5类别的区域中心项目,自2022年5月14日起至2027年9月30日生效。[1]
DHS is publishing this rule under the authority of the EB-5 Reform Act. The EB-5 Reform Act repealed prior statutory provisions for the Regional Center Program (Pub. L. 102-395, 106 Stat. 1828, sec. 610) and amended the Immigration and Nationality Act (INA) to reform the regional center program of the EB-5 category, effective May 14, 2022, through September 30, 2027.[1]
《EB-5改革法》授权国土安全部进行费用研究,并为与EB-5项目相关的移民福利申请设定费用。[2] 根据《EB-5改革法》第106条,国土安全部在本规则中制定费用,以收回管理EB-5项目的全部成本,并力求实现法定处理时间目标。 参见公法第117-103号,第BB分部,第106(b)条。
The EB-5 Reform Act authorizes DHS to conduct a fee study and set fees for EB-5 program-related immigration benefit requests.[2] Under section 106 of the EB-5 Reform Act, DHS is establishing the fees in this rule to recover the full costs of administering the EB-5 program and seek to attain statutory processing time goals. SeePublic Law 117-103, div. BB, sec. 106(b).
《EB-5改革法》还授权国土安全部将减免费用或免费申请的成本,以及最多百分之一的申请费用用于技术改进纳入其中。 参见公法第117-103号,第BB分部,第106(c)条。
The EB-5 Reform Act also authorizes DHS to include costs for reduced or no-fee applications and up to one percent of the petition fee for technology improvements. SeePublic Law 117-103, div. BB, sec. 106(c).
《EB-5改革法》还要求国土安全部收取EB-5诚信基金费用(《移民与国籍法》第203(b)(5)(J)条,8 U.S.C. 1153(b)(5)(J)),包括向区域中心收取年费以及对逾期或未缴费用处以罚款。
The EB-5 Reform Act further requires DHS to collect EB-5 Integrity Fund fees (INA sec. 203(b)(5)(J), 8 U.S.C. 1153(b)(5)(J)), including annual fees from regional centers and penalties for late or nonpayment.
(三) 对拟议规则的修改
C. Changes From the Proposed Rule
如本序言第三节及后文更详尽地说明,国土安全部根据就拟议规则收到的意见,或在行使其设定费用的权力时,结合数据支持的政策目标进行权衡,对本最终规则作出了若干修改。主要修改如下: (印刷页第61941页)
As explained more fully in section III and later in this preamble, DHS is making several changes in this final rule based on comments received on the proposed rule or in exercising its authority to establish fees while balancing policy objectives as supported by data. The main changes are as follows: ( printed page 61941)
1. 现行费用的变化
1. Change in Current Fees
2025年11月12日,美国科罗拉多州地区法院在 Moody 诉 Noem 案中作出裁决,2025 WL 3157554(D. Colo.),中止了由国土安全部法典化并于2024年4月1日起生效的某些EB-5相关费用。[3] 法院认定《EB-5改革法》禁止国土安全部在2022/2023财年费用规则中调整EB-5项目费用。 Moody 案第10页。国土安全部和美国公民及移民服务局(USCIS)认为法院的裁决有误,但已予以执行。2025年11月12日,美国公民及移民服务局恢复接受2024年3月31日之前有效的EB-5费用。因此,本序言中所示的现行费用为2022/2023财年费用规则生效之前有效的费用,而非拟议规则中列出的以及在8 CFR 106.2(a)中法典化的当前费用。[4]
On November 12, 2025, the United States District Court for the District of Colorado issued a decision in Moody v. Noem, 2025 WL 3157554 (D. Colo.), staying certain EB-5 related fees that were codified by DHS and became effective April 1, 2024.[3] The court determined that the EB-5 Reform Act precluded DHS from adjusting EB-5 program fees in the FY 2022/2023 fee rule. Moody at 10. DHS and USCIS believe the Court's decision is incorrect but have implemented it. On November 12, 2025, USCIS reverted to accepting the EB-5 fees that were in effect until March 31, 2024. As such, the current fees shown in this preamble are the fees which were in effect before the FY 2022/2023 fee rule instead of the current fees listed in the proposed rule and those codified at 8 CFR 106.2(a).[4]
2. 修订后的费用
2. Revised Fees
DHS在拟议规则中提出将移民审查费账户(Immigration Examinations Fee Account, IEFA)费用范围设定为55美元至29,935美元。在本最终规则中,DHS设定了经修订的费用,以回应意见并纳入最新数据。本序言后文的表1比较了现行费用、拟议费用和最终费用。如本序言后文所述,经修订的费用主要是预算和工作量假设变化的结果。我们根据意见修订了预算和工作量估算,以使用更新的规划信息。
DHS proposed a range of Immigration Examinations Fee Account (IEFA) fees from $55 to $29,935 in the proposed rule. In this final rule, DHS sets revised fees to respond to comments and incorporate current data. Table 1 later in this preamble compares current, proposed, and final fees. As explained later in this preamble, the revised fees are mainly the result of changes to budget and workload assumptions. We revised budget and workload estimates to use more recent planning information in response to comments.
3. EB-5费用研究的修订
3. Revisions to the EB-5 Fee Study
在本最终规则中,DHS更新了EB-5费用研究,以纳入2026/2027财年的预测并反映公众意见,取代了拟议规则中使用的2024/2025财年假设。 见90 FR 48516, 48522-48526。下文重点介绍的变化的更详细讨论见本序言第III.C节。
In this final rule, DHS updates the EB-5 fee study to incorporate FY 2026/2027 projections and to reflect public comments, replacing the FY 2024/2025 assumptions used in the proposed rule. See90 FR 48516, 48522-48526. A more detailed discussion of the changes highlighted below is provided in section III.C of this preamble.
EB-5申请的收件预测已使用数量预测委员会(Volume Projection Committee, VPC)的2026/2027财年估算进行修订,将预计的EB-5年平均收件量从11,262件提高到16,604件。完成率估算也已使用移民投资人项目办公室(Immigrant Investor Program Office, IPO)的最新数据进行更新,同时继续与《EB-5改革法》中规定的处理时间目标保持一致。
Receipt forecasts for EB-5 filings have been revised using FY 2026/2027 estimates from the Volume Projection Committee (VPC), increasing projected average annual EB-5 receipts from 11,262 to 16,604. Completion rate estimates have also been updated using more recent Immigrant Investor Program Office (IPO) data, while remaining aligned with the processing time goals in the EB-5 Reform Act.
关于成本,此前针对2024/2025财年的IEFA非加急年平均成本预测为53.16亿美元,现已被针对2026/2027财年的更新成本预测约69.60亿美元所取代。在拟议规则中,EB-5项目特定成本约为8,600万美元。在本最终规则中,经修订的EB-5项目特定成本约为1.05亿美元。
Regarding costs, the prior IEFA non-premium annual average cost projection of $5.316 billion for FY 2024/2025 is replaced with an updated FY 2026/2027 cost projection of approximately $6.960 billion. In the proposed rule, EB-5 program-specific costs were approximately $86 million. In this final rule, the revised EB-5 program-specific costs are about $105 million.
根据现行EB-5费用和更新后的假设,预计EB-5收入约为5,660万美元,由此产生的成本与收入差距约为4,840万美元。与拟议规则一致,最终EB-5费用表旨在弥合这一差距,并使收入与预计成本保持一致,符合《移民与国籍法》(INA)第286(m)条,8 U.S.C. 1356(m),以及《EB-5改革法》的要求。
Using current EB-5 fees and the updated assumptions, projected EB-5 revenue is approximately $56.6 million, resulting in a cost-revenue gap of roughly $48.4 million. The final EB-5 fee schedule, consistent with the proposed rule, is designed to close this gap and align revenue with projected costs, consistent with INA sec. 286(m),8 U.S.C. 1356(m), and the EB-5 Reform Act.
区域中心终止和重新确认的估计成本也已更新,并且,为回应公众意见,这些成本现在仅分摊至I-956表格(区域中心指定申请表)和I-956F表格(商业企业投资批准申请表)的初次申请,而不再分摊至I-956表格的修订申请。
The estimated cost of regional center terminations and reaffirmations is also updated, and, in response to public comments, those costs are now allocated only to initial Form I-956, Application for Regional Center Designation, and Form I-956F, Application for Approval of an Investment in a Commercial Enterprise, rather than to Form I-956 amendments.
4. EB-5诚信基金费用通货膨胀调整的变化
4. Change to Inflation Adjustment to EB-5 Integrity Fund Fees
DHS曾提议使用2022年上半年至2024年上半年的城市消费者消费价格指数(Consumer Price Index for All Urban Consumers, CPI-U)的通货膨胀率来提高EB-5诚信基金费用,并指出该时间段可能会根据最终规则发布时的当前通胀情况而改变。 见90 FR 48516, 48531-48532。在本最终规则中,DHS按照法律授权继续进行通货膨胀调整,但将时间段更改为反映2022年至2025年的CPI数据。因此,本最终规则所产生的增幅为10%。DHS因此将I-526E诚信基金费用从1,000美元提高到1,100美元;区域中心费用从10,000美元提高到11,000美元;对于某些区域中心,费用从20,000美元提高到22,000美元。[5] 这些调整有助于确保为项目诚信活动提供充足资金。
DHS proposed to increase EB-5 Integrity Fund fees by the rate of inflation using the Consumer Price Index for All Urban Consumers (CPI-U) from the first half of 2022 to the first half of 2024 and noted that the time period may change to reflect the current inflation at the time the final rule is issued. See90 FR 48516, 48531-48532. In this final rule, DHS maintains an adjustment for inflation, as authorized by statute, but changes the time period to reflect the CPI data from 2022 to 2025. As such, the resulting increase in this final rule is 10 percent. DHS thus increases the I-526E Integrity Fund fee from $1,000 to $1,100; the Regional Center fee from $10,000 to $11,000; and for certain Regional Center the fee goes from $20,000 to $22,000.[5] These adjustments help ensure sufficient funding for program integrity activities.
D. 最终费用摘要
D. Summary of Final Fees
下表1汇总了根据《EB-5改革法》(公法117-103,BB分部)由本规则设定的EB-5项目费用。“现行费用”栏反映DHS目前收取的费用,如本执行摘要前文及本序言后文所述。[6] “最终费用”栏显示由本规则设定的新费用,最后两栏显示美元差额和百分比差额。在某些情况下,最终费用包含额外的技术费,该技术费根据《EB-5改革法》第106(c)条的授权收取,用于支持USCIS信息技术系统的改进。其他费用包括区域中心终止和重新确认的成本,如本序言后文所述。所有经调整的费用均已纳入作为本次制定规则案卷一部分的经修订的G-1055表格,即费用表中。
Table 1 below summarizes the EB-5 program fees established by this rule pursuant to the EB-5 Reform Act (Public Law 117-103, div. BB). The Current Fee(s) column reflects the fees that DHS currently collects as explained earlier in this executive summary and later in this preamble.[6] The Final Fee(s) column shows the new fees set by this rule, with the last two columns displaying the dollar and percentage differences. In some cases, the final fee includes an additional technology fee, as authorized under section 106(c) of the EB-5 Reform Act, to support improvements to USCIS information technology systems. Other fees include the cost of regional center terminations and reaffirmations, as explained later in this preamble. All adjusted fees are incorporated into the revised Form G-1055, Fee Schedule, included in the rulemaking docket.

E. 成本和效益摘要
E. Summary of Costs and Benefits
国土安全部(DHS)最终确定的收费表将每年影响约16,600份EB-5项目表格申请,并使表格费用平均增加约70.7%,即按加权平均计算约增加2,945.90美元,影响个人投资者、区域中心以及参与推广项目投资的其他个人或企业。国土安全部估计,按未贴现计算,十年期和年化货币化成本分别约为806万美元和81万美元。按3%和7%的贴现率计算,十年期(2026财年至2035财年)数字分别为688万美元和566万美元。与提交新的I-527表格相关的影响,以及预期少量独立于主申请人提交的I-829表格申请所产生的影响,均被归类为成本,表格办理时间负担的变化亦归类为成本。最终的费用变更(针对现行的EB-5项目表格)将构成从申请人向国土安全部的转移支付,按3%和7%贴现率计算,十年期估计分别为41,674万美元和34,314万美元。罚款和费用也被归类为成本,但未进行估算和量化。
The fee schedule DHS finalizes will impact about 16,600 EB-5 program form filings annually and increase form fees by about 70.7 percent, or by about $2,945.90 (based on a weighted average) to individual investors, regional centers, and other persons or businesses involved in promoting program investments. DHS estimates that the 10-year and annualized monetized costs will be about $8.06 million and $0.81 million, in order, in undiscounted terms. At 3 and 7 percent discount rates, in order, the ten-year (FY 2026 through FY 2035) figures will be $6.88 million and $5.66 million. Impacts associated with filing the new Form I-527, as well as a few expected Form I-829 filings from dependents separate from the principal filers, are categorized as costs, as are changes in forms' time burdens. The final fee changes (for EB-5 program forms that currently exist) will constitute transfer payments from requestors to DHS, which are estimated at $416.74 million and $343.14 million, over a 10-year period at 3 and 7 percent discount rates, in order. Penalties and fees are also classified as costs but are not estimated and quantified.
根据有限的数据和信息,国土安全部确定,大多数区域中心以及几乎所有参与项目投资活动的新商业企业(NCE)和创造就业实体(JCE)根据1980年《监管灵活性法案》(RFA)将被视为小型实体。
Based on limited data and information, DHS determined that most regional centers and almost all New Commercial Enterprises (NCEs) and Job-Creating Entities (JCEs) involved in program investment activity will be small entities under the Regulatory Flexibility Act of 1980 (RFA).
二、背景
II. Background
A. EB-5项目
A. The EB-5 Program
国会于1990年设立EB-5项目,旨在通过移民投资者的创造就业和资本投资促进美国经济增长。《公法》101-649号,104 Stat. 4978(1990年11月29日)。区域中心项目于1992年增设(《公法》102-395号,第610条,106 Stat. 1828),并于2022年废止。根据《EB-5改革法》修订后,该项目每年向符合条件的移民及其受抚养人分配约10,000个签证名额,条件是其在美国企业投资至少1,050,000美元,或在目标就业地区(TEA)或基础设施项目中投资至少800,000美元,且该企业创造至少10个全职工作岗位。参见《移民与国籍法》(INA)第203(b)(5)(A)-(C)条,8 U.S.C. 1153(b)(5)(A)-(C)。多达90%的就业创造可通过与指定区域中心相关联的新商业企业间接创造的工作岗位来满足。INA第203(b)(5)(E)(iv)条,8 U.S.C. 1153(b)(5)(E)(iv)。
Congress established the EB-5 program in 1990 to promote U.S. economic growth through job creation and capital investment by immigrant investors. Public Law 101-649, 104 Stat. 4978 (Nov. 29, 1990). The regional center program was added in 1992 (Pub. L. 102-395, sect. 610, 106 Stat. 1828) and repealed in 2022. As amended by the EB-5 Reform Act, the program allocates approximately 10,000 visas annually to qualified immigrants and dependents who invest at least $1,050,000, or $800,000 in a targeted employment area (TEA) or infrastructure project, in a U.S. business that creates at least 10 full-time jobs. See INA sec. 203(b)(5)(A)-(C), 8 U.S.C. 1153(b)(5)(A)-(C). Up to 90 percent of job creation may be satisfied through indirect jobs in a new commercial enterprise associated with a designated regional center. INA sec. 203(b)(5)(E)(iv), 8 U.S.C. 1153(b)(5)(E)(iv).
美国公民及移民服务局(USCIS)负责管理EB-5项目并维护项目诚信,包括通过2013财年设立的投资者项目办公室(IPO)。《EB-5改革法》要求采取加强的欺诈、国家安全和公共安全措施,例如实地考察、背景调查和合规审计。参见INA第203(b)(5)(F)(iv)、(H)(iii)、(N)-(O)、(R)条,8 U.S.C. 1153(b)(5)(F)(iv)、(H)(iii)、(N)-(O)、(R);INA第103(a)(3)条,8 U.S.C. 1103(a)(3);8 CFR 103.2(b)(1)。USCIS开展调查、实地考察和审计,以核实资格及其支持证据。不利认定可能导致拒绝、撤销或终止身份。
USCIS administers the EB-5 program and maintains program integrity, including through the IPO established in FY 2013. The EB-5 Reform Act requires enhanced fraud, national security, and public safety measures, such as site visits, background checks, and compliance audits. See INA sec. 203(b)(5)(F)(iv), (H)(iii), (N)-(O), (R), 8 U.S.C. 1153(b)(5)(F)(iv), (H)(iii), (N)-(O), (R); INA sec. 103(a)(3), 8 U.S.C. 1103(a)(3); 8 CFR 103.2(b)(1). USCIS conducts investigations, site visits, and audits to verify eligibility and supporting evidence. Adverse findings may result in denial, revocation, or termination of status.
《EB-5改革法》授权国土安全部设定费用,以弥补审理、项目管理、审计和合规活动的成本。参见《公法》117-103号,BB分部,第106(b)条、第106(c)条。这些成本在本规则的后续部分中详细说明。 (印刷页第61944页)
The EB-5 Reform Act authorizes DHS to set fees to recover costs for adjudication, program management, audits, and compliance activities. SeePublic Law 117-103, div. BB, sec. 106(b), 106(c). These costs are detailed in subsequent sections of this rule. ( printed page 61944)
B. USCIS费用
B. USCIS Fees
USCIS的资金主要来源于向申请人、申请方和请求人就移民和入籍福利请求收取的费用。USCIS管理以下四个费用账户:
USCIS is primarily funded by fees charged to applicants, petitioners, and requesters for immigration and naturalization benefit requests. USCIS manages the following four fee accounts:
移民审查费账户(IEFA),其中包括加急处理收入(INA第286(m)、(n)、(t)和(u)条;8 U.S.C. 1356(m)、(n)、(t)和(u));
The IEFA, which includes premium processing revenues (INA secs. 286(m), (n), (t), and (u);8 U.S.C. 1356(m), (n), (t), and (u));
欺诈预防和侦测账户(INA第214(c)(12)和(13)条、第286(v)条;8 U.S.C. 1184(c)(12)和(13)条、1356(v)条);
The Fraud Prevention and Detection Account (INA secs. 214(c)(12) and (13), 286(v);8 U.S.C. 1184(c)(12) and (13), 1356(v));
H-1B非移民申请人账户(INA第214(c)(9)和(11)条、第286(s)条;8 U.S.C. 1184(c)(9)和(11)条、1356(s)条);以及
The H-1B Nonimmigrant Petitioner Account (INA secs. 214(c)(9) and (11), 286(s);8 U.S.C. 1184(c)(9) and (11), 1356(s)); and
EB-5诚信基金(INA第203(b)(5)(J)条,8 U.S.C. 1153(b)(5)(J))。
The EB-5 Integrity Fund (INA sec. 203(b)(5)(J),8 U.S.C. 1153(b)(5)(J)).
当美国公民及移民服务局(USCIS)提供裁决和入籍服务时，其有权将移民审查费账户(IEFA)费用设定在能够确保收回提供所有此类服务全部成本的水平。 见《移民与国籍法》(INA)第286(m)条，8 U.S.C. 1356(m)。从提交移民利益申请的个人和实体收取的费用存入移民审查费账户(IEFA)。 同上。这些费用用于资助裁决移民利益申请的成本，包括向难民、庇护申请人及某些其他申请人或请愿人免费提供的服务。在2025财年全年预算中，移民审查费账户(IEFA)约占USCIS总资金的95%。[7] EB-5诚信基金在2025财年资金中占比不到1%。USCIS剩余资金在2025财年来自拨款(约4%)或其他费用账户(约1%)。虽然加急处理费用也属于移民审查费账户(IEFA)费用，但本规则并未对加急处理费进行调整，也未将加急处理的成本或收入纳入拟议规则中所述的EB-5费用设定方法。 见90 FR 48516, 48522-48530。
When USCIS provides adjudication and naturalization services, it is authorized to set IEFA fees at a level that will ensure recovery of the full costs of providing all such services. See INA sec. 286(m), 8 U.S.C. 1356(m). The fees that are collected from individuals and entities filing immigration benefit requests are deposited into the IEFA. Id. These fees fund the cost of adjudicating immigration benefit requests, including those provided without charge to refugee, asylum, and certain other applicants or petitioners. The IEFA accounted for approximately 95 percent of total funding for USCIS in the FY 2025 full-year budget.[7] The EB-5 Integrity Fund represented less than 1 percent of FY 2025 funding. The remaining USCIS funding came from appropriations (approximately 4 percent) or other fee accounts (approximately 1 percent) in FY 2025. While premium processing funds are also IEFA fees, this rule does not make premium processing fee changes or consider premium processing costs or revenue as part of the EB-5 fee setting approach described in the proposed rule. See90 FR 48516, 48522-48530.
欺诈预防与检测账户[8]和H-1B非移民申请人账户[9]均由法律规定具体金额的费用提供资金。国土安全部(DHS)无权调整这些账户的费用。EB-5诚信基金是2023财年新设立的账户，将在本序言后文及拟议规则的单独章节中讨论。 见本序言第III.C节和第IV.F节；另见90 FR 48516, 48530-48535。
The Fraud Prevention and Detection Account [8] and H-1B Nonimmigrant Petitioner Account [9] are both funded by fees for which the dollar amount is set by statute. DHS has no authority to adjust the fees for these accounts. The EB-5 Integrity Fund, a new account established in FY 2023, is discussed later in this preamble and in a separate section of the proposed rule. See section III.C and IV.F of this preamble; see also90 FR 48516, 48530-48535l.
自EB-5项目设立以来，该项目一直由国土安全部(DHS)依据移民审查费账户(IEFA)授权设定的费用提供资金。历史上，为提供USCIS服务而收取并存入移民审查费账户(IEFA)的费用通常被称为“IEFA费用”。 参见，例如，89 FR 6194。提供此类服务的成本，通常作为制定IEFA费用的依据，被称为“IEFA成本”。 同上。如下文所述，2025年的一项法院裁决将EB-5费用恢复至2016年设定的水平。[10]
Since its inception, the EB-5 program has been funded by fees set by DHS under the IEFA authority. Historically, fees charged for USCIS services are deposited into the IEFA are generally described as “IEFA fees.” See, e.g.,89 FR 6194. The costs to provide such services, which are generally used as the basis to develop the IEFA fees, are described as “IEFA costs.” Id. A court ruling in 2025 reverted the EB-5 fees to those set in 2016, as explained below.[10]
C. 先前EB-5费用法规的现状
C. Status of Previous EB-5 Fee Regulations
在2022/2023财年费用规则中，国土安全部(DHS)根据《移民与国籍法》(INA)第286(m)条，8 U.S.C. 1356(m)，并依照行政管理和预算局(OMB)A-25号通知的规定，采用基于作业成本法(ABC)的全成本回收模型，调整了USCIS费用表，包括EB-5项目费用。 见89 FR 6194(2024年1月31日)；88 FR 402, 418(2023年1月4日)。该规则在各类利益申请中分摊IEFA成本，一般未将EB-5费用限定在低于全成本回收模型所示的金额。由于《EB-5改革法》所要求的EB-5专项费用研究尚未完成，当时的EB-5费用并未依据该法规定的处理时限目标及更为具体的项目参数设定。相比之下，本最终规则中的EB-5费用则是基于《EB-5改革法》框架、预期处理时限以及一项修订后的费用研究而设定的。
In the FY 2022/2023 fee rule, DHS adjusted the USCIS fee schedule, including EB-5 program fees, using a full cost recovery model based on Activity-Based Costing (ABC) under INA section 286(m), 8 U.S.C. 1356(m), consistent with OMB Circular A-25. See89 FR 6194 (Jan. 31, 2024); 88 FR 402, 418 (Jan. 4, 2023). That rule allocated IEFA costs across benefit types and generally did not cap EB-5 fees below the amounts indicated by the full cost recovery model. Because the EB-5-specific fee study required by the EB-5 Reform Act had not yet been completed, those EB-5 fees were not set using the statute's processing time goals and narrower program-specific parameters. By contrast, the EB-5 fees in this final rule are based on the EB-5 Reform Act framework, its anticipated processing times, and a revised fee study.
2025年11月12日，美国科罗拉多州地区法院在 Moody诉Noem案 中作出裁决，2025 WL 3157554 (D. Colo.)，中止了2022/2023财年费用规则中确立的若干EB-5相关费用，该规则于2024年4月1日起生效。 见89 FR 6194。法院认定，《EB-5改革法》禁止国土安全部(DHS)在该规则中调整EB-5项目费用。 Moody案，第*10-11页。截至2025年11月12日，USCIS已恢复接受2024年3月31日之前有效的EB-5费用。[11]
On November 12, 2025, the United States District Court for the District of Colorado issued a decision in Moody v. Noem, 2025 WL 3157554 (D. Colo.), staying certain EB-5-related fees established in the FY 2022/2023 fee rule, which became effective April 1, 2024. See89 FR 6194. The court concluded that the EB-5 Reform Act precluded DHS from adjusting EB-5 program fees in that rule. Moody, at *10-11. As of November 12, 2025, USCIS reverted to accepting the EB-5 fees that were in effect until March 31, 2024.[11]
D. 相关立法与政策
D. Related Rulemakings and Policies
国土安全部(DHS)正在进行多项处于不同制定阶段的移民相关立法工作。国土安全部(DHS)认识到，政策和法规的变化可能影响USCIS的人员配置需求、成本、费用收入及处理时限，包括EB-5工作量的相关事项。按照以往做法，国土安全部(DHS)已考虑其他相关规则对本EB-5费用规则可能产生的外围、重叠或相互关联的影响，并在可能的范围内分析了可能影响或与本行动在实质上重叠的规则的潜在影响。
DHS is engaged in multiple immigration-related rulemakings that are at various stages of development. DHS recognizes that policy and regulatory changes can affect USCIS staffing needs, costs, fee revenue, and processing times, including those for EB-5 workloads. Consistent with prior practice, DHS has considered other relevant rules for peripheral, overlapping, or interrelated effects on this EB-5 fee rule and has analyzed, to the extent possible, the potential effects of rules that may impact or substantively overlap with this action.
国土安全部(DHS)在可行范围内，还考虑了USCIS所知悉的、尚在进行中或预期中的立法及政策变化对本规则的影响。移民政策经常发生变化，由于规则制定和最终确定所需的时间，部分举措可能未能在本规则中完全体现。因此，国土安全部(DHS)不能保证已将美国政府各层级可能直接或间接影响EB-5项目的所有未来政策变化均纳入考量。
DHS has also considered, to the extent practicable, the effects on this rule of intervening or anticipated legislation and policy changes of which USCIS is aware. Immigration policy changes frequently, and some initiatives may not be fully reflected in this rule due to the time required for rule development and finalization. DHS therefore cannot represent that it has accounted for every future policy change at all levels of the U.S. Government that may directly or indirectly affect the EB-5 program.
然而,国土安全部(DHS)认为其已审查并考虑了本规则制定所涉及问题的所有相关方面,回应了所有实质性公众意见,并根据适用的法定授权(包括《EB-5改革法案》)对EB-5费用变更及相关规定提供了合理的解释。 (印刷页61945) 如果国土安全部的其他规则或重大政策变化对EB-5费用、运作或诚信措施产生直接且重大的影响,本序言的相关章节将讨论这些相互影响。
However, DHS believes it has examined and considered all relevant aspects of the issues addressed by this rulemaking, responded to all substantive public comments, and provided a reasoned explanation for the EB-5 fee changes and related provisions consistent with applicable statutory authorities, including the EB-5 Reform ( printed page 61945) Act. Where other DHS rules or major policy changes have a direct and material effect on EB-5 fees, operations, or integrity measures, those interactions are discussed in the relevant sections of this preamble.
1. 《2022年EB-5改革与诚信法案》;确保EB-5项目的诚信性;移民身份申请的自动撤销
1. EB-5 Reform and Integrity Act of 2022; Ensuring the Integrity of the EB-5 Program; Automatic Revocation of Petitions for Immigrant Classification
与本EB-5费用规则分开,国土安全部于2026年7月2日提议了一项名为《2022年EB-5改革与诚信法案;确保EB-5项目的诚信性;移民身份申请的自动撤销》的规则。参见91 FR 40676。该拟议规则将实施《EB-5改革法案》对EB-5签证类别和区域中心项目所做的法定改革。一般而言,根据EB-5项目,外国人如在美国对新设商业企业进行规定的投资,并为符合条件的美国工人创造10个永久性全职工作岗位,即有资格申请合法永久居民身份。该单独的规则制定提议进行改革,以提高该项目的诚信性并澄清资格要求,而本规则制定仅限于确立和调整与EB-5相关的费用及相关资金机制。
Separate from this EB-5 fee rule, on July 2, 2026, DHS proposed a rule titled EB-5 Reform and Integrity Act of 2022; Ensuring the Integrity of the EB-5 Program; Automatic Revocation of Petitions for Immigrant Classification. See91 FR 40676. This proposed rule would implement the statutory reforms that the EB-5 Reform Act made to the EB-5 visa category and the Regional Center Program. In general, under the EB-5 program, aliens are eligible to apply for lawful permanent resident status if they make the required investment in a new commercial enterprise in the United States and create 10 permanent full-time jobs for qualified U.S. workers. That separate rulemaking proposes changes to improve the integrity of the program and clarify eligibility requirements, whereas this rulemaking is limited to establishing and adjusting EB-5-related fees and associated funding mechanisms.
2. 美国公民及移民服务局(USCIS)对生物识别信息的采集与使用
2. Collection and Use of Biometrics by U.S. Citizenship and Immigration Services
2025年11月3日,国土安全部提议了一项单独的规则,以修订有关生物识别信息采集与使用的规定。参见90 FR 49062。该生物识别规则除其他事项外,将要求任何年龄的个人(如提交或涉及移民福利申请或其他受涵盖申请,除非获得豁免)提交生物识别信息;扩大在外国人被逮捕时采集生物识别信息的权限;对“生物识别信息”进行定义;将重复使用要求予以法典化;将DNA检测、使用和存储要求予以法典化并加以扩展;确立“特殊情况”标准以免除未能按时参加生物识别预约的责任;修改某些申请人证明良好道德品行的方式;并澄清可采集和使用生物识别信息的目的。
On November 3, 2025, DHS proposed a separate rule to amend regulations governing the collection and use of biometrics. See90 FR 49062. That biometrics rule would, among other things, require submission of biometrics by any individual, regardless of age, who files or is associated with an immigration benefit request or other covered request (unless exempted); expand biometrics collection authority upon alien arrest; define “biometrics;” codify reuse requirements; codify and expand DNA testing, use, and storage; establish an “extraordinary circumstances” standard to excuse failure to appear for a biometrics appointment; modify how certain applicants demonstrate good moral character; and clarify the purposes for which biometrics may be collected and used.
该生物识别规则制定与本EB-5费用规则不同,但在操作上与之相关。就生物识别政策对USCIS成本或流程产生的影响而言,现行生物识别要求的影响已反映在本费用规则所采用的基础成本和工作量假设之中,但2025年11月3日拟议规则中提出的变更并未纳入考虑,本EB-5费用规则本身也未确立或修订任何生物识别要求。
This biometrics rulemaking is distinct from, but operationally related to, this EB-5 fee rule. To the extent biometrics policies affect USCIS costs or processes, the effects of current biometrics requirements are reflected in the underlying cost and workload assumptions used in this fee rule, but the changes proposed in the November 3, 2025, proposed rule are not considered, and biometrics requirements themselves are not established or revised by this EB-5 fee rule.
E. 可分割性
E. Severability
国土安全部认为,本规则中的各项规定具有可分割性,可以独立运作,这与现行法规下对其他USCIS费用的处理方式一致。参见89 FR 6194, 6237-6238(2024年1月31日);另参见8 CFR 106.6。例如,EB-5诚信基金罚款费用即便被禁止执行或中止,也不会影响EB-5表格费用或技术费的有效性或运作。如果国土安全部被禁止征收本规则确立的任何特定费用,国土安全部认为,任何中止、禁令或撤销均可被严格限定于该特定费用或该组费用。在此情况下,USCIS可以继续开展EB-5相关业务,可能会在规模上有所缩减或进行资源调整,同时国土安全部将开展进一步的规则制定工作,以解决法院所指出的具体缺陷。国土安全部进一步认为,允许其余费用继续有效,将避免对EB-5项目造成不必要的干扰,并能更好地支持国会关于及时处理EB-5申请的目标。
DHS believes that the provisions in this rule are severable and can operate independently, consistent with the treatment of other USCIS fees under current regulations. See89 FR 6194, 6237-6238 (Jan. 31, 2024); see also8 CFR 106.6. For example, the EB-5 Integrity Fund penalty fees could be enjoined or stayed without affecting the validity or operation of the EB-5 form fees or the technology fee. If DHS were prohibited from collecting any particular fee established by this rule, DHS believes that any stay, injunction, or vacatur could be narrowly tailored to that specific fee or set of fees. In such circumstances, USCIS could continue EB-5 operations—potentially at a reduced level or with resource adjustments—while DHS undertakes additional rulemaking to address the specific deficiency identified by a court. DHS further believes that allowing the remaining fees to remain in effect would avoid unnecessary disruption to the EB-5 program and would better support Congress' objective of timely processing EB-5 petitions.
III. 对拟议规则的修改
III. Changes From the Proposed Rule
本最终规则采纳了经适当修改的、于2025年10月23日在《联邦公报》上公布的拟议规则文本。[12] 国土安全部(DHS)根据就拟议规则收到的意见或因这些变更产生的影响,在本最终规则中作出若干变更。例如,根据公众意见,国土安全部更新了数据和成本估算,采用了更新近的信息,这导致最终费用与拟议规则中的费用有所不同。[13] 正如本序言通篇所述,国土安全部行使其自由裁量权来确定费用。本最终规则也依据拟议规则中阐明的理由,但本规则通篇针对公众意见、新出现的情况和新信息所作的修改和说明除外。每项变更说明如下:
This final rule adopts, with appropriate changes, the regulatory text in the proposed rule published in the Federal Register on October 23, 2025.[12] DHS is making several changes in this final rule based on comments received on the proposed rule or as required by the effects of those changes. For example, based on public comments, DHS updated data and cost estimates to use more recent information which resulted in different final fees than in the proposed rule.[13] As explained throughout this preamble, DHS exercises its discretionary authority to establish fees. This final rule also relies on the justifications articulated in the proposed rule, except as modified and explained throughout this rule in response to public comments, intervening developments, and new information. A description of each change is as follows:
A. 现行费用的变更
A. Change in Current Fees
国土安全部在本最终规则中对拟议费用作出了变更。本序言中所示的现行费用与拟议规则中列出的费用有所不同。 参见,例如,《联邦公报》第90卷第48516、48517页。现行费用是2022/23财年费用规则之前的费用,这是依据 Moody诉Noem案 (2025 WL 3157554,(科罗拉多地区法院))判决的要求,该判决中止了国土安全部编纂的某些与EB-5相关的费用。 参见《联邦公报》第89卷第6194页(2024年1月31日)。2025年11月12日,美国公民及移民服务局(USCIS)恢复接受截至2024年3月31日前有效的EB-5费用。[14] USCIS告知公众,对于2025年11月26日之前邮戳的材料,将接受此前的费用。[15]
DHS is making changes to the proposed fees in this final rule. The current fees shown in this preamble are different from the fees listed in the proposed rule. See, e.g.,90 FR 48516, 48517. The current fees are those from before the FY 2022/23 Fee Rule, as required by the decision in Moody v. Noem, 2025 WL 3157554, (D. Colo.), that stayed certain EB-5 related fees that were codified by DHS. See89 FR 6194 (Jan. 31, 2024). On November 12, 2025, USCIS reverted to accepting the EB-5 fees that were in effect until March 31, 2024.[14] USCIS informed the public that they would accept the previous fee for items postmarked before November 26, 2025.[15]
(印刷页第61946页)
( printed page 61946)
B. 修订后的移民审查费账户(IEFA)费用
B. Revised IEFA Fees
国土安全部在拟议规则中提议的IEFA费用范围为55美元至29,935美元。 参见,例如,《联邦公报》第90卷第48516、48517页。在本最终规则中,国土安全部根据公众意见、EB-5费用研究的修订以及更新的数据,设定了修订后的费用。 请参阅本序言第I.D节前文中的表1,了解现行、拟议和最终费用的比较。修订后的费用主要是预算和工作量假设变更的结果。我们根据意见反馈修订了预算和工作量估算,以采用更新近的规划信息。与拟议规则一致,这些费用将仅用于回收EB-5项目的成本,而不会回收其他项目的成本,USCIS费用规则通常将此称为成本再分配。 参见,例如,《联邦公报》第90卷第48516、48525-48526页。
DHS proposed a range of IEFA fees from $55 to $29,935 in the proposed rule. See, e.g.,90 FR 48516, 48517. In this final rule, DHS sets revised fees in response to public comments, revisions to the EB-5 Fee Study, and updated data. See Table 1 earlier in section I.D. of this preamble for a comparison of the current, proposed, and final fees. The revised fees are mainly the result of changes to budget and workload assumptions. We revised budget and workload estimates to use more recent planning information in response to comments. Consistent with the proposed rule, these fees will only recover the cost of the EB-5 program and will not recover costs for other programs, which USCIS fee rules typically refer to as cost reallocation. See, e.g.,90 FR 48516, 48525-48526.
C. 对EB-5费用研究的修订
C. Revisions to the EB-5 Fee Study
在拟议规则中,国土安全部依据2024/2025财年费用审查的结果以及EB-5费用研究的结果来计算拟议费用。在本最终规则中,国土安全部修订了EB-5费用研究及其结果,采用了2026/2027财年费用审查中更新的估算数据。EB-5费用研究的两个版本采用相同的方法,但修订后的EB-5费用研究根据评论者的要求采用了更新的数据。
In the proposed rule, DHS relied on the results of an FY 2024/2025 fee review and the results of the EB-5 fee study to calculate the proposed fees. In this final rule, DHS revises the EB-5 fee study and its results to use more recent estimates from a FY 2026/2027 fee review. Both versions of the EB-5 fee study use the same methodology, but the revised EB-5 fee study uses newer data, as requested by commenters.
1. 数量和完成率估算的变更
1. Changes to Volume and Completion Rate Estimates
在拟议规则中,国土安全部说明了其所使用的数量和完成率,以及这些数据如何影响EB-5费用研究。[16] 例如,USCIS利用历史和近期数量趋势、统计预测以及USCIS各办公室的专业知识来估算年度工作量。完成率反映所谓的“接触时间”,即具有裁决职责的员工处理案件所花费的时间。[17] 工作量和完成率估算使USCIS能够确定人员配置,这会影响USCIS的预算和费用。
In the proposed rule, DHS explained the volumes and completion rates that it used and how those affected the EB-5 fee study.[16] For example, USCIS estimates annual workload for using historical and recent volume trends, statistical forecasts, and subject-matter expertise from various USCIS offices. Completion rates reflect what is termed “touch time,” or the time an employee with adjudicative responsibilities handles the case.[17] The workload and completion rate estimates allow USCIS to determine staffing allocations, which affect the USCIS budget and fees.
针对相关意见,国土安全部(DHS)修订了数量和完成率估算,以采用2026财年和2027财年的预测数据。与拟议规则中的数量一样,本最终规则中的数量估算系由美国公民及移民服务局(USCIS)数量预测委员会(VPC)商定。VPC的使命是促进工作量及费用预测数据的形成,并协调有关预测工作量的决策。这一机构内部小组为有关预测工作量的决策提供了一个论坛,其间融入了USCIS内部专业领域专家的意见,在某些情况下还包括来自其他政府机构的数据。VPC利用历史及近期的数量趋势、统计预测以及来自USCIS各业务部门和项目办公室(包括投资者项目办公室(IPO)、USCIS服务中心、国家福利中心以及各地区、分区和外勤办公室)的专业知识,预测USCIS的年度工作量。USCIS在2025年6月的会议期间制作了本最终规则中的大部分估算,以估算2026财年及其后的情况。这些会议是VPC最后一次为2026财年制作全面的数量估算。USCIS利用VPC的估算结果来确定人员配置水平、编制未来年度预算并估算未来收入。尽管VPC未对I-527表的工作量进行预测,但USCIS依据本规则所需的I-527表预测,采用了主题专家(SME)的估算结果。
In response to comments, DHS revises the volume and completion rate estimates to use FY 2026 and FY 2027 forecasts. Like the volumes in the proposed rule, the volume estimates in this final rule were agreed upon by the USCIS Volume Projection Committee (VPC). The mission of the VPC is to facilitate workload and fee projection data, and coordination of decisions about projected workload. This intra-agency group provides a forum for decisions about projected workload with input from subject matter experts from within USCIS and, in some cases, data from other government agencies. The VPC predicts USCIS annual workload volumes using historical and recent volume trends, statistical forecasts, and subject-matter expertise from various USCIS directorates and program offices, including the IPO, USCIS service centers, the National Benefits Center, and regional, district, and field offices. USCIS produced most of the estimates in this final rule during the meetings in June 2025 to estimate the FY 2026 and beyond. These meetings were the final time that the VPC produced comprehensive volume estimates for FY 2026. USCIS uses VPC estimates to determine staffing levels, budget for upcoming years, and estimate future revenue. While the VPC did not forecast Form I-527 workload, USCIS relied on SME estimates for the Form I-527 forecast required for this rule.
工作量是确定USCIS处理EB-5福利申请所需资源的关键要素,以便平均而言能够在《EB-5改革法案》规定的处理时限目标内完成处理。EB-5项目工作量是将活动成本分配给EB-5移民福利申请的主要成本驱动因素。表3列出了本最终规则中EB-5移民福利申请的预计年平均收件量:
Workload volume is a key element used to determine the USCIS resources needed to process EB-5 benefit requests on average within the processing time goals established in the EB-5 Reform Act. EB-5 program workload volume is the primary cost driver for assigning activity costs to EB-5 immigration benefit requests. Table 3 displays the projected average annual receipts for EB-5 immigration benefit requests in this final rule:

本最终规则中EB-5的总工作量高于拟议规则,但部分预测数值低于拟议规则。例如,USCIS预计本最终规则中I-956表的收件量预测将低于拟议规则中的预测。总体而言,这些差异是因为最终规则的预测采用了更新的信息。[18] 见下文表4,比较最终规则与拟议规则中的年平均收件量预测。

(印刷页61948)
( printed page 61948)
如拟议规则所述,USCIS完成率用以确定完成(即就特定移民福利申请作出决定)所需的裁决时间。[19] 每种福利类型的完成率代表一个平均值,复杂申请可能需要更多时间,而其他申请则可能需要较少时间。完成率反映的是所谓的“接触时间”,即具有裁决职责的员工处理案件所花费的时间。该比率并不反映“排队等待时间”,例如等待补充证据或主管批准所花费的时间。完成率并不反映申请人、请愿人和申请提出者在USCIS受理其申请后,等待案件决定所需的总处理时间。
As explained in the proposed rule, USCIS completion rates identify the adjudicative time required to complete (render a decision on) specific immigration benefit requests.[19] The completion rate for each benefit type represents an average and complex requests may require more time, and others less. Completion rates reflect what is termed “touch time,” or the time an employee with adjudicative responsibilities handles the case. This rate does not reflect “queue time,” or time spent waiting, for example, for additional evidence or supervisory approval. Completion rates do not reflect the total processing time applicants, petitioners, and requestors can expect to wait for a decision on their case after USCIS accepts it.
本次EB-5费用研究所使用的完成率估算,由USCIS绩效与质量办公室(OPQ)利用历史数据及IPO提供的主题专家意见制定而成。本最终规则中的大多数完成率使用了来自IPO 2026财年人员配置分配模型的信息。拟议规则中的大多数完成率则使用了较旧的信息。本最终规则中部分完成率估算未作变动,因为它们仍然代表对相关工作量的最佳估计。如拟议规则所述,部分工作量在计算费用时并未使用完成率。[20] 见表5,比较拟议规则与最终规则中所使用的完成率。
The completion rates for this EB-5 fee study are estimates developed by USCIS' Office of Performance and Quality (OPQ), using historical data and subject matter expert input from IPO. Most completion rates in this final rule use information from the IPO staffing allocation model for FY 2026. Most completion rates in the proposed rule used older information. Some completion rate estimates did not change in this final rule because they still represent the best estimate for the workload. Some workloads do not use completion rates to calculate the fees, as explained in the proposed rule. 20 See Table 5 for a comparison of the completion rates used in the proposed and final rules.
(印刷页61949)
( printed page 61949)
2. 成本预测的变化
2. Changes to Cost Projections
在拟议规则中,国土安全部说明,EB-5费用研究的成本预测系基于整体移民审查费账户(IEFA)非加急费用成本以及EB-5项目特定成本制定,并考虑了在职及新聘员工的工资及非工资成本、通货膨胀、资源调整,以及自2024财年运营计划起取消临时项目成本等因素。见90 FR 48516,48522-48524页;表6(汇总了约53.159亿美元的2024/2025财年年平均IEFA非加急费用成本预测),以及88 FR 402,484-485页(2023年1月4日);89 FR 6194,6277-6278页(2024年1月31日)(生物识别服务成本处理)。该预测使用了2024财年和2025财年的数量预测数据。见90 FR 48516,48523-48524页。
In the proposed rule, DHS explained that the EB-5 fee study cost projection was developed using overall IEFA non-premium costs in addition to EB-5 program-specific costs, and that it accounted for payroll and non-payroll for on-board and new staff, inflation, resource adjustments, and the removal of temporary program costs, starting from the FY 2024 Operating Plan. See90 FR 48516, 48522-48524; Table 6 (summarizing the FY 2024/2025 annual average IEFA non-premium cost projection of approximately $5,315.9 million), and 88 FR 402, 484-485 (Jan. 4, 2023); 89 FR 6194, 6277-6278 (Jan. 31, 2024) (biometric services cost treatment). It used volume forecasts for FY 2024 and FY 2025. See90 FR 48516, 48523-48524.
在本最终规则中,美国公民及移民服务局(USCIS)更新了移民审查费账户(IEFA)的非加急费用、项目成本和工作量预测,改为采用2026/2027财政年度双年期数据,以回应公众意见并为未来财政年度作出规划。这些更新后的预测取代了拟议规则中2024/2025财政年度的成本估算,用于最终EB-5费用的计算,并体现在本序言的经济分析中。美国公民及移民服务局依据最近完成的2026/2027财政年度费用审查所获得的信息,并作出了额外调整,以计入诸如I-527表格等并未纳入2026/2027财政年度费用审查范围的新工作量。也就是说,美国公民及移民服务局复制了2026/2027财政年度费用审查的内容,并对其作出了一些修改,例如加入I-527表格的数据,用于修订版EB-5费用研究。2026/2027财政年度修订后的IEFA非加急年平均成本预测约为69.60亿美元,费用模型中所采用的EB-5项目特定成本预测约为1.05亿美元。为得出该更新后的成本预测,美国公民及移民服务局以其总体2025财政年度运营计划为起点,并针对该财政年度剩余时间内估计的部分重返工作场所成本作出轻微调整。随后,美国公民及移民服务局在本次审查中作出以下调整:
In this final rule, USCIS updates the IEFA non-premium costs, program cost and workload projections to use the FY 2026/2027 biennial period to respond to public comments and plan for future fiscal years. These updated projections replace the proposed rule's FY 2024/2025 cost estimates for purposes of the final EB-5 fee calculations and are reflected in the economic analysis in this preamble. USCIS relied on information from the recently completed FY 2026/2027 fee review and made additional changes to account for new workloads, like Form I-527, which were not part of the FY 2026/2027 fee review. Meaning, USCIS copied the FY 2026/2027 fee review and made some changes to it, such as adding data for Form I-527, to use in the Revised EB-5 Fee Study. The revised IEFA nonpremium annual average cost projection for FY 2026/2027 is approximately $6,960.0 million, and the EB-5 program-specific cost projection used in the fee model is approximately $105 million. To arrive at this updated cost projection, USCIS started with its general FY 2025 Operating Plan, which was slightly adjusted for some return to workplace costs estimated for the remainder of the fiscal year. USCIS then made the following adjustments in this review:
根据2026财政年度和2027财政年度人员配置分配模型(SAM)的增编,以及针对欺诈检测与国家安全局(FDNS)提出的非SAM增编请求,增加了人员配置,到2027财政年度末,美国公民及移民服务局大多数办公室共计新增6,045个职位。SAM增编体现了近期行政命令的影响,以及该机构最新的结案率估算。FDNS非SAM增编为2025财政年度新增167个职位,已获批准,以应对第14157号和第14161号行政命令[21] 开始逐步扩大招聘,总体目标是增强美国公民及移民服务局的审查与甄别能力,每年平均成本为3,540万美元。FDNS的SAM增编包括574个职位,在该双年期内平均每年成本为9,380万美元,以继续落实上述行政命令;
Added staffing based on the FY 2026 and FY 2027 Staffing Allocation Model (SAM) enhancements and a non-SAM enhancement request for the Fraud Detection and National Security Directorate (FDNS), for a total of 6,045 new positions across most USCIS offices by the end of FY 2027. The SAM enhancements incorporate the effect of recent Executive Orders, as well as the most recent agency completion rate estimates. The FDNS non-SAM enhancement of 167 positions in FY 2025 was approved to start ramping up hiring in response to Executive Orders 14157 and 14161 [21] with the overall goal to enhance USCIS' vetting and screening capabilities and an average cost of $35.4 million per year. The FDNS SAM includes 574 positions with an average cost of $93.8 million per year over the biennial period to continue the implementation of those Executive Orders;
计入了薪资通胀以及晋升、档次内增薪等因素,其中包括联邦雇员年度薪资和生活成本调整。2026财政年度和2027财政年度假定的通胀率为3%;以及
Accounted for pay inflation and promotions/within-grade increases, which includes annual Federal employee pay and cost of living adjustments. The assumed inflation rate was 3 percent for FY 2026 and FY 2027; and
考虑了净增加成本,例如额外预算项目的成本。例如,美国公民及移民服务局计入了在该双年期内全面接管保险箱业务运营的成本(2.311亿美元)、为容纳新增人员而建设新设施及租赁所产生的成本(2.781亿美元)、新建国家档案中心的成本(1.147亿美元),以及新建选民核实系统的成本(7,530万美元)。 (印刷页第61950页)
Considered net additional costs, such as the costs of additional budget items. For example, USCIS added the cost of fully taking over lockbox operations during the biennial period ($231.1 million), the cost associated with building and rent of new facilities to accommodate additional staff ($278.1 million), the building of the new National Records Center ($114.7 million), and the new Voter Verification System ($75.3 million). ( printed page 61950)

Table 6 is a summary from the starting point of the FY 2025 IEFA non-premium annual operating plan to the FY 2026/2027 annual average cost projection. The FY 2026/2027 annual average cost projection is estimated to be $6,960 million.
3. 收入预测的变化
3. Changes to Revenue Projections
在拟议规则中,国土安全部(DHS)说明,EB-5收入预测基于由体量预测中心(VPC)制定并批准的内部收件预测,该中心采用统计建模、历史收件数据以及相关业务部门的专业意见,以预测各表格层面的申请量。 参见联邦公报第90卷第48516页,第48524至48526页。对于EB-5各表格,美国公民及移民服务局假定付费率为100%,因为IEFA的EB-5费用不适用费用减免或豁免,并估计按照现行IEFA费用(依据2022/2023财政年度费用规则设定)计算,在2024/2025财政年度期间,每年将产生约41.923亿美元的IEFA非加急收入,其中约1.075亿美元来自EB-5各表格。 参见拟议规则表7。
In the proposed rule, DHS explained that EB-5 revenue projections were based on internal receipt forecasts developed and approved by the VPC, which uses statistical modeling, historical receipt data, and subject-matter input from relevant directorates to project form-level volumes. See90 FR 48516, 48524-48526. For EB-5 forms, USCIS assumed a 100-percent fee-paying rate because IEFA EB-5 fees are not subject to fee waivers or exemptions, and estimated that current IEFA fees (as set in the FY 2022/2023 fee rule) would generate an average of approximately $4,192.3 million in annual IEFA non-premium revenue during FY 2024/2025, including about $107.5 million from EB-5 forms. See Table 7 of the proposed rule.
在本最终规则中,美国公民及移民服务局(USCIS)已根据公众意见、更新的规划数据以及更新的收据信息的可用性,对EB-5收入预测进行了更新。修订后的预测纳入了这些新数据、针对2026/2027财年调整后的数量预测,以及本规则采纳的最终费用水平。利用这些估算数据和现行费用,预计2026/2027财年EB-5年均收入约为5,660万美元,移民审查手续费账户(IEFA)非保费收入总额约为55.611亿美元。这些采用现行费用计算的修订后收入估算取代了拟议规则中针对2024/2025财年的预测,用于最终费用分析,并与更新后的成本预测一并用于评估最终EB-5费用表是否足以收回预计的项目成本。
For this final rule, USCIS has updated the EB-5 revenue projections in response to public comments, more recent planning data, and the availability of updated receipt information. The revised projections incorporate this new data, adjusted volume forecasts for FY 2026/2027, and the final fee levels adopted in this rule. Using those estimates and current fees, the estimated average annual EB-5 revenue is approximately $56.6 million and total IEFA non-premium revenue of approximately $5,561.1 million for FY 2026/2027. These revised revenue estimates with current fees replace the proposed rule's FY 2024/2025 projections for purposes of the final fee analysis and are used, together with the updated cost projections, to assess whether the final EB-5 fee schedule is sufficient to recover projected program costs.
4. 成本与收入差额的变化
4. Changes to the Cost and Revenue Differential
在拟议规则中,国土安全部(DHS)说明,EB-5费用研究将预计的EB-5项目成本与假设费用不变情况下现行费用表产生的预计收入进行了比较,以确定现行费用是否足以全额收回提供EB-5审理服务的成本。如果预计收入低于预计成本,DHS通常会提议设立新费用或提高费用以弥补缺口;如果预计收入超过成本,DHS则可选择削减某些成本或服务,或动用储备金。拟议规则在表8中将这一关系概括为EB-5成本收入差额。
In the proposed rule, DHS explained that the EB-5 fee study compared projected EB-5 program costs to projected revenue under the current fee schedule, assuming no fee changes, to determine whether existing fees were sufficient to recover the full cost of providing EB-5 adjudication services. If projected revenue was less than projected costs, DHS would generally propose new or increased fees to address the shortfall; if projected revenue exceeded costs, DHS could instead reduce certain costs or services or draw down reserves. The proposed rule summarized this relationship in Table 8 as the EB-5 cost-revenue differential.
在本最终规则中,USCIS已按前述各小节所述,对EB-5项目2026/2027财年的成本预测和收入预测均进行了修订。利用这些更新后的预测数据,EB-5费用研究确定预计成本为1.050亿美元,现行费用下的预计收入为5,660万美元,由此产生4,840万美元的成本收入缺口。本规则中的最终EB-5费用表旨在弥合该缺口,并使EB-5收入与预计项目成本保持一致,此举符合《移民与国籍法》(INA)第286(m)条、《美国法典》第8编第1356(m)条的规定,并符合《EB-5改革法》的要求。修订后的成本收入差额汇总于本序言表7中。
For this final rule, USCIS has revised both the cost and revenue projections for the EB-5 program for FY 2026/2027, as described in the preceding subsections. Using these updated projections, the EB-5 fee study identifies an anticipated cost of $105.0 million and projected revenue of $56.6 million under current fees, resulting in a cost-revenue gap of $48.4 million. The final EB-5 fee schedule in this rule is designed to close that gap and align EB-5 revenues with projected program costs, consistent with INA section 286(m), 8 U.S.C. 1356(m), and as required by the EB-5 Reform Act. The revised cost-revenue differential is summarized in Table 7 of this preamble.
(印刷页第61951页)
( printed page 61951)
5. 区域中心终止成本的修订
5. Revisions to Regional Center Termination Costs
在拟议规则中,DHS说明,《移民与国籍法》第203(b)(5)(J)条、《美国法典》第8编第1153(b)(5)(J)条要求DHS终止未缴纳EB-5诚信基金费用的区域中心,DHS还可因其他不合规行为终止区域中心资格。 参见,例如, 《移民与国籍法》第203(b)(5)(E)(vii)(III)条、《美国法典》第8编第1153(b)(5)(E)(vii)(III)条。终止资格是维护EB-5项目诚信的重要组成部分。USCIS为开展此类终止工作需承担相应成本,历来通过EB-5申请费用(此前为I-924表格,近期则为I-956和I-956F表格)为这些成本提供资金。
In the proposed rule, DHS explained that INA section 203(b)(5)(J), 8 U.S.C. 1153(b)(5)(J), requires DHS to terminate a regional center that does not pay the EB-5 Integrity Fund fee, and that DHS may also terminate a regional center for other forms of noncompliance. See, e.g., INA 203(b)(5)(E)(vii)(III), 8 U.S.C. 1153(b)(5)(E)(vii)(III). Terminations are an integral component of maintaining EB-5 program integrity. USCIS incurs costs to conduct these terminations and historically has funded those costs through EB-5 request fees (formerly a Form I-924, and more recently Forms I-956 and I-956F).
在拟议规则中,USCIS在EB-5费用研究中采用与其他投资人项目办公室(IPO)工作量相同的基于活动的成本核算(ABC)方法,对区域中心终止成本进行了单独估算。USCIS估计每次终止的完成时长为108小时,年均总成本约为680万美元。 参见88 FR 402, 509(2023年1月4日)。拟议规则进一步说明,在终止时点单独收取费用并不现实,特别是在终止理由可能是未缴纳包括诚信基金费用在内的应缴费用的情况下。 参见 《移民与国籍法》第203(b)(5)(J)(iv)(II)条、《美国法典》第8编第1153(b)(5)(J)(iv)(II)条。因此,DHS提议继续通过I-956和I-956F表格的费用来收回终止成本。 (印刷页第61952页)
In the proposed rule, USCIS estimated regional center termination costs separately in the EB-5 fee study, using the same ABC methodology applied to other IPO workloads. USCIS estimated a completion rate of 108 hours per termination and an average annual total cost of approximately $6.8 million. See88 FR 402, 509 (Jan. 4, 2023). The proposed rule further explained that it would not be practical to charge a separate fee at the point of termination, particularly where the basis for termination may be failure to pay required fees, including the Integrity Fund fee. See INA 203(b)(5)(J)(iv)(II), 8 U.S.C. 1153(b)(5)(J)(iv)(II). DHS, therefore, proposed to continue recovering termination costs through the fees for Forms I-956 and I-956F. ( printed page 61952)
在本最终规则中,美国公民及移民服务局(USCIS)更新了区域中心终止的预计成本,并澄清了相关假设。在本最终规则中,国土安全部(DHS)说明,称为“区域中心终止”的工作量不仅包括终止,还包括那些在终止前解决问题的区域中心的重新确认。例如,它包括某区域中心收到终止意向通知后作出充分回应从而解决导致该通知的问题所产生的估计成本。USCIS根据自拟议规则发布以来获得的更新的项目数据和经验,修订了区域中心终止和重新确认的数量。USCIS仍估计每件的完成率为108小时。采用相同的ABC方法并结合更新后的终止数量假设,USCIS现估计区域中心终止的年平均成本约为480万美元。这些修订后的成本估算已纳入最终费用计算中。
In this final rule, USCIS updates the projected cost of regional center terminations and clarifies the assumptions for it. In this final rule, DHS explains that the workload called Regional Center Terminations includes more than just terminations. It also includes reaffirmations for regional centers that resolve issues before their termination. For example, it includes the estimated cost of a regional center that received a Notice of Intent to Terminate and responds sufficiently to resolve the issues, which led to the notice. USCIS revises the number of regional center terminations and reaffirmations based on more recent program data and experience since publication of the proposed rule. USCIS still estimates the completion rate as 108 hours each. Using the same ABC methodology and updated termination volume assumptions, USCIS now estimates an average annual cost of approximately $4.8 million for regional center terminations. These revised cost estimates are incorporated into the final fee calculations.
针对公众意见,国土安全部完善了这些成本的分摊方式。具体而言,国土安全部不再将区域中心终止的成本分摊至表格I-956修正案,但继续将该成本分摊至表格I-956初次申请和表格I-956F。有评论者表示担忧,认为表格I-956修正案,特别是那些反映程序性或行政性变更的修正案,不应承担终止活动的成本。国土安全部认同,仅将终止成本分摊至表格I-956初次申请更能使这些成本与相关项目风险相符,并避免给表格I-956修正案申请带来过重负担。
In response to public comments, DHS has refined how these costs are allocated. Specifically, DHS no longer applies the cost of regional center terminations to Form I-956 amendments but continues to apply the cost to initial Form I-956 filings and Form I-956F. Commenters expressed concern that Form I-956 amendments—particularly those reflecting ministerial or administrative changes—should not bear the cost of termination activities. DHS agrees that allocating termination costs solely to initial Form I-956 applications more appropriately align those costs with the underlying program risk and avoids overburdening Form I-956 amendment filings.
如拟议规则中所述,依据移民与国籍法(INA)第203(b)(5)(J)(iii)条,即8 U.S.C. 1153(b)(5)(J)(iii)条设立的EB-5诚信基金,明确指定用于合规性、欺诈调查、审计和实地访查,并未明确提供单独的资金来源用于典型的裁决活动,例如终止。因此,国土安全部继续其长期以来的做法,即通过EB-5申请费为区域中心终止成本提供资金,本最终规则反映了针对这些活动更新后的成本、数量和分摊假设。
As discussed in the proposed rule, the EB-5 Integrity Fund, established at INA section 203(b)(5)(J)(iii), 8 U.S.C. 1153(b)(5)(J)(iii), is expressly designated for compliance, fraud investigation, audits, and site visits, and does not explicitly provide a separate revenue stream for typical adjudicative activities, such as terminations. DHS, therefore, continues its longstanding practice of funding regional center termination costs through EB-5 request fees, and this final rule reflects updated cost, volume, and allocation assumptions for those activities.
D. 修订后的EB-5技术费
D. Revised EB-5 Technology Fee
EB-5改革法案授权USCIS收取不超过某些费用百分之一的技术费。参见《美国公法》第117-103号,BB分部,第106(c)条。国土安全部可使用该费用收入改进用于处理、裁决和存档申请及请愿书的信息技术系统。同上。在拟议规则中,国土安全部为表格I-526和I-526E设定了95美元的EB-5技术费。参见90联邦公报48516、48530页。拟议费用为扣除EB-5技术费之前费用的百分之一,并向下舍入至最接近的5美元增量。同上。
The EB-5 Reform Act authorized USCIS to charge a technology fee not greater than one percent of some fees. SeePublic Law 117-103, div. BB, sec. 106(c). DHS may use the revenue from this fee to make improvements to the information technology systems which process, adjudicate, and archive applications and petitions. Id. In the proposed rule, DHS included a $95 EB-5 technology fee for Forms I-526 and I-526E. See90 FR 48516, 48530. The proposed fee was 1 percent of the fee before including the EB-5 technology fee, rounded down to the nearest $5 increment. Id.
在本最终规则中,国土安全部根据使用2026财年和2027财年估算数据进行的修订版EB-5费用研究结果,重新计算了EB-5技术费。采用与拟议规则相同的方法,EB-5技术费为75美元。本序言前文表1中的费用在适用时已包含该75美元。在拟议规则中,国土安全部未明确EB-5技术费不适用的情形,但在序言中列出了表格I-526E修正案的降低费用。参见90联邦公报48516、48517页;另见拟议的8 CFR 106.2(d)(2)条。在本最终规则中,国土安全部在EB-5费用相关法规中澄清了EB-5技术费的适用情形。参见新8 CFR 106.2(d)(2)(iii)条。
In this final rule, DHS recalculates the EB-5 technology fee based on the results of the revised EB-5 fee study using estimates for FY 2026 and 2027. Using the same methodology as the proposed rule, the EB-5 technology fee is $75. The fees in Table 1 earlier in the preamble include the $75 when it applies. In the proposed rule, DHS did not clarify when the EB-5 technology fee did not apply but listed a reduced fee for Form I-526E amendments in the preamble. See90 FR 48516, 48517; see also proposed 8 CFR 106.2(d)(2). In this final rule, DHS clarifies when the EB-5 technology fee applies in the regulations for EB-5 fees. See new8 CFR 106.2(d)(2)(iii).
E. EB-5诚信基金费用通货膨胀调整的变更
E. Change to Inflation Adjustment to EB-5 Integrity Fund Fees
国土安全部(DHS)提议自2022年3月15日《EB-5改革法案》颁布以来,按通货膨胀率提高EB-5诚信基金费用。 见联邦公报90 FR 48516, 48531-48532。《EB-5改革法案》授权国土安全部根据需要调整诚信基金费用,以确保基金内资金足以用于执行该基金的允许用途。 见《移民与国籍法》(INA)第203(b)(5)(J)(ii)(III)条,8 U.S.C. 1153(b)(5)(J)(ii)(III);另见8 U.S.C. 1153(b)(5)(J)(iii)。当时,国土安全部使用了2022年上半年至2024年上半年的城市消费者消费价格指数(CPI-U)。 见90 FR 48516, 48531-48532。然而,国土安全部表示,其可能会在最终规则中根据更新的信息修订相关金额。 同上。根据更近期的通货膨胀信息调整EB-5诚信基金费用,将使美国公民及移民服务局(USCIS)能够更多地收回其在维护EB-5项目诚信方面产生的运营成本,并有助于维持USCIS未来年度的相关工作。使用年度平均值还可消除通货膨胀调整中的季节性因素。
DHS proposed to increase EB-5 Integrity Fund fees by the rate of inflation since enactment of the EB-5 Reform Act on March 15, 2022. See90 FR 48516, 48531-48532. The EB-5 Reform Act authorized DHS to adjust the Integrity Fund fees as necessary to ensure that amounts in the Fund are sufficient to carry out the permissible uses of the fund. See INA sec. 203(b)(5)(J)(ii)(III), 8 U.S.C. 1153(b)(5)(J)(ii)(III); see also8 U.S.C. 1153(b)(5)(J)(iii). At the time, DHS used the CPI-U from the first half of 2022 to the first half of 2024. See90 FR 48516, 48531-48532. However, DHS indicated that it may revise the amounts based on more recent information in the final rule. Id. Adjusting the EB-5 Integrity Fund fees to account for more recent inflation information will allow USCIS to recover more of its operating costs associated with maintaining the integrity of the EB-5 program and help sustain USCIS efforts in future years. Using annual averages also removes seasonality from the inflation adjustment.
在本最终规则中,国土安全部使用2022年至2025年的CPI-U信息设定EB-5诚信基金费用。2022年的年度平均通货膨胀指数为292.655。[22] 2025年的年度平均值为321.943。 同上。因此,CPI-U从2022年到2025年增长了10.01%。[23] 将此适用于现行费用,I-526E表格的EB-5诚信基金费用1,000美元将增至1,100美元;10,000美元的区域中心诚信基金费用将增至11,000美元;20,000美元的区域中心诚信基金费用将增至22,000美元。[24]
In this final rule, DHS sets the EB-5 Integrity Fund Fees using CPI-U information from 2022 to 2025. The annual average inflation for 2022 was 292.655.[22] The annual average for 2025 was 321.943. Id. Therefore, the CPI-U increased by 10.01 percent from 2022 to 2025.[23] Applying this to the current fees, the Form I-526E EB-5 Integrity Fund Fee of $1,000 will increase to $1,100; the $10,000 Regional Center Integrity Fund Fee will increase to $11,000; and the $20,000 Regional Center Integrity Fund Fee will increase to $22,000.[24]
国土安全部在确定本最终规则的通货膨胀调整之前,考虑了不同的日期范围及由此产生的CPI-U百分比变化。例如,国土安全部曾考虑最终确定拟议规则中提出的8.25%的通货膨胀调整。[25] 然而,这种做法将忽略自拟议通货膨胀调整计算以来已发生的一年多的通货膨胀。尽管拟议规则于2025年10月发布,但该通货膨胀调整仅使用了2022年上半年至2024年上半年的数据。也就是说,最新的通货膨胀数据截至2024年6月。另一种替代方法是继续以2022年上半年为起点,仅将终点更新为2025年上半年。然而,这种做法将导致最终费用增加11%。[26] 因此,本最终规则中采用的10%增幅可被视为一种中等范围的通货膨胀调整,因为它低于2022年和2025年半年期间的通货膨胀率,但高于使用2022年至2024年半年度数据计算的拟议通货膨胀调整。
DHS considered different date ranges and the resulting percentage change in CPI-U before determining the inflation adjustment for this final rule. For example, DHS considered finalizing the proposed inflation adjustment of 8.25 percent.[25] However, this approach would omit over a year of inflation that has occurred since the calculation of the proposed inflation adjusted. While the proposed rule was published in October 2025, the inflation adjustment only used data from first half of 2022 to the first half of 2024. Meaning, the latest inflation data was as of June 2024. Yet another alternative approach would be to continue using a starting point of the first half of 2022 and only update the endpoint to the first half of 2025. However, such an approach would result in an 11 percent increase in final fees.[26] As such, the 10 percent increase used in this final rule may be considered a midrange inflation adjustment because it is less than the inflation semiannual periods in 2022 and 2025, but it is more than proposed inflation adjustment using semiannual data from 2022 to 2024.
诚信基金收入逐年有所变化,这影响了USCIS对EB-5诚信活动的规划工作。 见90 FR 48516。对于2026/2027财年期间,USCIS估计,按现行费用水平计算,诚信基金的年收款总额约为1,390万美元。本规则将诚信基金费用提高10%,预计将使年收入增加至约1,530万美元,并有助于维持USCIS在支持EB-5项目诚信的人员配置和信息技术方面投资的购买力。 (印刷页第61953页)
Integrity Fund revenue has varied from year to year, which affects USCIS planning for EB-5 integrity activities. See90 FR 48516. For the FY 2026/2027 period, USCIS estimates that Integrity Fund collections under the current fee levels will total approximately $13.9 million annually. Increasing the Integrity Fund fees by 10 percent in this rule is projected to increase annual revenue to approximately $15.3 million and help maintain the purchasing power of USCIS investments in staffing and information technology that support EB-5 program integrity. ( printed page 61953)
四、对拟议规则的公众意见回应
IV. Response to Public Comments on the Proposed Rule
A. 对拟议规则意见的概述
A. Summary of Comments on the Proposed Rule
DHS在拟议规则发布后设定了60天的评论期。DHS在USCIS-2025-0139号档案中就拟议规则收到了28份公众评论意见。在这28份意见中,22份为独立意见,其余意见为表格信函副本、与本规则无关,或所含评论和请求完全超出本规则的范围。部分意见为匿名提交,其余则来自个人、倡导团体、律师或律师事务所以及企业。部分评论人士表示支持拟议规则,或支持拟议规则的一项或多项具体条款,但未提出修改建议。部分评论人士反对该规则,并对一项或多项条款表示反对,但未提出修改建议。许多评论人士对拟议规则的各项条款既表示支持又表示反对,提出了附带修改建议的总体支持意见,提出了附带修改建议的总体反对意见,或者未明确表明其意见是支持还是反对拟议规则。DHS审查了针对拟议规则收到的所有公众评论意见,并按主题领域在本最终规则中回应了相关评论。DHS还收到了若干与拟议费用无关、超出拟议规则范围的评论意见。DHS未对这些意见逐一作出回应,但已对超出范围的评论进行了归纳总结,并在本序言第IV.H节中提供了总体回应。
DHS provided a 60-day comment period following publication of the proposed rule. DHS received 28 public comment submissions in docket USCIS-2025-0139 in response to the proposed rule. Of the 28 submissions, 22 were unique submissions and the remainder of the comments were form letter copies, not germane to the rule, or contained comments and requests that were entirely outside of the scope of the rule. Several submissions were anonymous, while the remaining were from individuals, advocacy groups, lawyers or law firms, and businesses. Some commenters expressed support for the proposed rule or supported one or more specific provisions of the proposed rule without recommending changes. Some commenters opposed the rule and expressed opposition to one or more provisions without recommending changes. Many commenters provided mixed comments of both support for and opposition to various provisions of the proposed rule, provided general support with suggested revisions, provided general opposition with suggested revisions, or were unclear on whether the comment supported or opposed the proposed rule. DHS reviewed all the public comments received in response to the proposed rule and addressed relevant comments in this final rule, grouped by subject area. DHS also received several comments on subjects unrelated to the proposed fees that are outside of the proposed rule's scope. DHS has not individually responded to these comments but has summarized out of scope comments and provided a general response in Section IV.H of this preamble.
B. 对本规则的总体意见反馈
B. General Feedback on the Rule
1. 对本规则的支持意见
1. Support for the Rule
评论意见: 多位评论人士对拟议规则表示总体支持。部分评论人士表示支持该规则,理由是费用调整将产生以下作用:
Comment: Several commenters expressed general support for the proposed rule. Some commenters expressed support for the rule reasoning that the fee adjustments would do the following:
激励寻求在美国投资的移民参与该项目。
Incentivize participation in the program by immigrants seeking to invest in the U.S.
由于彻底的背景调查,使资金来源得以合法化。
Allow the source of money to be legitimized, due to thorough background checks.
表明移民投资者是建设美国未来的合作伙伴。
Show that immigrant investors are partners in building America's future.
回应: DHS感谢评论人士对拟议规则的支持,并认可其就费用调整带来的积极影响所提供的观点。DHS同意,适当校准的费用是确保EB-5项目对包括小型企业在内的多元化投资者继续保持可行性和可及性的重要因素。DHS认识到,平衡的费用结构有助于防止小型实体被排除在参与之外,从而支持促进就业创造和投资的更广泛经济发展目标。
Response: DHS appreciates the commenters' support for the proposed rule and acknowledges the perspectives provided regarding the positive impacts of the fee adjustments. DHS agrees that appropriately calibrated fees are an important element in ensuring the continued viability and accessibility of the EB-5 program for a diverse range of investors, including small enterprises. DHS recognizes that a balanced fee structure can help prevent smaller entities from being excluded from participation, thereby supporting broader economic development objectives that foster job creation and investment.
DHS还指出,激励移民投资者参与符合国会刺激美国就业创造和资本投资的意图。EB-5项目旨在吸引致力于为美国经济作出贡献的个人,[27] 费用调整旨在为美国公民及移民服务局(USCIS)提供高效有效管理该项目所需的资源,包括强有力的审查和背景调查。这些措施有助于确保投资来源的合法性,并维护该项目的完整性。
DHS also notes that incentivizing participation by immigrant investors aligns with Congressional intent to stimulate job creation and capital investment in the United States. The EB-5 program is designed to attract individuals who are committed to contributing to the U.S. economy,[27] and the fee adjustments are intended to provide USCIS with the resources necessary to efficiently and effectively administer the program, including robust vetting and background checks. These measures help ensure the legitimacy of investment sources and maintain the integrity of the program.
此外,DHS认识到投资移民有助于美国的经济增长和就业创造。DHS将继续致力于按照法定要求管理EB-5项目,并通过清晰有效的监管程序维护项目的完整性。
Furthermore, DHS acknowledges that investment immigration contributes to economic growth and job creation in the United States. DHS remains focused on administering the EB-5 program in accordance with statutory requirements and maintaining program integrity through clear and effective regulatory processes.
在认真考虑这些支持性意见后,DHS未根据这些意见对最终规则作出修改,因为这些评论肯定了拟议规则的方向和目标。
After careful consideration of these supportive comments, DHS did not make changes to the final rule based on these remarks, as the comments affirm the direction and objectives of the rule as proposed.
2. 对本规则的反对意见
2. Opposition to the Rule
许多评论人士对拟议费用、费用调整的幅度或拟议规则中的具体政策变化表示总体反对。DHS在以下各节中对这些公众评论意见进行了归纳并作出回应:
Many commenters stated their general opposition to the proposed fees, the magnitude of the fee adjustments, or specific policy changes in the proposed rule. DHS summarizes and responds to these public comments in the following sections:
a. 对申请人的负面影响
a. Negative Impacts on Applicants
评论意见: 部分评论人士表示,新费用将对小型投资者造成不成比例的影响,USCIS应考虑采用分级或过渡性费用结构,以避免对项目参与造成意外障碍。一位评论人士表示,拟议费用可能使小型投资者无力负担,并降低大型投资者的积极性。
Comment: Some commenters stated the new fees would disproportionately affect smaller investors and USCIS should consider a scaled or transitional fee structure to avoid unintended barriers to program participation. A commenter stated the proposed fees may prevent smaller investors from being able to afford the fees and reduce the motivation of larger investors.
回应: 本收费规则并非旨在减少或限制小型或大型投资者。这些费用调整体现了美国国土安全部(DHS)在便利获取、经济可负担性、公平性以及维护国家利益之间所做的最大努力平衡,同时为美国公民及移民服务局(USCIS)提供维持充分服务所需的资金。USCIS未获得国会为EB-5项目拨付的任何款项,申请费对于提供履行与此类申请相关工作所需的资源是必要的。当费用无法完全弥补成本时,USCIS将无法维持足够的处理能力,以在《EB-5改革法》所述的处理时限内处理申请。
Response: This fee rule is not intended to reduce or limit small or large investors. These fee adjustments reflect DHS's best effort to balance access, affordability, equity, and benefits to the national interest while providing USCIS with the funding necessary to maintain adequate services. USCIS receives no Congressional appropriation for the EB-5 program, and filing fees are necessary to provide the resources required to perform the work associated with such filings. When fees do not fully recover costs, USCIS cannot maintain sufficient capacity to process requests within the processing times referenced in the EB-5 Reform Act.
美国国土安全部承认评论人对费用上涨可能对较小投资者产生影响的担忧,以及实施分级或过渡性收费结构的建议。美国国土安全部认真权衡了项目可及性与法律要求的全面收回EB-5项目管理成本之需求之间的平衡。本规则中的费用调整基于一项全面的费用研究,旨在确保USCIS拥有足够的资源,以维持对EB-5利益申请的及时有效处理,支持项目诚信,并履行法定职责。
DHS acknowledges commenters' concerns regarding the potential impact of increased fees on smaller investors and the suggestion to implement a scaled or transitional fee structure. DHS carefully considered the balance between program accessibility and the need to recover the full costs of administering the EB-5 program, as required by statute. The fee adjustments in this rule are based on a comprehensive fee study and are designed to ensure that USCIS has sufficient resources to maintain timely and effective processing of EB-5 benefit requests, support program integrity, and fulfill statutory mandates.
尽管美国国土安全部认识到费用上涨可能给部分申请人,特别是较小投资者带来挑战,但该部门必须确保费用反映裁决、防欺诈及合规活动的实际成本。若将费用设定在低于成本回收水平,可能会损害USCIS提供充分服务及维护项目诚信的能力。美国国土安全部考虑了包括分级或过渡性收费结构在内的多种方案,但最终认定统一收费表对于公平分摊成本及避免行政复杂性是必要的。
While DHS recognizes that higher fees may present challenges for some applicants, particularly smaller investors, the Department must ensure that fees reflect the actual costs of adjudication, fraud prevention, and compliance activities. Setting fees below cost recovery levels could compromise USCIS' ability to provide adequate services and maintain program integrity. DHS considered alternatives, including tiered or transitional fee structures, but determined that a uniform fee schedule is necessary to equitably distribute costs and avoid administrative complexity.
b. 对美国经济、雇主或劳工的负面影响
b. Negative Impacts on U.S. Economy, Employers, or Workers
评论: 其他评论人指出,新收费将:
Comment: Other commenters stated the new fees would:
对通过EB-5创造的就业机会造成不利影响。
Create detrimental impacts to the job opportunities created through EB-5.
危及投资者的移民身份,扰乱数百万美元的项目,并损害地方经济。
Jeopardize investor immigration status, disrupt multi-million-dollar projects, and damage local economies.
回应: 美国国土安全部承认,最终确定的费用可能对小实体产生较大影响,但并无证据表明这些变化将显著影响就业机会、投资或项目,或损害地方经济。此外, (印刷页 61954) 评论人并未提供任何研究或实证数据来支持其关于费用将产生此类影响的主张。EB-5投资的节奏和重点取决于众多经济、金融及国际商业因素,并非主要由表格相关费用驱动。最终确定的费用相对于大多数投资金额而言极小,尽管美国国土安全部认识到部分投资者和项目可能受到影响,但该部门认为此类影响的规模并不足以构成不最终确定该项依法要求的监管行动的理由。
Response: DHS acknowledges that the fees being finalized may generate a larger impact to small entities but does not have evidence that the changes will significantly impact job opportunities, investments, or projects, or damage local economies. Further, the ( printed page 61954) commenters did not provide any studies or empirical data to support their assertion that the fees would have such impacts. The pace and focus of EB-5 investments rely on numerous economic, financial, and international business factors, and are not primarily driven by form related fees. The fees being finalized are very small relative to most investment amounts and while DHS recognizes that some investors and projects may be impacted, the Department does not believe the scale of any such effects would warrant not finalizing the regulatory action, which is required by law.
美国国土安全部将继续致力于监测费用变化对项目参与情况的影响,并将持续评估未来是否需要进行调整或采取替代方案。目前,该收费表体现了美国国土安全部在符合法定要求的前提下,平衡获取渠道、经济可负担性及运营需求所做的最大努力。
DHS remains committed to monitoring the impact of fee changes on program participation and will continue to evaluate whether future adjustments or alternative approaches may be warranted. Currently, the fee schedule reflects DHS's best effort to balance access, affordability, and operational needs, consistent with statutory requirements.
c. 对机构运营的负面影响
c. Negative Impact on Agency Operation
评论: 一位评论人指出,没有法律或政策依据支持为该群体降低费用。该评论人表示,拟议规则并未讨论这些费用将对USCIS整体财政状况产生的影响。该评论人表示,拟议规则未讨论为何EB-5费用未能弥补USCIS免费开展的部分工作(例如人道主义裁决)的成本。
Comment: One commenter stated that there are no legal or policy justifications for reducing fees for this population. The commenter stated that the proposed rule did not discuss the impact that these fees would have on USCIS' overall fiscal picture. The commenter stated that there was no discussion on why EB-5 fees do not recover a portion of the work that USCIS does for free, such as humanitarian adjudications.
回应: 美国国土安全部承认评论人的担忧,即较低的EB-5费用可能对USCIS的资金产生负面影响,且拟议规则未充分讨论USCIS的整体财政前景。然而,美国国土安全部不认同该规则未讨论拟议EB-5费用的法律依据,以及未讨论为何这些费用未能弥补USCIS免费开展的部分工作成本这一说法。
Response: DHS acknowledges the commenter's concern that lower EB-5 fees could negatively affect USCIS funding and that the proposed rule did not sufficiently address USCIS' overall fiscal outlook. However, DHS disagrees that the rule did not discuss the legal justification for proposed EB-5 fees and why they did not recover the cost of a portion of the work that USCIS does for free.
国土安全部(DHS)根据《移民与国籍法》(INA)第286(m)条、8 U.S.C. 1356(m)以及适用的收费指南(包括行政管理和预算局(OMB)第A-25号通知)设定费用,以在切实可行的范围内收回提供裁决和入籍服务的全部成本。本规则中的EB-5费用基于一项针对该项目的专项收费研究,该研究纳入了预计的EB-5工作量、直接和间接成本以及《EB-5改革法》的法定框架。 参见《公法》117-103号,分部BB,第106条;90 FR 48516,48522-48530页。如拟议规则中所述,《EB-5改革法》授权国土安全部在EB-5项目费用中增加一定金额。 参见90 FR 48516,48525-48526页。然而,《EB-5改革法》中授权在EB-5费用中增加金额以支付免费服务费用的条款,与国土安全部历来设定美国公民及移民服务局(USCIS)费用的方式不一致。 参见同上。因此,出于审慎考虑、为降低诉讼风险,并且由于未转移的成本已由其他费用充分提供资金,国土安全部选择拟议不收回这些成本的费用。
DHS sets fees under INA section 286(m), 8 U.S.C. 1356(m), and applicable fee guidance (including OMB Circular A-25) to recover, to the extent practicable, the full costs of providing adjudication and naturalization services. The EB-5 fees in this rule are based on a program-specific fee study that incorporates projected EB-5 workloads, direct and indirect costs, and the statutory framework of the EB-5 Reform Act. SeePublic Law 117-103, div. BB, sec. 106; 90 FR 48516, 48522-48530. As discussed in the proposed rule, the EB-5 Reform Act authorizes DHS to add an amount to EB-5 program fees. See90 FR 48516, 48525-48526. However, the EB-5 Reform Act provision that authorized adding an amount to EB-5 fees to cover free services is inconsistent with how DHS has historically set USCIS fees. See id. Therefore, DHS chose to propose fees that do not recover those costs out of an abundance of caution, to reduce litigation risk, and because the costs not transferred are being funded adequately by other fees.
尽管部分EB-5费用低于2022/2023财年收费规则中的费用,但国土安全部并非随意削减费用。此次调整反映了更新后的成本和数量数据、EB-5的具体法定要求,以及本序言中讨论的诉讼情况。本规则设定的费用表明,USCIS能够继续支持EB-5运营和诚信保障活动。来自本规则制定范围之外其他工作量的收入,将为移民审查费账户(IEFA)提供所需的剩余资金。
Although some EB-5 fees are lower than those in the FY 2022/2023 fee rule, DHS is not reducing fees arbitrarily. The adjustments reflect updated cost and volume data, the specific EB-5 statutory requirements, and the litigation discussed in this preamble. The fees set in this rule demonstrate that USCIS can continue to support EB-5 operations and integrity activities. Revenue from other workloads beyond the scope of this rulemaking will supply the remaining funding necessary for the IEFA account.
国土安全部还指出,根据INA第203(b)(5)(J)条、8 U.S.C. 1153(b)(5)(J)设立的EB-5诚信基金,为某些欺诈侦测与合规活动提供了专项资金来源,从而缓解了对IEFA收入的压力。因此,国土安全部不同意有关这些费用调整缺乏法律或政策依据、或者会危及USCIS整体财政状况的说法。如拟议规则中所述,其他收入来源,例如新设立的庇护申请费用,可能会抵消USCIS传统上未曾收费的部分工作成本。 参见90 FR 48516,48526页。USCIS可以通过推进单独的规则制定、将成本转移至不同的收费账户,或削减IEFA非加急处理预算,来弥补IEFA成本与IEFA收入在其他项目或工作量方面的差额。例如,如2022/2023财年收费规则中所述,USCIS可将成本转移至加急处理账户。[28] 国土安全部最近依据法律授权按通胀率上调了加急处理费用。[29]
DHS also notes that the EB-5 Integrity Fund, authorized at INA section 203(b)(5)(J), 8 U.S.C. 1153(b)(5)(J), provides a dedicated source of funding for certain fraud detection and compliance activities, which mitigates pressure on IEFA revenues. Accordingly, DHS disagrees that there is no legal or policy basis for these fee adjustments or that they jeopardize USCIS' overall fiscal position. As discussed in the proposed rule, other revenue sources, such as new fees for asylum applications, may offset some of the cost of work, which USCIS has not traditionally charged a fee. See90 FR 48516, 48526. USCIS may offset the difference between IEFA cost and IEFA revenue for other programs or workloads by pursuing separate rulemakings, shifting costs to different fee accounts, or reducing the IEFA non-premium budget. For example, USCIS could shift costs to the premium processing account, as discussed in the FY 2022/2023 fee rule.[28] DHS recently increased the premium processing fees by inflation, as authorized by statute.[29]
C. 背景与法律依据
C. Background and Legal Authority
评论意见: 多位评论者提及应将处理时间目标纳入法规予以明确规定。他们指出,这将清楚表明机构致力于及时处理案件,并体现出源自授权法规的具体、可衡量的时间目标。
Comment: Several commenters mentioned codifying processing time goals in regulation. They stated that it would clearly articulate the agency's commitment to timely processing, and to specific and measurable time goals drawn from the authorizing statute.
答复: 国土安全部对有关将处理时间目标纳入法规的评论表示认可。《EB-5改革法》要求国土安全部在设定费用时,以实现EB-5福利申请特定平均处理时间为目标。本最终规则将这些法定处理时间目标纳入收费设定方法,并在序言中加以说明。
Response: DHS acknowledges the comment regarding the codification of processing time goals in regulation. The EB-5 Reform Act mandates that DHS set fees with the objective of achieving specific average processing times for EB-5 benefit requests. This final rule incorporates those statutory processing time goals as part of the fee-setting methodology and explains them in the preamble.
国土安全部致力于在裁决时限方面保持透明度和问责制,并将继续监测针对这些法定目标的执行情况。尽管国土安全部的法规中包含一些不具约束力的条款,但更好的做法是将法规条文限于各项要求,而不在《联邦法规汇编》中规定目标、指标或宗旨。因此,在规则及配套的监管分析中提及法定时限,而非将其写入法规正文,才是最恰当的做法。因此,国土安全部不会基于这些评论对本最终规则作出修改。国土安全部将继续评估项目绩效,并根据需要考虑进一步的监管或业务调整,以支持及时裁决。
DHS is committed to transparency and accountability in adjudication timelines and will continue to monitor performance against these statutory goals. While DHS regulations contain some non-binding provisions, the better practice is to limit codification to requirements and not promulgate goals, targets or objectives in the Code of Federal Regulations. Therefore, referencing the statutory timeframes in the rule and supporting regulatory analysis is the most appropriate approach, rather than in regulatory text. Thus, DHS makes no changes in this final rule based on these comments. DHS will continue to evaluate program performance and consider further regulatory or operational changes as needed to support timely adjudication.
评论意见: 国土安全部收到一条评论意见,要求USCIS出台全面的法规以落实《EB-5改革法》,包括对维持期、投资者保护以及《EB-5改革法》要求制定法规的其他方面予以明确。
Comment: DHS received a comment requesting that USCIS provide comprehensive regulations implementing the EB-5 Reform Act including clarification of the sustainment period, investor protections, and other aspects of the EB-5 Reform Act requiring regulations.
response: 如前所述，国土安全部（DHS）正在制定一项相关的规则制定，该规则将提议修改EB-5项目法规，如果最终确定，将全面落实《EB-5改革法案》所作的法定改革。 参见本序言第II.D.1节。该规则仍在制定中。因此，国土安全部不会根据此评论对本规则作出任何修改，因为国土安全部可能会在另一项规则制定中解决评论者的关切。在未来的规则制定中，国土安全部可能会根据法定和法规要求重新考虑EB-5相关费用。
Response: As noted earlier, DHS is working on a related rulemaking, which would propose amendments to EB-5 program regulations, that, if finalized, would fully implement the statutory reforms made by the EB-5 Reform Act. See section II.D.1 of this preamble. That rule is still in development. As such, DHS makes no changes to this rule based on this comment because DHS may address the commenter's concerns in another rulemaking. In future rulemaking, DHS may reconsider EB-5 fees based on statutory and regulatory requirements.
D. 费用设定方法
D. Fee Setting Approach
1. 处理时间目标
1. Processing Time Goals
评论： 国土安全部收到了许多与处理时间有关的评论，可概括如下：
Comment: DHS received many comments related to processing times, which can be summarized as follows:
平均处理时间目标需要改进。
The average processing time goals need to be improved.
处理时间目标应成为新费用的驱动因素。
The processing time goals should be the driving force behind the new fees.
如果费用增加伴随着更短的处理时间，区域中心和投资者将对此表示支持。
Regional Centers and investors would be supportive of fee increases if they were accompanied by shorter processing times.
在最终规则中，美国公民及移民服务局（USCIS）应建立审理处理目标，以促进处理过程中的可预测性和问责制。采用EB-5相关申请的审理时间框架将减少利益相关方的不确定性，并增强项目的完整性。 （印刷页第61955页）
In the final rule, USCIS should establish adjudication processing goals to promote predictability and accountability in processing. Adopting adjudication timeframes for EB-5- ( printed page 61955) related filings would reduce uncertainty for stakeholders and enhance program integrity.
强调根据《EB-5改革法案》第106条法定要求对EB-5审理时间框架进行基准测定的重要性。
Emphasis on the importance of benchmarking the EB-5 adjudication timeframes as statutorily required under EB-5 Reform Act section 106.
回应： 国土安全部感谢评论者就处理时间以及费用与审理速度之间关系提出的反馈意见。《EB-5改革法案》要求国土安全部设定费用的目标是实现EB-5福利申请的特定平均处理时间。在制定本规则的过程中，国土安全部考虑了这些法定时间框架，并将其纳入费用设定方法，以确保美国公民及移民服务局拥有实现处理目标所需的资源。
Response: DHS appreciates commenters' feedback regarding processing times and the relationship between fees and adjudication speed. The EB-5 Reform Act requires DHS to set fees with the goal of achieving specific average processing times for EB-5 benefit requests. In developing this rule, DHS considered these statutory timeframes and incorporated them into the fee-setting methodology to ensure USCIS has the resources necessary to meet processing goals.
国土安全部认识到及时审理对项目利益相关方的重要性，并将继续监测和报告处理时间。虽然费用调整旨在支持服务水平的提高，但实际处理时间可能受到申请量、人员配置和案件复杂程度等因素的影响。国土安全部仍致力于持续评估运营绩效，并将根据需要考虑进一步调整或流程改进，以提高审理速度和项目效率。
DHS recognizes that timely adjudication is important to program stakeholders and will continue to monitor and report on processing times. While fee adjustments are intended to support improved service levels, actual processing times may be affected by factors, such as application volume, staffing, and case complexity. DHS remains committed to ongoing evaluation of operational performance and will consider further adjustments or process improvements as needed to enhance adjudication speed and program efficiency.
国土安全部承认评论者关于为EB-5相关申请建立并对审理处理目标进行基准测定的建议。《EB-5改革法案》第106条规定了法定处理时间框架，国土安全部已将其纳入本规则的费用设定方法中。国土安全部同意明确的处理目标有助于促进可预测性、问责制和项目完整性。美国公民及移民服务局将继续监测针对这些法定基准的绩效表现。国土安全部仍致力于持续评估EB-5审理工作的运营效率和透明度，并将在可行的情况下考虑采取进一步措施，以增强利益相关方的信心并减少不确定性。但是，最终规则的法规文本未就处理时间目标作出任何修改。
DHS acknowledges commenters' recommendations to establish and benchmark adjudication processing goals for EB-5-related filings. The EB-5 Reform Act section 106 sets forth statutory processing timeframes that DHS has incorporated into the fee-setting methodology for this rule. DHS agrees that clear processing goals promote predictability, accountability, and program integrity. USCIS will continue to monitor performance against these statutory benchmarks. DHS remains committed to ongoing evaluation of operational efficiency and transparency in EB-5 adjudications and will consider further measures to enhance stakeholder confidence and reduce uncertainty where feasible. However, no changes are made to the final rule regulatory text to address processing time goals.
2. EB-5费用研究
2. EB-5 Fee Study
评论： 多位评论者要求费用设定方法具有更高的透明度。他们建议就费用如何分配特定成本（如欺诈检测、实地考察或区域中心监督）提供更清晰的说明。另一位评论者要求美国公民及移民服务局在最终确定规则时公布具体表格的完成时间和成本数据。另一位评论者表示，美国公民及移民服务局应使任何EB-5费用变更与行政管理和预算局（OMB）A-25号通告（修订版）以及《移民与国籍法》（INA）第286(m)条保持一致，包括透明的成本核算以及对费用替代方案（分阶段实施、小实体减免、费用上限或分期付款选项）的考虑。
Comment: Multiple commenters requested greater transparency in the fee-setting methodology.
