2. 经济影响
2. Economic Impacts
a. 与《拟议规则制定通知》(NPRM)相比的变化概要
a. Summary of Changes From the NPRM
在本最终规则中,国土安全部(DHS)针对成本效益分析所做的调整有两处实质性变更,这些变更与新拟议规则制定通知(NPRM)中的分析有关。第一,最终确定的费用影响是基于美国科罗拉多州地区联邦地方法院在 Moody 案判决生效后适用的EB-5费用变化计算的,该判决已在本序言中论述。第二,近期对表格数量预测进行了修订,这些更新已纳入2026财年和2027财年的预测中。国土安全部在本最终规则中还作出了另外两项调整。 (印刷页第61967页) 第一,在新拟议规则制定通知中,国土安全部按每年105份申请的小规模年度数量预测核算了I-526E表格修正案。国土安全部迄今仅收到少量此类申请,预计未来数量将非常少,可能接近于零。因此,国土安全部未在成本表中为I-562E修正案及初始表格单列项目。第二,就本规则制定而言,适用于I-829表格的时间负担不作变更。
There are two substantive changes that DHS is making in this final rule pertinent to the benefit-cost analysis as it applied to the NPRM. First, the fee impacts being finalized are based on a change from the EB-5 fees that became effective with the United States District Court for the District of Colorado decision in Moody, which is discussed in this preamble. Second, there have been recent revisions to the form volume projections, and those updates are included, under FY 2026 and FY 2027 projections. DHS is making two additional adjustments in this final rule. ( printed page 61967) First, in the NPRM, DHS accounted for an amendment Form I-526E under a small annual volume projection of 105 filings per year. DHS has received only a few such filings and expects a very small future volume, potentially close to zero. As such, DHS does not include a separate line item in the cost table for the I-562E amendment and initial form. Second, for purposes of this rulemaking the time burden applicable to the form I-829 is not set to change.
b. 货币化影响估算
b. Monetized Impact Estimates
在引言部分,国土安全部在表12中列出了从序言(表1至表3)中摘录的信息,以展示EB-5项目各表格当前及预计的费用。如表所示,我们根据各表格相对于年度总量的数量计算加权系数,以得出费用变化的加权平均值(该变化被核算为转移支付),不包括本规则新增的I-527表格。表中所示数量代表基于美国公民及移民服务局(USCIS)数量预测委员会(VPC)的年度平均预测。数量预测委员会负责协调工作量和费用预测数据、利益相关方协作、沟通以及围绕预计工作量的关键业务决策的协调工作。该机构内部小组为就预计工作量作出全局性决策提供了平台,这些决策得到美国公民及移民服务局内部知情的主题专家意见的支持,在某些情况下也得到其他政府机构数据的支持。
In introducing the analysis, DHS presents in Table 12 information captured from the preamble (Tables 1-3) to show the current and projected fees for the EB-5 program forms. As shown, we calculate weighting factor based on the volume for each form relative to the annualized total to generate a weighted average change in fees (which are accounted for as transfers), exclusive of Form I-527, which this rule is introducing. The volumes shown represent the average annual forecasts based on the USCIS Volume Projections Committee (VPC). The VPC facilitates workload and fee projection data, stakeholder collaboration, communication, and coordination of critical business decisions about projected workload. This intra-agency group provides a forum for making enterprise-wide decisions about projected workload supported by input from knowledgeable subject matter experts from within USCIS and, in some cases, data from other governmental agencies.
数量预测委员会利用历史及近期数量趋势、统计预测,以及来自美国公民及移民服务局各理事会和项目办公室(包括移民项目办公室(IPO)、美国公民及移民服务局各服务中心、国家福利中心以及各区域、地区和外勤办公室)的主题专家意见,预测美国公民及移民服务局的年度工作量。美国公民及移民服务局在2025年6月的会议期间制作了本最终规则中的大部分估算数据,以估算2026财年及以后年度的情况。美国公民及移民服务局使用数量预测委员会的估算结果来确定人员配置水平、编制未来年度预算以及估算未来收入。如果数量预测委员会未对本规则所需的某项工作量(如I-527表格)进行预测,则美国公民及移民服务局依赖主题专家的估算。
The VPC predicts USCIS annual workload volumes using historical and recent volume trends, statistical forecasts, and subject-matter expertise from various USCIS directorates and program offices, including the IPO, USCIS service centers, the National Benefits Center, and regional, district, and field offices. USCIS produced most of the estimates in this final rule during the meetings in June 2025 to estimate the FY 2026 and beyond. USCIS uses VPC estimates to determine staffing levels, budget for upcoming years, and estimate future revenue. If the VPC did not forecast a workload required for this rule, like Form I-527, then USCIS relied on SME estimates.
如表12最后几列所示,根据表3中预计的数量及最终确定的费用,费用增加2,945.9美元,增幅为70.7%。
As shown in the final columns of Table 12, based on the projected volumes in Table 3, and finalized fees, the fees increase by $2,945.9 or by 70.7 percent.
表13列出了适用于现有表格最终费用变化的经济影响。最后几列报告了所有受影响表格的年度总额,最后几行则依次报告了各表格的10年平均年度数字。虽然I-956表格只有一个表格,但我们列出了两列,分别对应初次申请(“i”)和修正案(“a”)。之所以将二者分开列出,是因为尽管二者目前的费用相同(均为17,795美元),但其最终费用将有所不同(分别为44,115美元和9,835美元)。需要说明的是,新设立的I-527表格(旧I-526表格修正案),其预计年度数量较小,为20份,未包含在表13中。该表格将采用与其他表格不同的核算方式,并在单独的模块中处理。具体而言,与该表格相关的费用影响将被核算为成本,而其他表格的费用影响将构成转移支付。表13列出了预计年度数量以及当前和最终的申请费用水平。表13之所以采用这种方式呈现,是因为预计各财年的数量将保持相同,为简洁起见,未逐年列示实际年度数据。该表还列出了当前与未来申请费用之间差额所构成的影响,最后一列显示各表格的10年总额。
Table 13 builds the economic impacts applicable to the final fee changes for existing forms. The final columns report the annual total across all impacted forms, while the final rows report the 10-year average annual figures for each form, in order. While there is a single Form I-956, we have included two columns to account for initial filings (“i”) and amendments (“a”). The reason for parsing them out is that while their current fee is the same ($17,795) their final fees will be different ($44,115 and $9,835, in order). It is noted that the new Form I-527, Amendment to Legacy Form I-526, with a small projected annual volume of 20, is not included in Table 13. This form will incur a different accounting protocol from the other forms and is treated in a separate module. Specifically, the fee impacts associated with this form will be accounted for as a cost while the others will constitute transfers. Table 13 presents the projected annual volumes as well as the filing fees at the current and final levels. Table 13 is set up this way because the volumes are projected to be the same each FY, and for brevity each actual year is therefore not shown. The table also presents the impact as the difference between current and future filing fees, and the final column shows the 10-year totals per form.
(印刷页第61968页)
( printed page 61968)
如表13所示,根据数量预测,按现行收费标准计算,与EB-5项目表格申请相关的费用在10年内为5.6628亿美元,未折现年化为5663万美元。根据最终费用标准计算,申请费用在10年内为10.5483亿美元,未折现年化为1.0548亿美元。其影响(差额)将构成10年内增加4.8855亿美元,或年化增加4885万美元(表13)。最终费用变更所带来的影响将代表申请人向国土安全部(DHS)转移支付的净增加。
As Table 13 reports, based on the volume projections, at current fee rates the costs associated with filing forms for the EB-5 program would be $566.28 million over 10 years or $56.63 million annually in undiscounted terms. Based on the final fees, the filing costs will be $1,054.83 million over 10 years or $105.48 million annually in undiscounted terms. The impact (difference) would constitute an increase of $488.55 million over 10 years or an increase of $48.85 million on an annual basis (Table 13). The impacts attributable to the final fee changes will represent a net increase in transfers from requestors to DHS.
c. 最终规则的成本
c. Costs of the Final Rule
除了与表格相关费用的转移支付影响外,还有若干影响被计入成本。国土安全部(DHS)确定,由于本规则,现行EB-5项目表格将出现轻微的时间负担变化。为估算时间影响的机会成本,我们需要依赖一个小时工资区间。这一点较为困难,因为EB-5实体可能涉及复杂的商业活动。国土安全部(DHS)没有关于个体申请人所从事具体职业的确切信息,但我们评估认为,参与该项目投资的大多数个人(对于区域中心、新设商业企业(NCE)和职位创造企业(JCE)而言)主要从事安排贷款和融资,以及管理与商业计划相关事务的业务。因此,国土安全部(DHS)从劳工统计局(BLS)标准职业分类代码(SOC)中选取了20个职业,我们认为这些职业能够合理地涵盖参与上述活动的个人。这些SOC职业名称及相应细分行业的平均小时工资列于表14。
In addition to transfer-impacts pertinent to form related fees, several impacts are accounted for as costs. DHS determines that there will be minor time burden changes applicable to the existing EB-5 Program forms due to this rule. To estimate the opportunity cost of time impacts, we need to rely on an hourly wage bound. This is difficult because EB-5 entities can involve complex business activities. DHS does not have salient information on the jobs the individual filers are involved in, but we assess that most individuals involved in the program investments are primarily involved (for regional centers, NCEs, and JCEs) in the business of arranging loans and financing and managing these efforts applicable to business plans. Therefore, DHS selected 20 occupations from the Bureau of Labor Statistics (BLS) Standard Occupational Codes (SOC) that we think reasonably capture the individuals involved in these activities. These SOC titles and associated terms mean hourly wage for the detailed industries are reported in Table 14.
(印刷页第61969页)
( printed page 61969)
上述区间的最低值、中间值和最高值依次为41.58美元、64.72美元,[52] 以及87.86美元。然而,通过递归计算,所得出的货币化影响仅受工资区间的极小影响,因此为简洁起见,我们将以中间值为基础进行估算。国土安全部(DHS)通过计算适用于劳工统计局(BLS)最新报告中有关全体文职人员在主要职业群体和行业中雇主平均薪酬成本的福利负担比例,来核算员工福利。福利占薪酬的负担比例为45%。[53] 国土安全部(DHS)将依据此负担比例,估算新员工所产生的全部成本,包括员工工资薪金以及带薪休假、保险、退休金及其他福利等全部福利成本。按1.45的福利负担乘数计算,每小时薪酬为93.84美元。[54]
The minimum, mid-point, and maximum of the above range are $41.58, $64.72,[52] and $87.86, in order. However, working recursively, the resulting monetized impacts are only very slightly affected by the wage range and thus, for brevity we will rely on the midpoint to base our estimates. DHS accounts for employee benefits by calculating a benefits-burden applicable to the most recent BLS report detailing the average employer costs for employee compensation for all civilian workers in major occupational groups and industries. The burden to compensation from benefits is 45 percent.[53] DHS will rely on this burden to estimate the full costs incurred by new employees, including employee wages and salaries and the full cost of benefits such as paid leave, insurance, retirement, and other benefits. With a benefits-burden multiple of 1.45, hourly compensation is $93.84.[54]
(印刷页第61970页)
( printed page 61970)
表15列出了当前、预计及变化的时间负担(以小时计)。
The current, projected, and change in the time burdens (in hours) are provided in Table 15.
为得出E列所报告的影响(机会成本),将数量乘以负担变化,再乘以中间薪酬(93.84美元)。F列报告了每种表格的年度及10年影响,底部行则提供了各表格的合计数。根据所提供的信息,年度总成本为540,004.7美元,十年总成本约为540万美元。除总计外,最后一列(G)提供了权重系数,该系数反映了每种表格的权重系数(见表12)乘以预计负担变化(表15的D列)。各权重系数之和为0.347小时,相当于约20.8分钟。
To obtain the impact (opportunity cost) reported in Column E, the volume is multiplied by the change in the burden and by the mid-point compensation ($93.84). Columns F report the annual and 10-year impacts per form, while the bottom rows provide the totals across forms. Based on the information provided, the annual total cost will be $540,004.7 and about $5.4 million over ten years. In addition to the totals, a weight factor is provided in the final column (G), which reflects the weight factor perform (see Table 12) multiplied by the projected burden change (column D table 15). The weight factors sum to 0.347 hours, which equates to about 20.8 minutes.
新的I-527表格的影响将计入提交申请的直接成本以及与提交所需时间负担相关的机会成本。最终费用为10,330美元,时间负担估计为1.44小时,根据上文所述的负担中点薪酬(93.84美元),每份申请产生的时间相关影响为135.14美元。将这两项成本相加,每份申请的总影响为10,465.14美元,按预计年申请量20份计算,每年产生209,000美元的影响,10年累计达209万美元。对于少数情况下,移民投资者的配偶及子女未被包含在移民投资者提交的I-829表格中,而需单独提交I-829表格申请的情形,如序言中所述,现行法规的最终修订不会产生任何额外的生物特征采集、travel旅行或相关机会成本。本规则预计产生的唯一成本为单独申请费用及相关机会成本。I-829表格的最终费用为5,000美元,时间负担为3.62小时。对于受抚养人,我们将采用低于投资者所使用的工资水平。由于缺乏关于这些适用申请人将获得何种工资的具体信息,我们假设他们从事各种不同层级的工作,并采用目前所有职业的平均工资,即32.66美元,经福利负担调整后为47.36美元。[55] 每位申请人将面临171.44美元的时间负担成本,加上申请费用后总计为5,171.44美元。根据2015财年至2023财年9年间平均每年11份申请的年度化数据,单独提交I-829表格的申请人每年将产生的货币化影响为56,885.88美元,10年累计约为57万美元。
The new Form I-527 impacts would accrue to the direct cost of filing plus the opportunity costs associated with the time burden of filing. The final fee is $10,330 and the time burden is estimated at 1.44 hours, which, based on the burdened mid-point compensation (discussed above of $93.84) yields a time-related impact of $135.14 per submission. Adding the two components amounts to $10,465.14 per filing, which, at the projected annual volume of 20, generates an impact of $209,000 annually and $2.09 million over 10 years. For the few cases in which an immigrant investor's spouse and children file separate Form I-829 petitions when they are not included in the Form I-829 filed by the immigrant investor, as stipulated in the preamble, the final revisions to the existing regulations would not impose any additional biometric, travel, or associated opportunity costs. The only costs expected from the rule would be the separate filing fee and associated opportunity cost. The final fee for Form I-829 is $5,000 and the time burden is 3.62 hours. For the dependents we would use a lower wage than was utilized for investors. Without salient information concerning the wages these applicable filers would earn, we will assume they are working at various levels and will rely on the current average wage across all occupations, which is currently $32.66, and is $47.36 when burdened for benefits.[55] Each filer would face a time burden cost of $171.44, which when added to the filing fee would be $5,171.44. Based on 11 annualized filings' average over 9 years (FY 2015 through FY 2023), the monetized impact that will accrue to the individual Form I-829 filers would be $56,885.88 annually, or about $.57 million over 10 years.
d. 总体货币化影响
d. Total Monetized Impacts
现在,我们可以根据能够合理得出量化估计的影响,汇总本规则的货币化影响。 (第61971页) 与现有表格费用变更相关的影响被归类为申请人向国土安全部(DHS)的转移支付。之所以将其核算为转移支付,是因为目前存在申请费用(包括目前费用为0美元的两种表格),且申请人期望从提交申请中获得直接利益。与新I-527表格相关的影响被归类为成本,如前所述,适用于I-829表格的表格负担及申请量变化同样归类为成本。在表16中,转移支付和成本分别列示,因为根据OMB第A-4号通告框架,二者的分类不同。转移支付,即EB-5申请人向国土安全部(移民审查手续费账户,IEFA)提交表格时支付的款项,反映了已最终确定的费用变更。成本栏包括主要由EB-5申请人承担的年度影响,这些影响源于新I-527表格以及少数单独提交的I-829受抚养人申请,以及相关表格负担。表16中列示的货币化影响按未贴现、以3%贴现和以7%贴现的顺序依次呈现。[56] 表16中列出了每个财政年度的数据,因为每年的贴现金额并不相同。
We can now compile the monetized impacts of the rule based on the impacts for which we can reasonably develop a quantified estimate. The impacts ( printed page 61971) associated with the existing forms' fee changes are categorized as transfers from requestors to DHS. They are accounted for as transfers because a filing fee currently exists (including the two forms in which it is currently $0) and the requestor expects to recoup a direct benefit from filing. The impacts associated with the new Form I-527 are classified as costs, as are the changes in the forms' burdens and filings applicable to the Form I-829, as discussed earlier. In Table 16 the transfers and costs are listed individually since they are categorized differently under the OMB Circular A-4 framework. The transfers, payments made by EB-5 requestors when filing forms to DHS (IEFA), reflect the fee changes finalized. The costs column comprises the annual impacts, mainly to EB-5 requesters, accruing to the new Form I-527 and the small number of separate I-829 dependent filers, as well as the forms' burdens. The monetized impacts are presented in Table 16 in order of terms undiscounted, then discounted at 3 and 7 percent, in order.[56] In Table 16 each FY is shown, since the discounted terms for each year are not the same.

e. 未量化的影响
e. Unquantified Impacts
国土安全部(DHS)还评估了适用于本规则的其他一些影响,尽管这些影响无法货币化,但国土安全部仍对其进行定性讨论。首先,阅读和理解本规则很可能产生熟悉成本。熟悉成本将计入所耗费时间的机会成本,即审阅者花费在熟悉规则上的小时数乘以审阅者的每小时薪酬。国土安全部不清楚将由何种职业人员审阅本规则,但将把该成本归于受过培训、能够阅读和解释本规则变更内容的律师。平均每小时薪酬为87.86美元,按1.45倍的福利负担系数计算,为每小时127.40美元。[57] 这反映的是内部律师的成本。对于外聘律师,我们采用2.5倍的系数,得出每小时费率为219.65美元。[58] 由于律师的收入大大高于大多数职业,国土安全部在估算时采取了较为宽松的做法。国土安全部不清楚用于此类熟悉过程将耗费多少时间。
There are some other impacts that DHS has evaluated applicable to the rule, and while these cannot be monetized, DHS offers a qualitative discussion concerning them. Foremost, there are likely to be familiarization costs associated with reading and understanding the rule. The costs of familiarization would accrue to the opportunity costs of the time embodied, which would constitute the number of hours spent on familiarization multiplied by the hourly compensation of the reviewer(s). DHS does not know who (in terms of what occupation) would review the rule but will attribute the costs to lawyers trained in reading and interpreting the rule's changes. The average hourly compensation would be $87.86, which, at a benefits-burden multiple of 1.45, is $127.40 per hour.[57] This reflects the cost of an in-house attorney. For outsourced attorneys, we utilize a multiplier of 2.5, which yields an hourly rate of $219.65.[58] By relying on the earnings of lawyers, which are substantially higher than that of most occupations, DHS is being liberal in its estimates. DHS does not know how much time would be expended on such familiarization.
《EB-5改革法案》授权对未提交年度报表或实施某些违规行为的区域中心施加分级制裁。鉴于此项授权,国土安全部（DHS）将对逾期缴纳诚信基金费用实施以下处罚：
The EB-5 Reform Act authorizes graduated sanctions for regional centers that fail to submit an annual statement or that commit certain violations. Considering this authorization, DHS will impose the following penalties for paying the Integrity Fund fee late:
对于在到期日后第31天至第60天（含第60天）之间缴纳费用的区域中心，处以所需诚信费用百分之十的罚款（例如，10,000美元或20,000美元的百分之十，此类所需金额可能因通货膨胀而作相应调整）[59]。
Ten percent of the required integrity fee (e.g., 10 percent of $10,000 or $20,000, subject to adjusting such required amounts for inflation) [59] for a regional center that pays its fees on day 31 through and including day 60 after the due date.
对于在到期日后第61天至第90天（含第90天）之间缴纳费用的区域中心，处以所需诚信费用百分之二十的罚款。
Twenty percent of the required integrity fee for a regional center if their fee is paid on day 61 through and including day 90 after it is due.
如果区域中心未能在费用到期之日起90天内缴纳该费用，则终止该区域中心的指定资格。
Terminate a regional center designation if it fails to pay the fee within 90 days of the date on which such fee is due.
如前言所述，在确定最终处罚时，国土安全部认为百分之十是设定处罚的合理起点。国土安全部还考虑了该金额本身是否合理。在本案中，百分之十将根据区域中心的不同相当于1,000美元或2,000美元，国土安全部认为这是对逾期30天未缴纳费用的合理滞纳金。百分之二十将根据区域中心（基于投资者人数）的不同相当于2,000美元或4,000美元，国土安全部认为这是对逾期60天未缴纳费用的合理滞纳金。
In determining the final penalties, as discussed in the preamble, DHS believed that 10 percent was a reasonable starting point in setting a penalty. DHS also considered whether the dollar amount itself was reasonable. In this case, the 10 percent would amount to $1,000 or $2,000 depending on the regional center, which DHS believes is a reasonable late charge for failing to pay the fee after 30 days. The 20 percent would amount to $2,000 or $4,000 depending on the regional center (based on the number of investors), which DHS believes is a reasonable late charge for failing to pay the fee after 60 days.
此类处罚的目的是有效遏制不合规行为。国土安全部认为，该处罚力度足以鼓励按时缴费并确保及时收取诚信基金费用，同时又不会过大以致具有惩罚性或造成财务损害。国土安全部无法估计将有多少实体会缴纳罚款，或他们将缴纳多少罚款。
The goal of the penalties is to effectively deter noncompliance. DHS believes that the penalties would be sufficient to encourage payment and ensure timely collection of the Integrity Fund fees, while not being so large as to be punitive or financially damaging. DHS cannot make an estimate of how many entities would pay penalties or how much they would pay.
本最终规则将为公众带来益处。所设定的费用水平将能够使国土安全部收回管理EB-5项目的成本；使该项目能够按照其最初设想，通过移民投资者创造就业和投资资本来促进美国经济增长；使美国公民及移民服务局（USCIS）能够实现法定处理时限目标；并确保项目办公室内部建立相应的程序/控制机制，以最大限度地提高实现法定目标的可能性。本规则还将改进国土安全部用于管理EB-5项目的信息技术系统。 （印刷页第61975页）
This final rule will generate benefits to the public. The set fees will support the level that would enable DHS to recover the costs of administering the EB-5 program; allow the program to promote U.S. economic growth through job creation and capital investment by immigrant investors as it was originally intended; enable USCIS to attain the statutory processing time goals; and ensure there would be internal procedures/controls in place within the program office to maximize the likelihood that the statutory goals will be met. It will make improvements to the information technology systems used by DHS to administer the EB-5 program. ( printed page 61975)
B. 《监管灵活性法案》（RFA）
B. Regulatory Flexibility Act (RFA)
1. 最终监管灵活性分析（FRFA）
1. Final Regulatory Flexibility Analysis (FRFA)
1980年《监管灵活性法案》（RFA）（《美国法典》第5编第601条及第602条），经《1996年小企业监管执行公平法案》（公法104-121号，第二编，110 Stat. 847（《美国法典》第5编第601条附注））修订，要求联邦机构在制定规则过程中考虑法规对小企业、小型政府辖区和小型组织可能产生的影响。“小实体”一词包括小企业、独立拥有和运营且在其所属领域不占主导地位的非营利组织，以及人口少于50,000人的政府辖区。[60]
The Regulatory Flexibility Act of 1980 (RFA), 5 U.S.C. 601 and 602, as amended by the Small Business Regulatory Enforcement Fairness Act of 1996 (Pub. L. 104-121, tit. II, 110 Stat. 847 (5 U.S.C. 601 note)), requires Federal agencies to consider the potential impact of regulations on small businesses, small governmental jurisdictions, and small organizations during the development of their rules. The term “small entities” comprises small businesses, not-for-profit organizations that are independently owned and operated and are not dominant in their fields, and governmental jurisdictions with populations of less than 50,000.[60]
国土安全部依据《监管灵活性法案》的要求，对拟议规则及本最终规则进行了审查。国土安全部拟定稿的费用表每年将影响涉及九种现行表格和一种新表格的约16,600份EB-5项目申请。就九种现行表格而言，其总体费用将在现有水平基础上增加约70.7%，即每份表格增加约2,945.90美元。[61]此外，某些申请和表格负担还会给申请人带来相关成本。虽然与移民福利相关的表格主要适用于个人，但技术费和诚信基金费用及罚款直接影响实体。在《监管灵活性法案》涉及EB-5项目及本规则方面，共评估了四类实体：（1）区域中心；（2）新成立企业（NCE）；（3）工作创造实体（JCE）；以及（4）投资者。国土安全部已确定，该项目中的投资者属于自愿选择将其资金投入该项目的个人，就《监管灵活性法案》而言不被视为小实体。《监管灵活性法案》并未将“个人”定义为小实体，个人因规则产生的成本不在《监管灵活性法案》的考量范围之内。[62]基于此项认定，个人不包含在本最终监管灵活性分析（FRFA）范围内，国土安全部将本分析重点置于与EB-5项目投资直接相关的商业主体方面。
DHS reviewed the proposed rule and this final rule as required by the RFA. The fee schedule DHS is finalizing will impact approximately 16,600 EB-5 program filings annually across nine current forms and one new form. For the nine current forms the collective fees will increase from their current level by about 70.7 percent, or by about $2,945.90 per form.[61] In addition there are costs associated with certain filings and form burdens to requestors. While the forms related to immigration benefits mainly apply to individuals, the Technology Fee and the Integrity Fund Fees and Penalties directly impact entities. There are four types of entities that were evaluated in terms of the RFA as it pertains to the EB-5 program and the rule: (1) regional centers; (2) NCEs; (3) JCEs; and (4) investors. DHS has determined that the investors in the program are individuals who willingly choose to invest their capital in the program and are not considered small entities for purposes of the RFA. An “individual” is not defined by the RFA as a small entity and costs to an individual from a rule are not considered for RFA purposes.[62] As a result of this determination, individuals are not covered in this Final Regulatory Flexibility Analysis (FRFA), and DHS focuses this analysis on the business components pertinent to the EB-5 program directly involved in its investments.
a. 对制定本规则的必要性及其目标的说明。
a. A statement of the need for, and objectives of, the rule.
根据《EB-5改革法》的要求,国土安全部开展了一项针对EB-5项目的专项费用研究。该研究得出的结论是,本规则最终确定的适用于EB-5项目的各项费用将设定在一个水平,国土安全部认为该水平能够使其:收回管理EB-5项目的成本;使该机构能够达到处理时限目标;并确保在项目办公室内部建立相应的程序/管控机制,以最大限度地提高实现法定目标的可能性。此外,还旨在改进国土安全部用于管理EB-5项目的信息技术系统。本最终规则的目标是使国土安全部调整EB-5受益请求费用,以满足《EB-5改革法》规定的要求,并充分资助管理EB-5项目的成本。国土安全部打算通过以下方式实现该目标:(一)按照序言中列明的费用表设定费用;(二)设立美国公民及移民服务局(USCIS)EB-5技术费;以及(三)将EB-5诚信基金费用及罚款纳入法规。
As is required by EB-5 Reform Act, DHS conducted an EB-5-specific fee study. The determination from the study is that the fees being finalized as applicable to the EB-5 program will be set at a level that the Department has determined would enable it to: recover the costs of administering the EB-5 program; allow the Agency to attain the processing times goals; and ensure there are internal procedures/controls in place within the program office to maximize the likelihood that the statutory goals are met. It is intended, further, to make improvements to the information technology systems used by DHS to administer the EB-5 program. The objective of this final rule is for DHS to adjust EB-5 benefit request fees to meet the requirements provided in the EB-5 Reform Act and adequately fund the cost of administering the EB-5 program. DHS intends to meet this objective by: (i) setting fees according to the schedule presented in the preamble; (ii) establishing the USCIS EB-5 Technology Fee; and (iii) codifying EB-5 Integrity Fund Fees and Penalties.
根据《EB-5改革法》,国土安全部最终确定各项费用,以充分收回提供此类服务的成本,并实现平均在以下期限内完成审批的目标:
In accordance with the EB-5 Reform Act, DHS is finalizing the fees to sufficiently recover the costs of providing such services, and attaining the goal of completing adjudications, on average, not later than:
(1) 自收到区域中心申请或对新商业企业(NCE)投资申请之日起180天内;[63]
(1) 180 days after receiving a regional center application or application for investment in a new commercial enterprise (NCE); [63]
(2) 自收到对位于目标就业区(TEA)内的新商业企业(NCE)投资申请之日起90天内;[64]
(2) 90 days after receiving an application for investment in an NCE that is located in a TEA; [64]
(3) 自收到依据该法第203(b)(5)(E)条申请分类的移民投资者申请,或依据该法第216A条申请解除条件之日起240天内;以及
(3) 240 days after receiving an immigrant investor petition for classification under section 203(b)(5)(E) of the Act or a petition to remove conditions under section 216A of the Act; and
(4) 自收到依据该法第203(b)(5)(E)条就目标就业区(TEA)内投资申请分类的移民投资者申请之日起120天内。
(4) 120 days after receiving an immigrant investor petition for classification under section 203(b)(5)(E) of the Act with respect to an investment in a TEA.
国土安全部根据《EB-5改革法》赋予的权力最终确定本规则。除其他事项外,《EB-5改革法》立即废止了此前根据1993年《商务、司法、国务院、司法机构及相关机构拨款法》(公法102-395,106 Stat. 1828,第610条)授权区域中心计划的法定条款,并在《移民与国籍法》(INA)中新增了授权条款,自2022年5月14日起对区域中心计划进行了实质性改革。经改革的区域中心计划的授权期限延续至2027年9月30日。本规则也符合关于费用、预算程序及联邦会计原则的非法定指导意见。[65] 国土安全部采用《行政管理和预算局通告A-25号》(OMB Circular A-25)作为确定移民福利请求使用费的指导依据。如需申请拨款以抵消部分《移民审查执行账户》(IEFA)成本,国土安全部还遵循《行政管理和预算局通告A-11号》(OMB Circular A-11)中的年度指导意见。国土安全部采用《通告A-25号》和《通告A-11号》所支持的作业成本法(ABC方法)制定了最终的EB-5项目费用表。
DHS finalizes this rule under the authority of the EB-5 Reform Act. Among other things, the EB-5 Reform Act immediately repealed the former authorizing statutory provisions for the Regional Center Program under the Departments of Commerce, Justice, and State, the Judiciary, and Related Agencies Appropriations Act 1993, Public Law 102-395, 106 Stat. 1828, sec. 610, and added new authorizing provisions to the INA, substantially reforming the Regional Center Program effective May 14, 2022. The reformed Regional Center Program is authorized through September 30, 2027. This rule is also consistent with non-statutory guidance on fees, the budget process, and Federal accounting principles.[65] DHS uses OMB Circular A-25 as guidance for determining user fees for immigration benefit requests. DHS also follows the annual guidance in OMB Circular A-11 if it requests appropriations to offset a portion of IEFA costs. DHS used the ABC methodology supported in OMB Circulars A-25 and A-11 to develop the final EB-5 program fee schedule.
b. 关于公众对初步监管灵活性分析(IRFA)所提意见中提出的重大问题的说明,本机构对此类问题的评估说明,以及因此类意见而对本规则所作任何修改的说明。
b. A statement of the significant issues raised by the public comments in response to the IRFA, a statement of the assessment of the agency of such issues, and a statement of any changes made in the rule as a result of such comments.
一位评论者指出,尽管本规则对数百个小实体(区域中心、新商业企业及相关小型企业)产生直接影响,但本规则遗漏了《监管灵活性法》分析,或仅提供了结论性的分析,且未能按照5 U.S.C. 603-604条的要求考虑负担较轻的替代方案。另一位评论者建议,美国公民及移民服务局应编制并公布一份初步监管灵活性分析(IRFA),或者,如援引5 U.S.C. 605(b)条,则应提供支持任何认证结论的详细事实依据,并考虑能够将对小实体影响降至最低的重大替代方案。 (印刷页第61976页)
A commenter stated that the rule omits, or provides only a conclusory, Regulatory Flexibility Act analysis despite direct impacts on hundreds of small entities (RCs, NCEs, and related small businesses) and fails to consider less burdensome alternatives as required by 5 U.S.C. 603-604. Another commenter suggested that USCIS prepare and publish an Initial Regulatory Flexibility Analysis (IRFA) or, if invoking 5 U.S.C. 605(b), provide a detailed factual basis supporting any certification, and consider significant alternatives to minimize small-entity impacts. ( printed page 61976)
DHS进行并发布了初步监管灵活性分析(IRFA)[66],认定大多数参与EB-5项目的企业为小型企业,并在本最终规则中维持该认定。尽管DHS认识到较高的费用可能会给部分申请人,特别是较小的投资者带来挑战,但此次确定的费用反映了审理、防止欺诈、合规活动以及项目管理的实际成本。若将费用设定在成本回收水平之外,可能会损害美国公民及移民服务局(USCIS)提供充分服务和维护项目完整性的能力。DHS考虑了包括分级或过渡性费用结构在内的其他替代方案,但认定统一的费用表是分摊成本和避免行政复杂性的最有效方式。
DHS conducted and published an initial regulatory flexibility analyses (IRFA) [66] and determined that most businesses involved with the EB-5 program would be small and sustains that determination in this final rule. While DHS recognizes that higher fees may present challenges for some applicants, particularly smaller investors, the fees being finalized reflect the actual costs of adjudication, fraud prevention, compliance activities, and administration of the program. Setting fees outside cost-recovery levels could compromise USCIS' ability to provide adequate services and maintain program integrity. DHS considered alternatives, including tiered or transitional fee structures, but determined that a uniform fee schedule presents the most effective way to distribute costs and avoid administrative complexity.
DHS将密切监测费用变化对项目参与情况的影响,并将根据需求和资格变化的情况,评估未来是否有必要进行调整或采取其他替代方式。鉴于预测参与情况和收入所固有的不确定性,现行费用表旨在满足运营需求和法定要求。
DHS will closely monitor the effects of fee changes on program participation and will assess the need for future adjustments or alternative approaches as warranted by changes in demand and eligibility. The current fee schedule is designed to address operational needs and statutory requirements, with recognition of the uncertainty inherent in forecasting participation and revenue.
c. 该机构对小企业管理局(Small Business Administration)倡导首席法律顾问(Chief Counsel for Advocacy)就拟议规则提交的任何意见作出的回应,以及因该等意见而对拟议规则在最终规则中所作任何修改的详细说明。
c. The response of the agency to any comments filed by the Chief Counsel for Advocacy of the Small Business Administration in response to the proposed rule, and a detailed statement of any change made to the proposed rule in the final rule as a result of the comments.
小企业管理局倡导首席法律顾问未就本次规则制定提交相关意见。
Chief Counsel for Advocacy of the Small Business Administration did not submit a comment relevant to this rulemaking.
d. 对该规则将适用的小实体数量的说明和估计,或者若无法作出此类估计,说明其原因。
d. A description of and an estimate of the number of small entities to which the rule will apply or an explanation of why no such estimate is available.
希望根据EB-5项目移民美国的人员,须提交《独立投资人移民申请》(Form I-526)或《区域中心投资人移民申请》(Form I-526E),其中须包含有关其投资的信息。该投资必须投入指定区域中心内按照区域中心项目设立的新商业企业(NCE),或投入区域中心项目之外的独立新商业企业(NCE)。区域中心是指美国国土安全部(DHS)根据旨在促进经济增长(包括预期创造就业机会和增加国内资本投资)的提案所指定的美国境内商业实体。区域中心将多名投资者的资金汇集起来,通常安排其投资于其管辖下的新商业企业(NCE)。新商业企业(NCE)可以直接创造就业机会(非区域中心投资所要求),也可以作为独立的工作创造实体(JCE)的资金来源(区域中心投资所允许)。
A person wishing to immigrate to the United States under the EB-5 program is required to file an Immigrant Petition by Standalone Investor (Form I-526) or Immigrant Petition by Regional Center Investor (Form I-526E), containing information about their investment. The investment must be made into either an NCE within a designated regional center in accordance with the regional center program or a standalone NCE outside of the regional center program. A regional center is a business entity in the United States designated by DHS based on a proposal for the promotion of economic growth, including prospective job creation and increased domestic capital investment. Regional centers pool the capital of multiple investors together and arrange them typically as investments in NCEs under their purview. The NCE may create jobs directly (required for non-regional center investments) or serve as a source of funding for separate JCEs (allowable for regional center investments).
DHS无法准确评估此次最终确定的变更可能影响的小实体数量,也无法确定这些变更对参与该项目的小实体可能产生何种影响,或其可能如何应对这些影响。[67]EB-5投资和商业结构往往较为复杂,涉及多层次的商业和金融活动。本部门用于支持小实体分析的信息和数据有限。然而,根据现有数据,DHS可以为初步评估提供一些标准。如前所述,投资者不被视为《监管灵活性法》(RFA)管辖范围内的对象。此外,典型个人投资的金额本身,即已降低的最低投资额80万美元,以及投资资本总额,均不适宜作为本次评估的收入来考虑。[68]因此,在这两项说明前提下,首先评估区域中心,随后评估与该项目相关的其他EB-5企业。
DHS cannot provide a precise assessment of the number of small entities that could be impacted by the changes being finalized, nor can the Department determine what such impacts might be to small entities involved in the program or how they might respond to them.[67] EB-5 investment and business structures tend to be complex and involve multiple layers of business and financial activity. The Department has limited information and data to support a small entity analysis. However, based on available data, DHS can provide some criteria for an initial assessment. As noted earlier, investors are not considered under the purview of the RFA. Further, neither the amount of a typical individual investment itself—which is the reduced minimum investment amount of $800,000—nor the pool of total investment capital, is appropriate to consider as income for this assessment.[68] Therefore, with these two caveats regional centers are assessed first, followed by other EB-5 businesses associated with the program.
一、区域中心
i. Regional Centers
根据本部门三十年来管理区域中心项目的经验,其认定区域中心通过三种主要机制获得收入。DHS在接下来的三段中描述这三种机制。
Based on the Department's thirty years of experience administering the regional center program, it determined that regional centers earn income through three primary mechanisms. In the next three paragraphs DHS describes these three mechanisms.
第一,区域中心向投资者收取行政费,用于与投资项目相关的营销和运营费用。该费用还可能涵盖与文件编制、法律监督以及用于建模和估算影响与创造就业的经济分析相关的费用。该行政费通常为个人投资额的10%,因此美国国土安全部(DHS)将依据适用于大多数预期投资额80万美元的典型百分比,估算每位投资者的基准费用为8万美元。[69] 这一依据的合理性在于,几乎所有EB-5活动均集中在较低门槛的投资上,即符合当前降低后的80万美元投资要求,而非1,050,000美元的标准金额。[70] 在2016财年至2025财年期间,通过区域中心进行的投资共计50,766笔,其中几乎全部,即50,703笔,均以降低后的金额进行。[71]
First, regional centers charge investors an administrative fee earmarked to expenses for marketing and operations pertinent to the investment offering. The fee may also cover expenses related to document preparation, legal oversight, and the economic analysis utilized to model and estimate impacts and job creation. This administrative fee is typically 10 percent of the individual investment amount; hence DHS will rely on the typical percentage applied to most expected investments of $800,000 to estimate an amount of $80,000 per investor as a baseline.[69] This reliance is justified on grounds that almost all EB-5 activity has accrued to investments at the reduced threshold—which qualify for the current reduced investment requirement of $800,000 as opposed to the standard amount of $1,050,000.[70] For the period FY 2016 through FY 2025, there were 50,766 investments made under regional centers, of which almost all, 50,703, were made at the reduced amount.[71]
第二,区域中心还可以根据与其关联的新设商业企业(NCE)和职位创造实体(JCE)之间的安排,收取营销费、销售费及其他费用和收入。一些区域中心在提交给DHS的商业计划书或修正案中提供了有关这些活动的信息,但这并非强制要求,且DHS没有足够的官方数据来支持对该类收入来源的分析。第三,区域中心可以赚取剩余收入。它们可能从其打包提供给投资者的贷款条款与实际返还给投资者的金额之间的差额中获得收入。也可能存在来自JCE所开展的最终经济活动所产生利润形式的投资回报。此类投资回报可能与其他参与的商业实体分享,但DHS没有足够的数据涉及区域中心所获得的利息或利润,以评估此类收入。[72] 为进行初步监管灵活性分析(IRFA),DHS使用了截至分析开展之日(2023年11月14日)处于批准状态的640个已获批准的区域中心。[73] 在基于订阅的开源商业数据提供商中,逐一查询这些区域中心名称,以获取区域中心的收入信息。搜索共获得339条有效匹配记录,这些记录包含收入数字和北美行业分类系统(NAICS)代码。所提供的收入数据点被称为“销售收入”,我们评估认为,这些数据中报告的收入最有可能是归因于销售、营销及其他相关费用的收入,而非向投资者收取的行政费用或贷款、投资利润。尽管339的样本量足以满足基于总体规模(640)的95%置信水平和5%置信区间的要求,但该数据仍存在限制。NAICS代码提供至6位数的详细行业层级,但其中半数实体(173个,占51.0%)报告的代码为999990,该代码对应“无法分类机构”。因此,没有可供比较以判定小实体身份的小企业管理局(SBA)规模标准。[74] 因此,仅有166个实体可支持该分析。为弥补这一不足,DHS将搜索范围扩大至2016财年至2022财年期间获批的区域中心。从匹配结果中,DHS剔除了初次搜索结果(640个现有区域中心中的339个)中的重复项,以及未包含NAICS代码(含无法分类代码)或销售数字之一或两者的记录。经过这一筛选过程,又获得32个额外实体,加上最初有效匹配的166个实体,共计198个实体。该数字仍低于最佳样本量241,但对精度的影响并不算过于严重,因为误差幅度为5.8%,而非预期的5.0%。正如DHS将要讨论的那样,出于所面临限制的必要性,本评估沿多条不同且非常规的路径展开。因此,表17列出了(仅就来自网络数据的收入而言)“完整”样本组(339个目前已获批准、可分类与不可分类的区域中心,加上在补充搜索中获得的32条记录)以及“限定”(仅可分类)组的指标。
Second, regional centers can also collect marketing, sales fees and other charges and income owed to arrangements with their affiliated NCEs and JCEs. Some regional centers provide information concerning these activities in their business plans or amendments submitted to DHS, but it is not required, and DHS does not have sufficient official data on this source of income to ( printed page 61977) support an analysis. Third, regional centers can earn residual income. They may capture income from the differential on the terms of the loans they bundle and what is returned to investors. There may also be return on investment in the forms of profit from the end-state economic activity being conducted by the JCE. Some of this return on investment may be split with other business entities involved, but DHS does not have an adequate amount of data involving interest or profit accruing to regional centers to assess this type of income.[72] To conduct the IRFA analysis, DHS utilized the 640 approved regional centers that were in approval status at date the analysis was conducted (November 14, 2023).[73] to run their respective regional center names in subscription-based, open-source business data providers to obtain income information on the regional centers. The search yielded 339 viable record matches that included an income figure and a North American Industry Classification System (NAICS) code. The income data point provided is deemed “sales revenue” and it is our assessment that the income reported in these data is most likely revenue attributed to sales, marketing, and other related charges involved, and neither the administrative fees charged to investors nor profits on loans or investment. While the sample size of 339 is more than sufficient to satisfy a 95-percent level of confidence level and a 5-percent confidence interval based on the population size (640), the data pose a constraint. The NAICS codes are provided at the 6-digit detailed industry level, but half the entities (173, or 51.0 percent) reported code 999990, which benchmarks “Non-Classifiable Establishments.” There is thus no SBA size standard to weigh against for small entity status.[74] As a result, there would only be 166 entities to support an analysis. To attempt to mitigate this shortcoming, DHS extended the search query for regional centers approved from FY 2016 through FY 2022. From the matches, DHS culled the results to remove duplicates from the initial search result (339 of the of the 640 current regional centers), plus records that did not include both or either of a NAICS code (including non-classifiable) or a sales figure. This cleansing process yielded 32 additional entities, which when added to the 166 initial valid matches, resulted in 198 entities. This figure is still below the optimal sample size of 241, but the charge to precision is not overly debilitating, as the margin of error is 5.8 percent instead of the desired 5.0 percent. As DHS will discuss, out of necessity of the constraints faced, the assessment is conducted along several different and unconventional paths. Hence, Table 17 presents metrics (in terms of the income alone from the web-based data) for both the “full” sample group (339 currently approved regional centers that are both classifiable and non-classifiable plus the 32 records obtained in the ancillary search) as well as the “restricted” (classifiable-only) group.
较大的差异体现在中位数低于均值,这表明数据结构呈非正态的正偏态分布,其中少数较大数值对均值产生了不成比例的影响,极端值范围也进一步说明了这一点。如表18所示,两个样本组之间的均值和中位数也存在差异。
The large differences captured as the medians being below the means are indicative of non-normal, positively skewed data structures in which a small number of large values exert disproportionate weight on the means, as further indicated by the extreme ranges. As seen in Table 18, there are also differences between the means and the medians across the two sample-groups.
在获得区域中心销售收入的有效数据后,国土安全部(DHS)转向下一个收入来源,即向投资者收取的管理费。为进行本模块评估,国土安全部查询了内部EB-5数据库,以获取上述模块中所收集的区域中心的投资者人数数据。投资者人数的替代指标为在该区域中心管辖范围内提交的I-526表格申请数量。[75] 适用于投资者的关键统计数据见表18。
Having valid data on regional center sales revenue, DHS turns to the next income source, administrative fees charged to investors. To conduct this module of the assessment, DHS queried internal EB-5 data repositories to obtain a figure for the number of investors for the regional centers acquired in the above module. A proxy for the number of investors is developed as the number of Form I-526 filings submitted under the purview of the regional center.[75] Key statistics applicable to investors are provided in Table 18.
(印刷页第61978页)
( printed page 61978)
与区域中心销售收入的情况相同,均值与中位数之间的显著差异以及极端值范围表明,每个区域中心的投资者数量同样呈非正态的正偏态分布。
As was the case with regional center sales revenue, the substantial differences between the means and medians, as well as the extreme range, demonstrate that the number of investors per regional center is also a non-normal distribution that is positively skewed.
国土安全部将投资者人数乘以降低后的80,000美元费用,以估算各区域中心的管理费总额。[76] 国土安全部随后将该数字加上基于订阅数据所得的销售收入。此外,不能排除区域中心也将诚信基金费用转嫁给投资者的可能性。对于投资者总数为20人或以下的区域中心,国土安全部计入10,000美元的费用;对于投资者总数超过20人的区域中心,则计入20,000美元的费用。通过综合这些组成部分,国土安全部得以对样本区域中心的收入进行估算。在全部样本中,确定有48.5%的区域中心缴纳10,000美元的费用,51.5%的区域中心缴纳20,000美元的费用,按照分析进行时640个区域中心的年度总数计算,分别相当于310个和330个区域中心。由于投资者人数是基于该区域中心管辖范围内提交的I-526表格数据得出的,而国土安全部并未根据前言中讨论的与该区域中心相关的I-829表格申请所适用的调整数据来计算总数,因此该比例分布可能略有不同。鉴于迄今讨论的数据限制,为确保稳健性,我们将通过三种不同的方法论途径评估这些实体的小实体身份。尽管国土安全部拥有198个可分类实体所列出的北美行业分类系统(NAICS)代码,但国土安全部经过广泛审查各类NAICS代码后确定,六位数的详细行业NAICS代码522310,即“抵押贷款与非抵押贷款经纪人”,其定义为“主要从事以佣金或收费方式撮合借款人与贷款人以安排贷款的机构所组成的行业”,是适用于区域中心运营的适当NAICS代码。[77]
DHS multiplied the number of investors by the reduced $80,000 fee to capture an estimate of total administrative fees by regional center.[76] DHS next added this figure to sales revenue found in the subscription-based data. In addition, it cannot be ruled out that regional centers pass the Integrity Fund fees onto the investors as well. For regional centers with 20 or fewer total investors, DHS included the $10,000 fee and for those with more than 20 total investors, a $20,000 fee was added. By combining these components, DHS was able to make a revenue estimate for the sample of regional centers. Of the full sample, it is determined that 48.5 percent pay the $10,000 fee and that 51.5 percent pay $20,000, which based on the annual population of 640 (at the time the analysis was conducted), would be 310 and 330 regional centers, in order. The breakdown could be slightly different, as the number of investors is based on the Form I-526 submissions under the purview of the regional center, as DHS did not calculate the total based on the adjustment applicable to Form I-829 filings associated with the regional center discussed in the preamble. Given the data constraints discussed thus far, for robustness we will assess the entities' small entity status along three different methodological approaches. While DHS has the listed NAICS codes for the 198 classifiable entities, DHS extensively reviewed various NAICS codes and determined that the 6-digit, detailed industry NAICS code 522310, Mortgage and Nonmortgage Loan Brokers, defined as an “industry [that] comprises establishments primarily engaged in arranging loans by bringing borrowers and lenders together on a commission or fee basis,” is an appropriate NAICS code under which regional centers operate.[77]
国土安全部的意思是,虽然数据中提供的NAICS代码通常适用于区域中心借贷所针对的下游项目类型,但区域中心本身通常并不直接参与这些活动,而是从事将投资者资金整合为贷款的工作。所选NAICS类别的2022年美国小企业管理局(SBA)规模标准基于1,500万美元的收入。[78] 在为可分类行业提供的实际NAICS代码中,半数归入三位数子行业523“证券、商品合约及其他金融投资及相关活动”下的若干六位数代码。数据提供方在“业务描述”栏目中将这些实体描述为“投资服务”,而NAICS子行业523中的所有个别行业均适用4,700万美元的规模标准。两个规模标准(1,500万美元和4,700万美元)之间差异较大,因此为确保稳健性,我们将按照各自的金额分别评估全部实体样本。国土安全部还根据数据中列出的实际NAICS代码对受限样本进行了评估。结果列于表19。
By this DHS means that while the NAICS code provided in the data often applies to the types of downstream projects that the regional centers gear loans toward, the regional center is usually not involved directly in those activities and is rather involved in bundling the investors' funds into loans. The year 2022 SBA size standard for the NAICS category chosen is based on revenue of $15.0 million.[78] Of the actual NAICS codes provided for classifiable industries, half accrued to several 6-digit codes under the 3-digit subsector 523, “Securities, Commodity Contracts, and Other Financial Investments and Related Activities.” The data providers describe these entities as “investment services” in the “business description” tab and all the individual industries in NAICS subsector 523 ensconce a size standard of $47.0 million. The difference between the size standards ($15.0 million and $47.0 million) is large, and therefore for robustness we will evaluate the full sample of entities under each of the respective amounts. DHS also evaluates the restricted sample based on the actual NAICS code listed in the data. The results are presented in Table 19.
(印刷页第61979页)
( printed page 61979)
从表19可以看出，限制样本组的中位数和平均值均小于完整样本组的相应数值。正如预期的那样，在一般投资服务项下采用较高的4,700万美元规模标准时，属于小型实体的区域中心所占百分比更大。然而，即使在较低的规模标准下，绝大多数区域中心仍属于小型实体。基于这些数据，美国国土安全部（DHS）可以确定，在《管制弹性法案》（RFA）的背景下，EB-5区域中心中有多数，至少87.1%，属于小型实体。[79]
As can be seen from Table 19 the median and means for the restricted sample-group are smaller than that for the full sample-group. As would be expected, the percentage of regional centers that are small is larger at the higher size standard of $47 million under general investment services. However, still the large majority is small at the lower size standard. Based on these data, DHS can determine that a majority—at a minimum, 87.1 percent—of EB-5 regional centers are small entities in the context of the RFA.[79]
然而，上述确定存在两个重要的限制条件，二者综合起来可能会对属于小型实体的区域中心的数量和百分比产生净减少或净增加的影响。如前所述，本确定未考虑区域中心可能收取的贷款利息收入或最终用户所产生的利润收入，因为DHS没有足够的数据支持对此类收入进行分析。此类贷款差额或利润收入可能相当可观，并可能降低真实的小型实体占比。但本分析的另一项局限性，即关于投资和管理费收取时间的局限性，可能产生相反的影响。实际上，管理费无需在一年内全部收取完毕，因为投资和费用可以分多年收取。然而，DHS将所有区域中心的收入都归并到一年内核算。鉴于本分析所查询的数据结构，试图将与管理费相关的收入流纳入时间维度将极为困难。DHS无法对本规则对区域中心小型实体可能产生的影响进行分布性分析。具体而言，对于173个已确定为小型实体且已匹配到收入数据的实体，从概念上讲，可以将本规则所产生的影响在每个实体的收入中进行划分，从而得出该影响占收入的百分比。DHS估计，按7%的折现率计算，可能累积给EB-5实体的影响（即申请费和表格填写时间负担的增加）每年约为300万美元。
There are two important caveats to the determination made above; however, which taken together could have a net effect of reducing or increasing the number and percentage of regional centers that are small entities. As was noted earlier, this determination did not consider income accruing to interest income on loans or end-user derived profit that regional centers could collect, as DHS does not have sufficient data to support an analysis concerning such income. Such loan differential or profit income could be substantial and could reduce the true small entity share. But a limitation of this analysis that could have a countervailing effect owes to the timing of investments and administrative fees. In practice the administrative fees need not be collected in one year, as investments and fees could be collected over multiple years. However, DHS abridged all the regional center income to one year. It would be extremely difficult given the data structures we queried for this analysis to attempt to incorporate a time dimension to the income stream as it pertains to administrative fees. DHS is unable to conduct a distributional analysis of the potential impacts to small entities of regional centers. Specifically, for the set of 173-found small entities with matched revenue data, it is conceptually possible to divide into the income for each entity the impacts from the rule, to derive a percentage of income the impact could embody. DHS estimates that a seven percent rate of discount, the impacts that could accrue to EB-5 entities ( i.e., filing fees and increases in form time burdens) could be about $3 million annually.
在实践中，实际成本会更高，但DHS无法对此进行估算。然而，我们没有办法将已量化的成本分摊到各区域中心，因此无法确定它们将受到何种影响。就区域中心而言，适用于I-956表格（初始申请和修正申请）的费用变化可以按实体收入进行分摊，尽管这将依赖于一个较为牵强的假设，即初始申请与修正申请是在同一年提交的。然而，这仅构成部分影响，因为DHS并不知晓与区域中心活动相关的其他表格的填报活动将如何影响该商业实体。其他表格将由个人提交，我们不清楚其中部分影响是否会由区域中心承担、转移给区域中心，或转嫁给其他实体。因此，DHS无法确定对属于小型实体的区域中心将产生何种影响。
In practice, the costs would be higher, but DHS cannot estimate costs. However, we have no way of distributing the quantified costs across regional centers and therefore cannot determine how they will be impacted. As it relates to regional centers, the fee changes applicable to the Form I-956 (initial and amendment) could be divided against entity income—although this would rest on the tenuous assumption that the initial and amendment filing were in the same year. However, this would constitute only a partial impact because DHS does not know how activity related to the other forms applicable to regional center activity would impact the business entity. The other forms would be filed by individuals, and we do not know if some of the impacts would be borne by the regional center, transferred to them, or passed through to other entities. As a result, DHS cannot determine what the impact to small entity regional centers would be.
二、其他EB-5企业
ii. Other EB-5 Businesses
对于参与投资活动的非区域中心企业，DHS出于必要，采用了一种非常规的多步骤方法进行小型实体分析。首先，DHS能够从EB-5项目内部数据和跟踪数据库中获取2018财年至2022财年期间获批的约5,000个独特的新商业企业（NCE）名称和约3,000个工作创造实体（JCE）名称。 （印刷页第61980页） 这些实体被汇总并随机打乱，以便在基于订阅的开源商业信息提供商处对其中的400个进行来源确认和检索，力图满足95%的置信水平。[80] 检索结果中只有111项结果能够被合理验证为匹配项。其中一个挑战在于，DHS记录中的实体名称与其他来源中的名称在句法上可能难以匹配。数据提供商依赖匹配查询以获得精确匹配的结果，但由于这些提供商与DHS记录系统中的企业名称可能存在细微的句法差异，因此很可能无法产生匹配结果。[81]
For nonregional center businesses involved in investment activity, DHS employed out of necessity an unconventional, multi-step approach to ( printed page 61980) the small entity analysis. First, DHS was able to obtain about 5,000 unique NCE names and about 3,000 JCE names that were approved between FY 2018 through FY 2022 from the internal EB-5 program data and tracking databases. These entities were pooled and randomly scrambled to source and to run searches in the subscription-based, open-source business information providers on 400 of them, to attempt to satisfy a 95-percent level of confidence.[80] The searches yielded only 111 results that could reasonably be validated as matches. One of the challenges is that it can be difficult to match syntax in the entity names between DHS records and that in the other sources. The data providers relied upon match queries to results with close-fitting precision, but because there can be minor syntax differences in the names of the businesses in these providers and DHS record systems, there is a strong likelihood a match would not result.[81]
除了匹配率较低之外,还遇到了另外两项挑战。首先,国土安全部(DHS)面临着与我们在评估区域中心时相同的问题;超过三分之一的实体(42个,占37.8%)无法分类,因此无法按照美国小企业管理局(SBA)规模标准对其身份进行评估。其次,在可分类的企业中,几乎五分之一(13个,占18.8%)缺少北美行业分类系统(NAICS)代码或营收数据,或两者均缺失。这些限制因素使样本量缩减至仅56个实体。
In addition to the low match-rate, two additional challenges were encountered. First, DHS faced the same issue as we did for regional centers; over one-third of the entities (42, or 37.8 percent) were non-classifiable and therefore incompatible to evaluate against an SBA size standard for status. Second, of the classifiable businesses, almost one-fifth (13, or 18.8 percent) were missing either or both of a NAICS code or a revenue figure. These constraints rendered the sample size down to a mere 56 entities.
鉴于上述所阐明的挑战,国土安全部采用了一种非常规的第二步方法。国土安全部单独针对“EB5”一词的各种“变体”进行了查询,共获得885条检索结果。我们开展了一项筛选程序,首先剔除了数据缺失的记录(销售收入或NAICS代码缺失其一或两者均缺失),并剔除了无法分类的机构。随后,国土安全部通过剔除包含“regional”与“center”(“区域”与“中心”)两词联合出现的检索结果,预先排除了可能属于区域中心的条目。接着,国土安全部进一步加强了该筛选程序,剔除了那些已被纳入本信息征询(RFA)上述模块中区域中心样本的区域中心名称,或此前已获批但目前不再活跃的区域中心名称。最后,国土安全部对剩余的每一个实体进行人工评估,并剔除了那些合理判断为并非直接从事该计划投资活动的企业。这些附属活动主要包括为该计划相关企业提供服务的律师事务所、商业顾问机构或分析咨询机构,但这些机构本身被认为并未直接从事该计划的投资活动。筛选方案汇总于表20,其中展示了逐步筛选的方法。将表中所示的两个小计相加,我们得到了一个可用样本量为489,这足以满足95%的置信水平要求。
Given the challenges elucidated above, DHS employed an unconventional second-step approach. DHS ran queries against “variations” of the term “EB5” separately, which yielded 885 returns. We engaged a filtering process that first removed records with missing data (either or both of sales revenue or NAICS codes) and removed non-classifiable establishments. DHS then backed out likely regional centers first by culling any results that contained the conjoined terms “regional” and “center.” DHS next bolstered this filtering process by further eliminating any regional center names either captured in our sample of regional centers, from that above module of this RFA, or that were otherwise approved in the past but are not currently active. Finally, DHS manually appraised each remaining entity and removed those that reasonably appeared to be businesses not directly involved with program investment activity. These ancillary activities would primarily ensconce law firms, business advisories, or analytical consultancies that provide services to program businesses, but are themselves assumed to not be directly involved in the investment activity of the program. The filtering schema is summarized in Table 20, which shows the stepwise method. By adding the two subtotals shown, we obtain a viable sample of 489, which is more than sufficient to satisfy a confidence level of 95 percent.
(印刷页第61981页)
( printed page 61981)
如上所述,合资企业(JCE)和新商业企业(NCE)在第一步查询中被合并统计,而对于第二步查询所得出的433个额外实体,我们假定其中大部分或全部为合资企业和新商业企业,尽管国土安全部无法具体区分哪些是新商业企业,哪些是合资企业。最终,区分二者并不重要,因为与区域中心所采用的方法不同(该方法依赖多种评估手段,包括两次基于我们认为最契合的单一行业描述来推定NAICS代码),对于非区域中心企业,我们仅依据与所报告NAICS代码相对照的单一试验来确定NAICS代码。分析结果列于表21。
As was mentioned above, the JCEs and NCEs were pooled in the first-step query, and for the 433 additional entities resulting from the second-step query, we assume that most or all of them are JCEs and NCEs, though DHS cannot distinguish which are specifically NCEs and which are JCEs. It is ultimately unimportant to distinguish them, because, unlike the approach to regional centers in which we relied on several evaluation methods—including imputing a NAICS codes based (twice) on the single industry description we believe best fits—for the non-regional center businesses we based the NAICS codes solely on a single trial benchmarked to the reported NAICS code. The results of the analysis are captured in Table 21.
尽管收入范围极为悬殊,但仅有1个实体(即最大值)超过了适用的SBA规模标准,这实质上意味着100%的实体均属于小型实体。然而,正如区域中心的情形一样,我们并不知晓适用于这些企业的收入是否仅限于所报告的销售收入。如果它们从放贷活动或其他形式的利润回报中获得部分收入,结果可能会大不相同,届时可能并非所有实体均属于小型实体。
While there is an extreme range for the income, only 1 entity (the maximum) exceeded the applicable SBA size standard, which essentially means that 100 percent are small. However, as was the case with regional centers, we do not know if the income applicable to these businesses is limited to the reported sales revenue. If they receive some income from lending activity, or some other form of return in profits, the results could be quite different as potentially not all would be small entities.
国土安全部无法对小型实体所受的潜在影响进行分布分析。具体而言,对于具有匹配营收数据的488个小型实体而言,从概念上可以将收入逐一进行划分。这些总体影响构成转移支付和成本。就这些企业而言,适用于相关表格的费用变化将由提交申请的个人承担。国土安全部无法确定这些影响是否会以及如何影响相关企业,因此无法确定对小型实体的影响程度。
DHS is unable to conduct a distributional analysis of the potential impacts to small entities. Specifically, for the set of 488 small entities with matched revenue data, it is conceptual to divide into the income for each. These gross impacts constitute transfers and costs. As it relates to the businesses, the fee changes applicable to the forms would accrue to individuals filing the petitions. DHS cannot say if and how these impacts would impact the related businesses involved and hence cannot determine what the impact to small entities would be.
三、结语
iii. Concluding Remarks
美国国土安全部(DHS)为拟议规则证明有效性所依据的初步监管灵活性分析(IRFA),以及国土安全部为本最终行动所编制的最终监管灵活性分析(FRFA)均表明,参与EB-5项目投资活动的大多数实体,即至少87%的区域中心以及几乎所有其他直接参与的商业实体(根据我们的最佳评估,这将包括新商业企业(NCE)和合资企业(JCE)),都可能属于小实体。然而需要强调的是,这一判定是在信息不完整的情况下作出的,因为目前尚无足够数据可用于说明此类实体可能获得的某些类型的收入。为了对这一保留意见提供一定的背景说明,国土安全部对2018财年至2022财年期间通过合资企业(JCE)进行投资的1,402个EB-5项目进行了评估,这些项目可提取出关于所投资资本金额的有效数据。按顺序,项目专用资本的中位数、平均数和最大数额分别为700万美元、6,720万美元和110.7亿美元。略低于四分之一(22.2%)的项目混合使用了非项目资本。对于混合资本项目而言,按同样顺序的数字分别为5,220万美元、3.275亿美元和125.857亿美元。仅从这些数字的规模来看,可以合理推测,即使贷款金额或投资资本中只有一小部分被作为剩余收益支付,属于小实体的实体数量和比例也将低于我们分析中所得出的结果。例如,大型金融服务与咨询公司德勤(Deloitte)的研究发现,2021年投资的总体平均回报率约为6.1%。[83] 将该比率应用于上述混合资本投资的平均值和最大值,回报额可能介于650万美元至7.67亿美元之间。如果区域中心或其他企业获取了这部分潜在回报的全部或部分,那么属于小实体的比例几乎可以肯定会更低。
The IRFA that DHS certified to support the proposed rule, and the FRFA that DHS prepared for this final action, suggests that the majority—at least 87 percent of regional centers and essentially all other directly involved business entities (which to the best of our assessment would comprise NCEs and JCEs) involved in EB-5 program investment activity—could be small entities. However, it is emphasized that this determination is made on incomplete information, as sufficient data are not available on certain types of income that could accrue to such entities. To provide some context to this caveat, DHS evaluated 1,402 EB-5 projects in which an investment was conducted through a JCE between FY 2018 through FY 2022, for which viable data could be extracted on the amount of capital invested. The median, average, and maximum amount of program-specific capital was $7.0 million, $67.2 million, and $11,070.0 million, in order. A little less than a quarter (22.2 percent) blended nonprogram capital. For the blended capital projects, the figures, in order again, were $52.2 million, $327.5 million, and $12,585.7 million. From the size of these figures alone, it is reasonable to conjecture that if even a small portion of the loan amount or invested capital is renumerated as residual income, the number and share of entities that are small would be lower than that found in our analysis. For example, the large financial services and advisory company, Deloitte, found that the general average rate of return on investments in 2021 was about 6.1 percent.[83] Applied to the average and maximum blended capital investments above, the return could be between $6.5 million and $767 million. If some, or all, of this potential return were captured by regional centers or other businesses, the share that would be small would almost certainly stand to be lower.
本最终监管灵活性分析(FRFA)所作判定的第二个保留意见是,国土安全部采用了替代方法。因此,相关结论是基于仅部分随机的样本得出的。如前所述,原因在于随机抽样程序未能产生足够的样本规模,尽管没有理由认为非随机抽样部分存在报告或选择偏差,但也不能完全排除这种可能性。正如相关经济分析中所述,费用变更所产生的影响将体现为申请人向国土安全部的转移支付。与诚信基金(Integrity Fund)费用相关的潜在罚款(未作估算)将被视为因《EB-5改革法案》而产生的成本。正如本小实体分析第VI.B.2.C.i节所述,我们将诚信基金费用视为区域中心的收入,尽管 (印刷页第61982页) 这对区域中心而言是一项成本,但其理由在于区域中心可能会尝试将部分此类成本转嫁给投资者或其他企业。需要指出的是,从复式记账的角度来看,一笔被指定用于支付成本的收入流可被视为净值为零的交易。但在《监管灵活性法案》(RFA)的适用范围内,该收入流仍将被视为对适用的美国小企业管理局(SBA)规模标准的收入抵免(行政费用的情况即是如此,区域中心为所提供的服务支付费用,但随后将其全部或部分转嫁给投资者,因此该费用构成收入)。
A second caveat to the determinations made in this FRFA is that DHS relied on alternative methodologies. As such, the findings are based on samples that are only partially random. The reason, it is recalled, is that the randomized procedures did not yield sufficient sample sizes, and while there is no reason to assume that there is any reporting or selection bias in the nonrandom-sampled portions, it cannot be completely ruled out either. As described in the associated economic analysis, the impacts of the fee changes would accrue to transfers from requestors to DHS. The potential penalties associated with the Integrity Fund fees, which are not estimated, would be accounted for as costs due to the EB-5 Reform Act. As was noted in Section VI.B.2.C.i of this small entity analysis, we treated Integrity Fund fees as income to regional centers, even ( printed page 61982) though it is a cost to them, on grounds that they may attempt to pass some of those costs through to investors or other businesses. It is noted here that from a double-entry accounting perspective, an income flow earmarked to a cost could be considered a net zero-value transaction. But under the RFA purview, the flow would still be considered an income credit against the applicable SBA size standard (this is the case with the administrative fees—the regional center pays for the services embodied but then passes all or some of it to investors, and it is therefore income).
因此,区域中心或其他企业因本次变更而产生并转嫁或转移给其他实体的任何此类成本和转移支付,也可能影响对EB-5企业小实体的判定。例如,我们虽无证据表明,但也不能排除,对于某些实体而言,适用的费用变更幅度可能足够大,以致可能被转嫁给投资者或其他实体。
Therefore, any such costs and transfers that regional centers or other businesses would incur from the changes that are transferred or passed through to other entities could also affect the small entity determinations for EB-5 businesses. For example, we have no evidence to suggest, but cannot rule out, that for some entities the applicable fee changes might be large enough that they might be passed to investors or other entities.
e. 对本规则预计产生的报告、记录保存及其他合规要求的说明,包括对将受该要求约束的小实体类别的估计,以及编制报告或记录所需的专业技能类型。
e. A description of the projected reporting, recordkeeping, and other compliance requirements of the rule, including an estimate of the classes of small entities which will be subject to the requirement and the type of professional skills necessary for preparation of the report or record.
国土安全部认为,本最终规则不会对参与EB-5项目的个人和企业施加额外的报告、记录保存及其他合规要求。虽然可能对小实体产生影响,但这些影响不太可能涉及需要专业技能的报告工作。
DHS does not believe that this final rule will impose additional reporting, recordkeeping, and other compliance requirements on individuals and businesses participating in the EB-5 program. While there may be impacts to small entities, these are not likely to involve reporting that requires professional skills.
f. 该机构为将对小实体造成的重大经济影响降至最低程度所采取的措施说明，须符合相关制定法规所述目标，包括说明选择最终规则中所采用方案的事实、政策和法律理由，以及该机构所考虑的其他影响小实体的各项重大替代方案被否决的理由。
f. A description of the steps the agency has taken to minimize the significant economic impact on small entities consistent with the stated objectives of applicable statutes, including a statement of the factual, policy, and legal reasons for selecting the alternative adopted in the final rule and why each of the other significant alternatives to the rule considered by the agency which affect the impact on small entities was rejected.
各项费用是通过一项专门的费用研究确定的。其费用水平将：使美国国土安全部（DHS）能够收回管理EB-5项目的成本；实现《EB-5改革法》规定的EB-5处理时限目标；以及对美国国土安全部用于管理EB-5项目的信息技术系统进行改进。
The fees were determined via a specific fee study. Their level would: enable DHS to recover the costs of administering the EB-5 program; meet the EB-5 processing time goals as provided in the EB-5 Reform Act, and; make improvements to the information technology systems used by DHS to administer the EB-5 program.
由于费用结构直接源自《EB-5改革法》所要求进行的成本研究，美国国土安全部曾考虑但未采纳为小企业设定较低费用的方案，原因在于美国公民及移民服务局（USCIS）几乎完全依赖用户收费维持运作。此外，收取较低费用（包括根据企业规模制定不同费用）可能会影响处理时限，这可能与《EB-5改革法》所规定的处理时限目标相抵触。此外，鉴于本监管灵活性分析（RFA）中前述关于抽样程序、数据和信息完整性以及不可分类实体等方面所存在的挑战，美国国土安全部若要为向小企业与大企业收取不同费用而制定一个便于操作的“小企业”定义，将十分困难，且可能带有主观性。因此，若要提出既能实现《EB-5改革法》目标、又能继续为管理EB-5项目提供充足资金，同时减轻小实体负担的替代方案，例如：（1）针对小实体的资源情况制定不同的合规或报告要求或时间表；（2）对适用于此类小实体的规则下的合规和报告要求进行澄清、合并或简化；（3）采用绩效标准而非设计标准；以及（4）对此类小实体豁免适用该规则或其部分内容，将具有挑战性且带有主观性。美国国土安全部曾向公众征求关于减轻小实体负担的替代方案或方式的建议，并就迟缴诚信基金罚款费用的百分比考虑了从零到更高数额的广泛范围。
Because the fee structure is derived directly from the cost-study, which is mandated by the EB-5 Reform Act, DHS considered, but did not adopt, reduced fees for small businesses, because USCIS is almost entirely dependent on user fees. Moreover, charging less in fees (including different fees for businesses based on size) could potentially impact processing times, which could stand in contrast to process time goals outlined in the EB-5 Reform Act. Additionally, given the challenges in this RFA described above, applicable to the sampling procedures, data and information completeness, and unclassifiable entities, it would be very difficult and probably subjective for DHS to come up with an easily administrable definition of “small business” for the purpose of charging lower fees for small businesses vs. larger businesses. As a result, it would be challenging and subjective to attempt to present alternatives that could achieve the objectives of the EB-5 Reform Act, continue to adequately fund the cost of administering the EB-5 program, and reduce burdens on small entities such as: (1) The establishment of differing compliance or reporting requirements or timetables that take into account the resources available to small entities; (2) The clarification, consolidation, or simplification of compliance and reporting requirements under the rule for such small entities; (3) The use of performance rather than design standards; and (4) An exemption from coverage of the rule, or any part thereof, for such small entities. DHS solicited suggestions from the public on alternatives or ways in which small entities' burdens could be reduced and considered a wide range of percentages for the late Integrity Fund fee penalties from zero to a higher amount.
C. 1995年《无资金授权改革法》（UMRA）
C. Unfunded Mandates Reform Act of 1995 (UMRA)
1995年《无资金授权改革法》（UMRA）旨在遏制向州、地方和部落政府施加无资金联邦授权的做法等事项。[84] UMRA第二章要求每个联邦机构就拟议规则或美国公民及移民服务局已就其发布拟议规则的最终规则中任何联邦授权的影响编写书面声明，其中包括任何可能导致州、地方和部落政府合计或私营部门在任何一年内支出1亿美元或以上（每年根据通货膨胀进行调整）的联邦授权。 参见2 U.S.C. 1532(a)。
The Unfunded Mandates Reform Act of 1995 (UMRA) is intended, among other things, to curb the practice of imposing unfunded federal mandates on State, local, and Tribal governments.[84] Title II of UMRA requires each Federal agency to prepare a written statement assessing the effects of any Federal mandate in a proposed rule, or final rule for which USCIS published a proposed rule, which includes any Federal mandate that may result in a $100 million or more expenditure (adjusted annually for inflation) in any one year by State, local, and Tribal governments, in the aggregate, or by the private sector. See2 U.S.C. 1532(a).
本规则在经通货膨胀调整后（根据所有城市消费者消费价格指数（CPI-U）[85]计算，2025年为2.11亿美元）并未超过任何一年1亿美元的支出限额，且本规则制定并不包含UMRA所定义的此类联邦授权。因此，UMRA第二章的要求不适用，故美国国土安全部未依据UMRA编写声明。
This rule does not exceed the $100 million expenditure in any one year when adjusted for inflation ($211 million in 2025 based on the Consumer Price Index for All Urban Consumers (CPI-U) [85] ), and this rulemaking does not contain such a federal mandate as the term is defined under UMRA. The requirements of title II of UMRA therefore do not apply, thus DHS has not prepared a statement under UMRA.
D. 《国会审查法》（CRA）
D. Congressional Review Act (CRA)
《国会审查法》（CRA）作为第E小节纳入1996年《小企业监管执行公平法》（SBREFA），公共法律104-121号，110 Stat. 847, 868页及其后各页。 根据美国国土安全部（DHS）的评估，信息与监管事务办公室已确定本最终规则不属于《国会审查法》5 U.S.C. 804(2)所定义的重大规则。本规则不会导致每年对经济产生1亿美元或以上的影响；不会导致成本或价格的重大增加；也不会对竞争、就业、投资、生产力、创新或美国本土企业与外国企业在国内及出口市场竞争的能力产生重大不利影响。 参见5 U.S.C. 804(2)。因此，国土安全部可以不适用《国会审查法》规定的60天延迟生效要求。然而，出于实施目的，国土安全部在本最终规则中仍设定了60天的延迟生效期。国土安全部已遵守《国会审查法》的报告要求，并已按照5 U.S.C. 801(a)(1)的规定将本规则提交国会及审计长。
The Congressional Review Act (CRA) was included as Subsection E of the Small Business Regulatory Enforcement Fairness Act of 1996 (SBREFA), Public Law 104-121, 110 Stat. 847, 868, et seq. Based on DHS's assessment, the Office of Information and Regulatory Affairs has determined that this final rule is not a major rule as defined under the CRA in 5 U.S.C. 804(2). This rule will not result in an annual effect on the economy of $100 million or more; a major increase in costs or prices; or significant adverse effects on competition, employment, investment, productivity, innovation, or an ability of the United States-based companies to compete with foreign-based companies in domestic and export markets. See5 U.S.C. 804(2). Therefore, DHS may forgo the CRA's 60-day delayed effective date requirement. However, DHS for implementation purposes has provided for a 60-day delayed effective date with this final rule. DHS has complied with the CRA's reporting requirements and has sent this rule to Congress and to the Comptroller General as required by 5 U.S.C. 801(a)(1).
E. 第13132号行政命令（联邦制）
E. Executive Order 13132 (Federalism)
本最终规则不会对各州、国家政府与各州之间的关系，或各级政府之间权力与责任的分配产生实质性的直接影响。因此，根据第13132号行政命令第6节的规定，兹确定本 （印刷页第61983页） 最终规则不具有足以需要编制联邦制影响摘要声明的联邦制影响。
This final rule will not have substantial direct effects on the States, on the relationship between the National Government and the States, or on the distribution of power and responsibilities among the various levels of government. Therefore, in accordance with section 6 of Executive Order 13132, it is determined that this ( printed page 61983) final rule does not have sufficient federalism implications to warrant the preparation of a federalism summary impact statement.
F. 第12988号行政命令（民事司法改革）
F. Executive Order 12988 (Civil Justice Reform)
本最终规则符合第12988号行政命令第3(a)节和第3(b)(2)节所规定的适用标准。
This final rule meets the applicable standards set forth in sections 3(a) and 3(b)(2) of Executive Order 12988.
G. 家庭评估
G. Family Assessment
国土安全部已依据1999年《财政部总拨款法》第654节（公共法律105-277号，A分部）对本最终行动进行了评估。据此，国土安全部已依照第654(c)(1)节所规定的标准对本行动进行了评估。
DHS has assessed this final action in accordance with section 654 of the Treasury General Appropriations Act, 1999, Public Law 105-277, Div. A. Accordingly, DHS has assessed this action in accordance with the criteria specified by section 654(c)(1).
国土安全部没有数据表明本最终规则会对包括美国公民子女在内的某些家庭和儿童的可支配收入或贫困状况产生任何影响。国土安全部承认，本规则将提高某些EB-5项目投资者（及其家庭）必须缴纳的费用，因此可能影响某些家庭的可支配收入。然而，本最终规则将为美国公民及移民服务局（USCIS）提供资金，用于管理EB-5投资者项目、满足法定处理时限，并资助USCIS为受虐待儿童和配偶、难民、犯罪活动或人口贩运受害者及其他群体提供的免费和减费服务。国土安全部被要求管理EB-5项目，并被授权设定和收取费用，除通过收费产生的收入外，不获得任何其他资金用于此目的。虽然这些费用可能对选择成为EB-5项目投资者的家庭产生财务影响，但国土安全部除本规则制定外别无其他替代方案。国土安全部还确定，本最终规则不会对家庭作为一种制度的自主性或完整性产生任何影响。
DHS has no data that indicate that this final rule will have any impacts on disposable income or the poverty of certain families and children, including U.S. citizen children. DHS acknowledges that this rule will increase the fees that some EB-5 program investors' (and thus their families) must submit and thus it may affect the disposable income for certain families. However, the final rule will provide USCIS with funds that will be used to administer the EB-5 investor program, meet the statutory processing times, and fund free and reduced fee services USCIS provides to abused children and spouses, refugees, victims of criminal activity or human trafficking, and other populations. DHS is required to administer the EB-5 program, is authorized to set and collect fees, and receives no funding to do so aside from the revenue generated by charging fees. While those fees could have a financial impact on a family that chooses to become an investor in an EB-5 program project, DHS has no alternatives other than this rulemaking. DHS also determined that this final rule will not have any impact on the autonomy or integrity of the family as an institution.
H. 第13175号行政命令（与印第安部落政府的协商与协调）
H. Executive Order 13175 (Consultation and Coordination With Indian Tribal Governments)
根据第13175号行政命令《与印第安部落政府的协商与协调》，本最终规则不具有部落影响，因为它不会对一个或多个印第安部落、联邦政府与印第安部落之间的关系，或联邦政府与印第安部落之间权力与责任的分配产生实质性直接影响。
This final rule would not have Tribal implications under Executive Order 13175, Consultation and Coordination with Indian Tribal Governments, because it would not have a substantial direct effect on one or more Indian Tribes, on the relationship between the Federal Government and Indian Tribes, or on the distribution of power and responsibilities between the Federal Government and Indian Tribes.
I. 《国家环境政策法》（NEPA）
I. National Environmental Policy Act (NEPA)
国土安全部及其下属部门分析最终行动，以确定《国家环境政策法》（NEPA）（公共法律91-190号，83 Stat. 852（1970年），编入42 U.S.C. 4321-4347）是否适用于这些行动，如适用，则确定所需的分析程度。国土安全部《国家环境政策法实施》第023-01号指令第01版修订版（第023-01号指令）[86] 以及《第023-01-001-01号实施手册第01版修订版：国家环境政策法的实施》（实施手册）[87] 规定了国土安全部及其下属部门用以遵守NEPA及环境质量委员会（CEQ）实施NEPA条例（40 CFR第1500部分至第1508部分）的程序。
DHS and its components analyze final actions to determine whether the National Environmental Policy Act (NEPA), Public Law 91-190, 83 Stat. 852 (1970) (codified at 42 U.S.C. 4321-4347), applies to them and, if so, what degree of analysis is required. DHS “Implementation of the National Environmental Policy Act”, Directive 023-01, Revision 01 (Directive 023-01) [86] and “Instruction Manual 023-01-001-01 Revision 01, Implementation of the National Environmental Policy Act” (Instruction Manual) [87] established the procedures that DHS and its components use to comply with NEPA and the Council on Environmental Quality (CEQ) regulations for implementing NEPA, 40 CFR parts 1500 through 1508.
CEQ(环境质量委员会)条例允许联邦机构在经CEQ审查和同意后,确定若干类别的行动(“分类排除”),根据经验表明,这些行动单独或累积均不会对人类环境产生重大影响,因此不需要编制环境评估(EA)或环境影响报告书(EIS)。40 CFR 1501.4、1507.3(e)(2)(ii)、1508.1(d)。
The CEQ regulations allow Federal agencies to establish, with CEQ review and concurrence, categories of actions (“categorical exclusions”) which experience has shown do not individually or cumulatively have a significant effect on the human environment and, therefore, do not require an Environmental Assessment (EA) or Environmental Impact Statement (EIS). 40 CFR 1501.4, 1507.3(e)(2)(ii), 1508.1(d).
《操作手册》附录A表1列出了美国国土安全部(DHS)认定不具有此类影响的各类分类排除。根据DHS的NEPA实施程序,一项行动若要被归入分类排除,必须同时满足以下三个条件:(1)该行动整体明确符合一项或多项分类排除;(2)该行动不是更大行动的一部分;以及(3)不存在可能产生重大环境影响的特殊情况。[88]
The Instruction Manual, Appendix A, Table 1 lists Categorical Exclusions that DHS has found to have no such effect. Under DHS NEPA implementing procedures, for an action to be categorically excluded, it must satisfy each of the following three conditions: (1) The entire action clearly fits within one or more of the categorical exclusions; (2) the action is not a piece of a larger action; and (3) no extraordinary circumstances exist that create the potential for a significant environmental effect.[88]
本最终规则仅限于修订DHS关于EB-5项目及其费用的相关法规。因此,DHS经审查认为,本规则所颁布的修订不会对环境造成重大影响,也不会导致环境影响发生任何变化。
This final rule is limited to amending DHS regulations governing the EB-5 program and its fees. As such, DHS has reviewed this final rule and finds that no significant impact on the environment, or any change in environmental effect will result from the amendments being promulgated in this rule.
因此,DHS认定,本最终规则对现行法规所作的修订,明确符合DHS在其NEPA实施程序中确立的分类排除A3,即对现有法规进行行政性修改且不改变环境影响的情形,不属于更大联邦行动的一部分,也不存在可能产生重大环境影响的特殊情况。因此,该等监管修订被归入分类排除范围,无需作进一步的NEPA审查。
Accordingly, DHS finds that the promulgation of this final rule's amendments to current regulations clearly fits within categorical exclusion A3 established in DHS's NEPA implementing procedures as an administrative change amending an existing regulation with no change in environmental effect, is not part of a larger Federal action, and does not present extraordinary circumstances that create the potential for a significant environmental effect. Therefore, the regulatory amendments are categorically excluded from further NEPA review.
J. 文书削减法(PRA)
J. Paperwork Reduction Act (PRA)
根据《文书削减法》(PRA),44 U.S.C. 3501-3512,DHS必须将规则中固有的任何报告要求提交管理和预算办公室(OMB)审查批准,除非该等要求属于豁免范围。DHS于2025年10月23日在《联邦公报》90 FR 48516上发布了一项拟议规则制定通知,在该通知中就与本规则制定相关的受影响信息收集征求了公众意见。DHS已在本最终规则第IV节中对这些意见作出回应。下表21列出了属于本最终规则一部分的信息收集情况摘要。
Under the PRA, 44 U.S.C. 3501-3512, DHS must submit to OMB, for review and approval, any reporting requirements inherent in a rule unless they are exempt. DHS published a notice of proposed rulemaking at 90 FR 48516 on October 23, 2025, in which comments on the impacted information collections associated with this rulemaking were requested. DHS responded to those comments in Section IV. of this final rule. Table 21 below lists the summary of information collections that are part of this final rule.

( 1) 指已投资或正积极投资于某区域中心新商业企业(该企业已根据该法第203(b)(5)条获得分类或正在申请分类)的个人数量,减去已根据该法第216A条就该投资提交解除条件申请的此类个人数量。

(ii) Penalties for Failure to Submit Regional Center Integrity Fee. (A) If a regional center does not pay the fee on or before October 31 of each year and instead pays the fee from November 1 until the end of the day on November 30, a monetary penalty equal to 10 percent of the required fee will be imposed on the regional center.
(B) 如果区域中心未能在11月30日或之前缴纳费用，而是在12月1日至12月30日当天结束前缴纳费用，该区域中心将被处以相当于应缴费用20%的罚款。
(B) If a regional center does not pay the fee on or before November 30 and instead pays the fee from December 1 until the end of the day on December 30, a monetary penalty equal to 20 percent of the required fee will be imposed on the regional center.
(C) 如果区域中心未能在12月30日或之前缴纳费用及任何适用的罚款，美国公民及移民服务局将终止该区域中心的designation（指定资格）。
(C) If a regional center does not pay the fee plus any applicable penalty on or before December 30, USCIS will terminate the designation of such regional center.
(1) 在终止之前，美国公民及移民服务局将发出终止意向通知，并给予区域中心机会证明其已在12月30日或之前以正确金额缴纳了费用及适用的滞纳金。
( 1) Prior to termination, USCIS will send a notice of intent to terminate and provide the opportunity for a regional center to prove that the fee and applicable late fees were paid in the proper amount on or before December 30.
(2) 根据本条(d)(10)(ii)(C)款终止区域中心的决定，可依照8 CFR 103.3规定提出上诉。
( 2) Termination of a regional center under paragraph (d)(10)(ii)(C) of this section may be appealed as provided by 8 CFR 103.3.

第216部分 合法永久居民身份的附条件基础
PART 216—CONDITIONAL BASIS OF LAWFUL PERMANENT RESIDENCE STATUS

4. Amend § 216.6 by revising paragraph (a)(1)(ii) to read as follows:
(a) * * *
(a) * * *
(1) * * *
(1) * * *
(ii) 投资人可以将其配偶及子女列入解除条件申请中，前提是该配偶及子女是基于与投资人的关系而获得附条件永久居民身份的。如果投资人的配偶及子女未被列入投资人的解除条件申请中，除非投资人已去世，否则该配偶及每名子女必须各自提交解除其永久居民身份条件的申请。任何未被列入投资人解除条件申请中的配偶或子女，均可在投资人被要求提交解除条件申请的期间内的任何时候，提交解除其居民身份条件的申请。
(ii) An investor may include their spouse and children on a petition to remove conditions if the spouse and children obtained conditional permanent resident status based on their relationship to the investor. If the investor's spouse and children are not included on the investor's petition to remove conditions, the spouse and each child must each file their own petition to remove the conditions on their permanent resident status, unless the investor is deceased. Any spouse or child not included on the investor's petition to remove conditions may file a petition to remove the conditions on their residence at any time during the period when the investor is required to file a petition to remove conditions.
(A) 如果投资人已去世，配偶及子女可以分别提交申请，也可以合并列入一份申请中。无论哪种情况，配偶及子女均必须在投资人原本应被要求提交解除条件申请的期间内的任何时候提交申请，并证明其符合解除条件的资格。
(A) If the investor is deceased, the spouse and children may file separate petitions or may be included in one petition. In either case, the spouse and child must file the petition(s) at any time during the period when the investor would have been required to file a petition to remove conditions and establish eligibility to remove conditions.
(B) 投资人可以将在附条件永久居民身份期间年满21周岁或已结婚的子女列入其解除条件申请中。如果投资人未将该子女列入其解除条件申请中，该子女必须自行提交解除条件的申请。
(B) An investor may include any child who turned 21 years of age or married during the period of conditional permanent resident status on their petition to remove conditions. If the investor does not include the child on their petition to remove conditions, the child must file their own petition to remove conditions.
(C) 投资人可以将在附条件永久居民身份期间与投资人离婚的前配偶列入其解除条件申请中。如果投资人未将该前配偶列入其解除条件申请中，该前配偶必须自行提交解除条件的申请。
(C) An investor may include a former spouse who was divorced from the investor during the period of conditional permanent resident status on their petition to remove conditions. If the investor does not include the former spouse on their petition to remove conditions, the former spouse must file their own petition to remove conditions.
(D) 如果投资人未提交解除条件申请，任何基于与投资人关系而获得附条件永久居民身份的配偶、前配偶或子女，若能证明其符合解除条件的资格，均可解除其身份上的条件。该配偶、前配偶或子女必须在投资人原本应被要求提交解除条件申请的期间内的任何时候提交申请。
(D) If an investor does not file a petition to remove conditions, any spouse, former spouse, or child that obtained conditional permanent resident status based on their relationship to the investor may remove the conditions on their status if they can establish eligibility to remove conditions. The spouse, former spouse, or child must file the petition(s) at any time during the period when the investor would have been required to file a petition to remove conditions.
* * * * *
* * * * *
马克韦恩·穆林，
Markwayne Mullin,
美国国土安全部（DHS）部长。
Secretary, U.S. Department of Homeland Security.

Barnhart v. Peabody Coal Co., 537 U.S. 149, 159 (2003) (quoting United States v. James Daniel Good Real Prop., 510 U.S. 43, 63 (1993)).
