8. 针对为H-1B、H-2B和L-1签证类别非移民工人申请的某些雇主收取的防欺诈和侦查费是由法律规定的。所得收入用于与预防和侦查移民福利申请中的欺诈行为有关的活动。 参见8 U.S.C. 1356(v)(2)(B)(“存入防欺诈和侦查账户的款项中有三分之一应继续供国土安全部部长使用,直至用罄,用于预防和侦查移民福利欺诈的方案和活动,包括与根据本篇第1184(c)条第(1)款或第(2)(A)款提交的、旨在给予外国人本篇第1101(a)(15)条(H)项或(L)项所述非移民身份的申请有关的欺诈。”)。所得收入由USCIS、美国国务院和美国劳工部(DOL)平均分配。自2018年7月25日起,USCIS还收取并留存50美元的北马里亚纳群岛联邦欺诈费。 参见48 U.S.C. 1806(a)(6)(A)(iv)。国土安全部将防欺诈和侦查账户的授权解释为提供补充资金,以支付与欺诈预防和侦查有关的活动费用,而非规定只能将这些资金用于该目的。欺诈侦查与国家安全局(FDNS)由移民审查费账户(IEFA)和防欺诈和侦查账户共同提供资金。存入防欺诈和侦查账户的费用由法律固定,不足以支付FDNS的全部成本。因此,USCIS同时使用防欺诈和侦查账户资金和IEFA资金来支付FDNS的费用。
8.  The Fraud Prevention and Detection fees charged to certain employers petitioning for nonimmigrant workers in the H-1B, H-2B, and L-1 visa classifications are set by statute. Revenue is used for activities related to preventing and detecting fraud in immigration benefit requests. See 8 U.S.C. 1356(v)(2)(B) (“One-third of the amounts deposited into the Fraud Prevention and Detection Account shall remain available to the Secretary of Homeland Security until expended for programs and activities to prevent and detect immigration benefit fraud, including fraud with respect to petitions filed under paragraph (1) or (2)(A) of section 1184(c) of this title to grant an alien nonimmigrant status described in subparagraph (H) or (L) of section 1101(a)(15) of this title.”). Revenue is shared equally among USCIS, the U.S. Department of State, and the U.S. Department of Labor (DOL). Effective July 25, 2018, USCIS also collects and retains the $50 Commonwealth of the Northern Mariana Islands fraud fee. See 48 U.S.C. 1806(a)(6)(A)(iv). DHS interprets Fraud Prevention and Detection Account authority as providing supplemental funding to cover activities related to fraud prevention and detection and not prescribing that only those funds may be used for that purpose. The Fraud Detection and National Security Directorate (FDNS) is funded out of both the IEFA and the Fraud Prevention and Detection Account. The fees deposited in the Fraud Prevention and Detection Account are fixed by statute and are insufficient to cover the full costs of FDNS. Therefore, USCIS uses both Fraud Prevention and Detection Account and IEFA funds for FDNS costs.
返回引文
Back to Citation
9. 某些H-1B费用是其他法律所要求的。所得收入由USCIS、DOL和国家科学基金会分享。USCIS获得这些资金的5%。USCIS将H-1B非移民申请人账户用作有限H-1B申请及移民工人申请裁决活动的补充资金,这些活动是法律授权的。 参见8 U.S.C. 1356(s)(5)。H-1B非移民申请人账户并不能完全为USCIS的H-1B项目提供资金。因此,USCIS还使用IEFA费用来管理该项目。对于第1356(s)(5)条所授权或要求的有限用途,不需要使用IEFA费用。
9.  Certain H-1B fees are required by other laws. Revenue is shared among USCIS, DOL, and the National Science Foundation. USCIS receives 5 percent of these funds. USCIS uses the H-1B Nonimmigrant Petitioner Account as supplemental funding for the limited H-1B petition and petition for immigrant worker adjudication activities authorized by statute. See 8 U.S.C. 1356(s)(5). The H-1B Nonimmigrant Petitioner Account does not fully fund the H-1B program at USCIS. As such, USCIS also uses IEFA fees to administer the program. IEFA fees are not required for those limited purposes authorized or required by sec. 1356(s)(5).

67. 参见《美国公民及移民服务局收费表及其他若干移民福利申请要求的变更》,89 FR 6194(2024年1月31日),见第V.B节“最终监管灵活性分析”,第6374页至第6376页,其中说明了评估区域中心的难度,以及区域中心在结构上存在多种不同形式,可能涉及多种商业和金融活动,其中一些活动可能对将投资者资金与新商业企业(NCE)及创造就业机会的项目或实体相联系发挥直接或间接作用。区域中心在分析方面也构成挑战,因其结构往往复杂,并可能涉及许多与EB-5活动并无直接关联的相关商业和金融活动。区域中心可能由若干复杂的商业和金融活动层级构成,这些活动旨在将外国投资者资金与开发项目相匹配,以获取高于市场的回报差额。国土安全部(DHS)在审查I-956表格时确实考虑了区域中心申请人提供的信息,然而,这些信息并不足以让国土安全部可靠地确定个别申请人的小实体身份。尽管区域中心申请人通常会报告其计划将投资者资金投向的行业所对应的北美行业分类系统(NAICS)代码,但这些代码不一定适用于区域中心本身。此外,提交给国土安全部的有关区域中心的信息通常不包括区域中心的收入或雇佣情况。
67.  See U.S. Citizenship and Immigration Services Fee Schedule and Changes to Certain Other Immigration Benefit Request Requirements, 89 FR 6194 (Jan. 31, 2024), See Section V.B. Final Regulatory Flexibility Analysis, pages 6374-6376 explains the difficulty of assessing regional centers and on how they are structured in a variety of different ways, and can involve multiple business and financial activities, some of which may play a direct or indirect role in linking investor funds to new commercial enterprise (NCEs), and job-creating projects or entities. Regional centers also pose a challenge for analysis as the structure is often complex and can involve many related business and financial activities not directly involved with EB-5 activities. Regional centers can be made up of several complex layers of business and financial activities that focus on matching foreign investor funds to development projects to capture above market return differentials. DHS did consider the information provided by regional center applicants as part of the Forms I-956; however, it does not include adequate data to allow DHS to reliably identify the small entity status of individual applicants. Although regional center applicants typically report the NAICS codes associated with the sectors they plan to direct investor funds toward, these codes do not necessarily apply to the regional centers themselves. In addition, information provided to DHS concerning regional centers generally does not include regional center revenues or employment.

70. 符合降低投资金额条件的目标就业区(TEA)适用于农村地区,或失业率至少为全国平均水平150%的地区。国土安全部在投资者提交I-526表格时,即对该投资是否符合降低投资金额的条件作出认定。因此,投资者申请中须包含充分证据,证明实际创造就业项目所在地符合适用降低投资门槛的标准。更多信息可参阅: https://www.uscis.gov/​working-in-the-united-states/​permanent-workers/​employment-based-immigration-fifth-preference-eb-5/​about-the-eb-5-visa-classification。 根据2022年《改革法案》,降低后的投资门槛同样适用于基础设施投资。
70.  TEAs that qualify for the reduced amount apply to either rural areas or to areas with unemployment rates at least 150 percent of the national average. DHS makes the determination that an investment qualifies for the reduced amount when the Investor files the I-526 form. Investor petitions therefore need to contain sufficient evidence that the location of the actual job creation project meets the standards for the reduced investment threshold. Additional information can be found at: https://www.uscis.gov/​working-in-the-united-states/​permanent-workers/​employment-based-immigration-fifth-preference-eb-5/​about-the-eb-5-visa-classification. As a result of the 2022 Reform Act, the reduced investment threshold also applies to infrastructure investments.

72. 另一个难以评估区域中心从下游项目获得的收入的原因是,关联的新商业企业(NCE)可以设立为有限合伙企业,而区域中心贷款收入归属于普通合伙人,而该普通合伙人可能并非区域中心本身。换言之,将区域中心与其剩余收入相联系时可能存在一定程度的分离。
72.  Another reason that it is difficult to assess income to regional centers from downstream projects, is that the affiliated NCE could be set up as limited partnership, and the regional center loan income accrues to a general partner that may not be the regional center itself. Stated differently, there can be a degree of separation in linking the regional center and its residual income.

75. 依赖I-526表格批准数量作为区域中心投资者代理指标存在一个需要说明的情况。部分个人投资者可能提交多份I-526表格,这种情况可能出现在最初的投资申请因某种原因被拒绝,或未予实施,随后在该区域中心下提出新的投资申请时。国土安全部(DHS)不清楚在此情形下区域中心是否会收取额外的管理费,因此以其管辖范围内的投资者申请数量作为该项费用收入的计算基础,可能会高估此项收入。
75.  There is a caveat to relying on the number of Form I-526 approvals as a proxy for regional center investors. Some individual investors may file more than one Form I-526, which could arise when an initial investment filing is denied for some reason or is not undertaken and a new investment under the regional center is promulgated. DHS does not know if the regional center would collect an additional administrative fee under this scenario, so it is possible that the basing such fee revenue on the number of investor petitions under their purview may overstate this revenue.

Back to Citation
76. 国土安全部(DHS)指出,一小部分(1.36%)的区域中心投资是按标准投资额105万美元进行的。因此,按标准10%的管理费计算,1,080万美元可被视为我们随后对区域中心收入估算被低估的最大金额。仅此差异不太可能改变随后的小实体认定结果。该最大金额将按某种分布方式分配给2016财年至2021财年间积极投资的所有区域中心,然后再外推至我们的小规模区域中心样本库。若部分区域中心在非目标就业区域(TEA)进行了多项投资(此情形通常极为罕见),则部分个别区域中心的总收入可能被低估。
76.  DHS notes that a small portion (1.36 percent) of RC investments were made at the standard investment amount of $1.05 million. Therefore, based on a standard 10 percent administrative fee, $10.8 million can be thought of as the maximum amount by which our ensuing estimates of RC income are understated. This discrepancy alone would not likely change the ensuing small entity determination. This maximum amount would be allocated along some type of distribution to all RCs that actively invested between FY 2016 through FY 2021 and then extrapolated to our small pool of RCs. If Some RCs had multiple investments in non-TEA areas (which is generally very rare) then it is possible that some individual RCs may have their total income understated.

77. 在数据中提供的区域中心北美行业分类系统(NAICS)代码中,部分代码与522310不同,但我们认为采用该单一代码是适当的。尽管区域中心贷款适用于不同行业下的不同类型项目,但总体而言,区域中心本身并不参与这些活动,而是负责为相关各方安排和构建贷款结构。相关说明可参见: https://www.census.gov/​naics/​。
77.  Where NAICs codes for regional centers were provided in the data, some were different than 522310, but we believe that this singular code is appropriate. While the regional center loans apply to different types of projects under different industries, as a general matter the regional center itself is not involved in those activities and is responsible for arranging and structuring the loans for the parties involved. The description can be found at: https://www.census.gov/​naics/​.

Back to Citation
79. 在2022/2023年费用规则中,美国公民及移民服务局(USCIS)当时无法确定区域中心属于大型实体还是小型实体。本次初步监管灵活性分析(IRFA)中作出不同认定(基于数据和分析,并考虑上述需说明事项),其原因主要有两点。首先且最为重要的是,在进行2022/2023财年费用规则分析时,用于评估收入的数据库中仅发现极少数区域中心(这一情况自2020财年EB-5现代化规则,见84 FR 35750(2019年7月24日)以来一直如此)。而在当前数据库中,列出的区域中心数量大幅增加,且对已列出的区域中心有更多的数据。其次,美国公民及移民服务局(USCIS)经济学家审查了一个内部的美国公民及移民服务局投资者计划办公室(USCIS-IPO)数据库,该数据库记录了更多关于区域中心及其关联企业/活动的数据。该数据库提供了更多可供分析影响的数据和信息,使我们能够进行更好的检索和匹配,并有助于剔除误判和漏判。由此得出的分析结果因而更为可靠。
79.  In the 2022/2023 fee rule, USCIS could not determine at the time if RCs were large or small. The different determination in this IRFA (based on the data and analysis and considering the caveat noted above) is driven by two factors. First and foremost, when the FY 2022/2023 fee rule analysis was conducted, very few regional centers were found in the databases utilized to assess income (which was also the case going back to the FY 2020 EB-5 Modernization rule, at 84 FR 35750 (July 24, 2019)). In the current databases there are many more regional centers listed and there is more data on the ones that are listed. Second, USCIS economists reviewed an internal USCIS-IPO database that captures more data on regional centers and affiliated businesses/activities. This database provided more data and information to analyze for impacts, enabled better searches and matching, and allowed us to root out both false positives and false negatives. The resulting analysis is thus more robust.

80. 新商业企业(NCE)的年平均数为5,672个(表3)。新商业企业与创造就业企业(JCE)并非一一对应,但由于后者的数量至少与前者相等,我们将总体数量视为11,344,在此基础上,满足95%置信水平所需的样本量为372。
80.  The annual average for NCEs was 5,672 (Table 3). NCEs do not map one-to-one to JCEs, but since there are at least as many of the latter as the former, we consider the population to be 11,344, for which the sample size required to satisfy a confidence level of 95 percent is 372.

85. 参见美国劳工统计局(BLS),“所有城市消费者消费价格指数(CPI-U)历史数据:美国城市平均值,所有项目,按月”, https://www.bls.gov/​cpi/​tables/​supplemental-files/​historical-cpi-u-202512.xlsx (最后访问日期:2026年9月2日)。通货膨胀计算方法:(1) 计算基准年(1995年)和当前年(2025年)的月均CPI-U;(2) 用当前年CPI-U减去基准年CPI-U;(3) 将基准年CPI-U与当前年CPI-U之差除以基准年CPI-U;(4) 乘以100 = [(2025年月均CPI-U−1995年月均CPI-U) ÷ (1995年月均CPI-U)] × 100 = [(321.943 × 152.383) ÷ 152.383] = (169.560/152.383) = 1.113 × 100 = 111.3% = 111%(四舍五入)。通货膨胀调整值计算:1995年美元的1亿美元 × 2.11 = 2025年美元的2.11亿美元。
85.   See BLS, “Historical Consumer Price Index for All Urban Consumers (CPI-U): U.S. city average, all items, by month,” https://www.bls.gov/​cpi/​tables/​supplemental-files/​historical-cpi-u-202512.xlsx (last visited Sept. 2, 2026). Calculation of inflation: (1) calculate the average monthly CPI-U for the reference year (1995) and the current year (2025); (2) subtract reference year CPI-U from current year CPI-U; (3) divide the difference of the reference year CPI-U and current year CPI-U by the reference year CPI-U; (4) multiply by 100 = [(average monthly CPI-U for 2025−average monthly CPI-U for 1995) ÷ (average monthly CPI-U for 1995)] × 100 = [(321.943 × 152.383) ÷ 152.383] = (169.560/152.383) = 1.113 × 100 = 111.3 percent = 111 percent (rounded). Calculation of inflation-adjusted value: $100 million in 1995 dollars × 2.11= $211 million in 2025 dollars.
